*hāranūpurakeyūrakirīṭamaṇikuṃḍalaiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*yajñasūtrottarīyasrakkṣaumamālyāṃgulīyakaiḥ \|*  
*puṣpatāṃbūlakarpūracaṃdanāgurulepanaiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*dhūpadīpasitacchatravyajanadhvajacāmaraiḥ \|*  
*anyairdivyopahāraiśca vāṇmanotītavaibhavaiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*patiyogyaiḥ paśvalabhyaistau samarcayatāṃ patim \|*  
*yadyacchreṣṭhatamaṃ vastu patiyogyaṃ hitaddhvaje \|\| 6 \|\|*

3-6. The personal things constitute those natural things of long and short duration such as necklaces, anklets, bracelets coronets, ear-rings, sacrificial threads, upper cloth of lace border, garlands, silk cloth chokers, rings, flowers, betel leaves, camphor, sandal paste, Aguru unguents, incense, lamps, white umbrella, fans, banners, chowries and other divine offerings whose greatness cannot be expressed or even thought of. Both of them adored the lord with all these things worthy of the lord and inaccessible to Paśu (the animal i.e. the individual soul). All excellent things are worthy of the lord, O brahmin.

## \|\| 1.9.7 \|\|

*tadvastvakhilamīśopi pāraṃ paryacikīrṣayā \|*  
*sabhyānāṃ pradadau hṛṣṭaḥ pṛthaktatra yathākramam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. In order to set up a precedence the delighted lord handed over all those articles to the attendants assembled according to the order of priority.

## \|\| 1.9.8-10 \|\|

*kolāhalo mahānāsīttatra tadvastu gṛhṇatām \|*  
*tatraiva brahmaviṣṇubhyāṃ cārcitaḥ śaṃkaraḥ purā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*prasannaḥ prāha tau namrau sasmitaṃ bhaktivardhanaḥ \|*  
*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*tuṣṭo'hamadya vāṃ vatsau pūjayā'sminmahādine \|\| 9 \|\|*

*dinametattataḥ puṇyaṃ bhaviṣyati mahattaram \|*  
*śivarātririti khyātā tithireṣā mama priyā \|\| 10 \|\|*

8-10. The bustle of those who came to receive them was too much. It was there that Brahmā and Viṣṇu adored Śaṅkara at first. When they stood there humbly, the gratified lord spoke smilingly heightening their devotion.

## \|\| 1.9.11 \|\|

*etatkāle tu yaḥ kuryātpūjāṃ malliṃgaberayoḥ \|*  
*kuryāttu jagataḥ kṛtyaṃ sthitisargādikaṃ pumān \|\| 11 \|\|*

Īśvara said:—

Dear children, I am delighted at your worship on this holy day. Henceforth this day will be famous as “Śivarātri” the holiest of holy days pleasing to me.

11\. He who performs the worship of my phallic emblem and the embodied image on this day will be competent, to perform the task of creation and the maintenance etc. of the universe.

## \|\| 1.9.12 \|\|

*śivarātrāvahorātraṃ nirāhāro jiteṃdri yaḥ \|*  
*arcayedvā yathānyāyaṃ yathābalamavaṃcakaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The devotee shall observe fast on Śivarātrī, both during the day and the night. He shall perfectly restrain his sense-organs. He shall adore (with flowers) to the extent of his strength. He shall not deceive any one.

## \|\| 1.9.13 \|\|

*yatphalaṃ mama pūjāyāṃ varṣamekaṃ niraṃtaram \|*  
*tatphalaṃ labhate sadyaḥ śivarātrau madarcanāt \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. By the worship on Śivarātrī day the devotee attains that fruit which usually accrues to one who continuously worships me for a year.

## \|\| 1.9.14 \|\|

*maddharmavṛddhikālo'yaṃ caṃdra kāla ivāṃbudheḥ \|*  
*pratiṣṭhādyutsavo yatra māmako maṃgalāyanaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. This is the time when the virtue of devotion to me increases like the tide in the ocean at the rise of the moon. Festivities like the installation of my idols etc. on that day are very auspicious.

## \|\| 1.9.15 \|\|

*yatpunaḥ staṃbharūpeṇa svāvirāsamahaṃ purā \|*  
*sa kālo mārgaśīrṣe tu syādādrā r ṛkṣamarbhakau \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The day on which I manifested myself in the form of a column of fire is the Ārdrā star in the month of Mārgaśīrṣa (November-December), O children.

## \|\| 1.9.16 \|\|

*ādrā ryāṃ mārgaśīrṣe tu yaḥ paśyenmāmumāsakham \|*  
*madberamapi vā liṃgaṃ sa guhādapi me priyaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He who sees me on the day of Ārdrā star in the month of Mārgaśīrṣa in the company of Umā and worships my phallic emblem or embodied image is dearer to me than even Guha (Kārtikeya).

## \|\| 1.9.17 \|\|

*alaṃ darśanamātreṇa phalaṃ tasmindine śubhe \|*  
*abhyarcanaṃ cedadhikaṃ phalaṃ vācāmagocaram \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. On that auspicious day the vision alone accords ample results. If he worships too, the result cannot be adequately described.

## \|\| 1.9.18 \|\|

*raṇaraṃgatale'muṣminyadahaṃ liṃgavarṣmaṇā \|*  
*jṛṃbhito liṃgavattasmālliṃgasthānamidaṃ bhavet \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Since I manifested myself in the form of phallic emblem in the field of battle, this place will be known as Liṅgasthāna.

## \|\| 1.9.19 \|\|

*anādyaṃtamidaṃ staṃbhamaṇumātraṃ bhaviṣyati \|*  
*darśanārthaṃ hi jagatāṃ pūjanārthaṃ hi putrako \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O sons, this column without root or top will henceforth be diminutive in size for the sake of the vision and worship of the world.

## \|\| 1.9.20 \|\|

*bhogāvahamidaṃ liṃgaṃ bhuktiṃ muktyekasādhanam \|*  
*darśanasparśanadhyānājjaṃtūnāṃ janmamocanam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The phallic emblem confers enjoyment. It is the only means of worldly enjoyment and salvation. Viewed, touched or meditated upon, it wards off all future births of the living beings.

## \|\| 1.9.21 \|\|

*analācalasaṃkāśaṃ yadidaṃ liṃgamutthitam \|*  
*aruṇācalamityeva tadidaṃ khyātimeṣyati \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Since the phallic emblem rose high resembling a mountain of fire, this shall be famous as Ruddy (Aruṇa) mountain.

## \|\| 1.9.22 \|\|

*atra tīrthaṃ ca bahudhā bhaviṣyati mahattaram \|*  
*muktirapyatra jaṃtūnāṃ vāsena maraṇena ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Many holy centres will spring up here. A residence or death in this holy place ensures liberation.

## \|\| 1.9.23 \|\|

*rathotsavādikalyāṇaṃ janāvāsaṃ tu sarvataḥ \|*  
*atra dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The celebration of chariot festivals, the congregation of devotees, the presentation of ordinary as well as sacrificial gifts and offering of prayers at this place shall be millionfold efficacious.

## \|\| 1.9.24 \|\|

*matkṣetrādapi sarvasmātkṣetrametanmahattaram \|*  
*atra saṃsmṛtimātreṇa muktirbhavati dehinām \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Of all my sectors this sector shall be the greatest. A mere remembrance of me at this place shall accord salvation to all souls.

## \|\| 1.9.25 \|\|

*tasmānmahattaramidaṃ kṣetramatyaṃtaśobhanam \|*  
*sarvakalyāṇasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvamuktikaraṃ śubham \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Hence this sector shall be greater than all other sectors, very auspicious, full of all sorts of welfare and according salvation to everyone.

## \|\| 1.9.26-27 \|\|

*arcayitvā'tra māmeva liṃge liṃginamīśvaram \|*  
*sālokyaṃ caiva sāmīpyaṃ sārūpyaṃ sārṣṭireva ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*sāyujyamiti paṃcaite kriyādīnāṃ phalaṃ matam \|*  
*sarvepi yūyaṃ sakalaṃ prāpsyathāśu manoratham \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Worshipping me in my supreme phallic form at this place and performing the other sacred rites shall accord the five types of salvation—Sālokya, Sāmīpya, Sārūpya, Sārṣṭi and Sāyujya. May all of you achieve all your cherished desires.

## \|\| 1.9.28-29 \|\|

*naṃdikeśvara uvāca \|*  
*ityanugṛhya bhagavānvinītau vidhimādhavau \|*  
*yatpūrvaṃ prahataṃ yuddhe tayoḥ sainyaṃ parasparam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*tadutthāpayadatyarthaṃ svaśaktyā'mṛtadhārayā \|*  
*tayormauḍhyaṃ ca vairaṃ ca vyapanetumuvāca tau \|\| 29 \|\|*

Nandikeśvara said:—

28-29. Thus blessing Brahmā and Viṣṇu who had been made humble, the lord resuscitated by His nectar-like power all the soldiers of the two deities that had been killed in the battle before and spoke to them in order to remove their foolishness and mutual enmity.

## \|\| 1.9.30 \|\|

*sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ ceti svarūpadvayamasti me \|*  
*nānyasya kasyacittasmādanyaḥ sarvopyanīśvaraḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. I have two forms: the manifest and the unmanifest. No one else has these two forms. Hence all else are non-Īśvaras.

## \|\| 1.9.31-32 \|\|

*purastātstaṃbharūpeṇa paścādrū peṇa cārbhakau \|*  
*brahmatvaṃ niṣkalaṃ proktamīśatvaṃ sakalaṃ tathā \|\| 31 \|\|*

*dvayaṃ mamaiva saṃsiddhaṃ na madanyasya kasyacit \|*  
*tasmādīśatvamanyeṣāṃ yuvayorapi na kvacit \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. Dear sons, first in the form of the column and afterwards in this embodied form I have expounded to you my formless Brahma-hood, and embodied Īśa-hood. These two are present only in me and not in anyone else. Hence no one else, not even you too can claim Īśatva (Īśa-hood).

## \|\| 1.9.33 \|\|

*tadajñānena vāṃ vṛttamīśamānaṃ mahādbhutam \|*  
*tannirākartumatraivamutthito'haṃ raṇakṣitau \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. It is out of your ignorance of this fact that you were swept away by your false prestige and pride of being Isa, surprising as it is. I rose up in the middle of the battle-field for quelling the same.

## \|\| 1.9.34 \|\|

*tyajataṃ mānamātmīyaṃ mayīśe kurutaṃ matim \|*  
*matprasādena lokeṣu sarvopyarthaḥ prakāśate \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Cast off your false pride. Fix your thought in me as your lord. It is out of my favour that all the objects in the world are illuminated.

## \|\| 1.9.35 \|\|

*gurūktirvyaṃjakaṃ tatra pramāṇaṃ vā punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*brahmatattvamidaṃ gūḍhaṃ bhavatprītyā bhaṇāmyaham \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The statement of the preceptor is the reminder and the authority on all occasions. This secret truth of Brahman I am revealing to you out of love.

## \|\| 1.9.36 \|\|

*ahameva paraṃ brahma matsvarūpaṃ kalākalam \|*  
*brahmatvādīśvaraścāhaṃ kṛtyaṃ menugrahādikam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. I am the supreme Brahman. My form is both manifest and unmanifest in view of my Brahma-hood and Isvaratva. My duty is blessing etc.

## \|\| 1.9.37 \|\|

*bṛhattvādbṛṃhaṇatvācca brahmāhaṃ brahmakeśavau \|*  
*samatvādvyāpakatvācca tathaivātmāhamarbhakau \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Brahmā and Viṣṇu, I am Brahman because of Bṛhatva (huge size) and Bṛṃhaṇatva (causing to grow). O children, similarly I am Ātman due to Samatva (equality) and Vyāpakatva (Pervasiveness).

## \|\| 1.9.38-39 \|\|

*anātmānaḥ pare sarve jīvā eva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*anugrahādyaṃ sargāṃtaṃ jagatkṛtyaṃ ca paṃkajam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*īśatvādeva me nityaṃ na madanyasya kasyacit \|*  
*ādau brahmattvabuddhyarthaṃ niṣkalaṃ liṃgamutthitam \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. All others are Anātmans, individual souls undoubtedly. There are five activities\[2\] in respect of the universe beginning with Anugraha (liberation) and ending with Sarga (creation). Therefore these activities devolve on me because I am Īśa and not on anyone else. It is to make my Brahmatva understood that my phallic emblem rose up.

## \|\| 1.9.40 \|\|

*tasmādajñātamīśatvaṃ vyaktaṃ dyotayituṃ hi vām \|*  
*sakalohamato jātaḥ sākṣādīśastu tatkṣaṇāt \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. In order to clarify my Īśatva, unknown hitherto, I have manifested myself immediately in the embodied form of Isa.

## \|\| 1.9.41 \|\|

*sakalatvamato jñeyamīśatvaṃ mayi satvaram \|*  
*yadidaṃ niṣkalaṃ staṃbhaṃ mama brahmatvabodhakam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The Īśatva in me is to be known as the embodied form and this symbolic column is indicative of my Brahmatva.

## \|\| 1.9.42 \|\|

*liṃgalakṣaṇayuktatvānmama liṃgaṃ bhavedidam \|*  
*tadidaṃ nityamabhyarcyaṃ yuvābhyāmatra putrakau \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Since it has all the characteristic features of my phallic emblem, it shall be my symbol. O sons, you shall worship it every day.

## \|\| 1.9.43 \|\|

*madātmakamidaṃ nityaṃ mama sānnidhyakāraṇam \|*  
*mahatpūjyamidaṃ nityamabhedālliṃgasiṃginoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The phallic symbol and the symbolised Śiva are non-different. Hence this phallic emblem is identical with me. It brings devotees quite near to me. It is worthy of worship therefore.

## \|\| 1.9.44 \|\|

*yatrapratiṣṭhitaṃ yena madīyaṃ liṃgamīdṛśam \|*  
*tatra pratiṣṭhitaḥ sohamapratiṣṭhopi vatsakau \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. O dear sons, if phallic emblem of this sort in installed I can be considered installed, though my idol is not installed.

## \|\| 1.9.45 \|\|

*matsāmyamekaliṃgasya sthāpane phalamīritam \|*  
*dvitīye sthāpite liṃge madaikyaṃ phalameva hi \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The result of installing the phallic emblem is the attainment of similarity with me. If a second phallic emblem is installed, the result is union with me.

## \|\| 1.9.46 \|\|

*liṃgaṃ prādhānyataḥ sthāpyaṃ tathāberaṃ tu gauṇakam \|*  
*liṃgābhāvena tatkṣetraṃ saberamapi sarvataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The installation of the phallic emblem is primary and that of embodied idol is secondary. A temple with the embodied idol of Śiva is unfructuous if it has no phallic image.

## Chapter 10 - The five-fold activities (pañcakṛtya) and the Oṃkāra-mantra

## \|\| 1.10.1 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇū ūcatuḥ \|*  
*sargādipaṃcakṛtyasya lakṣaṇaṃ brūhi nau prabho \|*  
*śiva uvāca \|*  
*matkṛtyabodhanaṃ guhyaṃ kṛpayā prabravīmi vām \|\| 1 \|\|*

Summary: The Evanescence of Śiva after expounding the Five-fold duties (pañcakṛtya) and the Oṃkāra mantra to Brahmā and Viṣṇu.

Brahmā and Viṣṇu said:—

1\. O Lord, please tell us the characteristic feature of the five-fold duties beginning with creation.

## \|\| 1.10.2 \|\|

*sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiśca saṃhārastirobhāvo'pyanugrahaḥ \|*  
*paṃcaiva me jagatkṛtyaṃ nityasiddhamajācyutau \|\| 2 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

I shall tell you the great secret of the five-fold duties, out of compassion for you.

2\. O Brahmā and Viṣṇu, the permanent cycle of the five-fold duties consists of creation, maintenance, annihilation, concealment, and blessing.

## \|\| 1.10.3 \|\|

*sargaḥ saṃsārasaṃraṃbhastatpratiṣṭhā sthitirmatā \|*  
*saṃhāro mardanaṃ tasya tirobhāvastadutkramaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Sarga is the creation of the world; Sthiti is its maintenance; Saṃhāra is the annihilation; Tirobhāva is the removal and concealment;

## \|\| 1.10.4 \|\|

*tanmokṣo'nugrahastanme kṛtyamevaṃ hi paṃcakam \|*  
*kṛtyametadvahatyanyastūṣṇīṃ gopurabiṃbavat \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Liberation (from the cycle of birth and death) is blessing. These five are my activities but are carried on by others silently as in the case of the statue at the Portal.

## \|\| 1.10.5 \|\|

*sargādi yaccatuṣkṛtyaṃ saṃsāraparijṛṃbhaṇam \|*  
*paṃcamaṃ muktiheturvai nityaṃ mayi ca susthiram \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The first four activities concern the evolution of the world and the fifth one is the cause of salvation. All these constitute my prerogatives.

## \|\| 1.10.6-8 \|\|

*tadidaṃ paṃcabhūteṣu dṛśyate māmakairjanaiḥ \|*  
*sṛṣṭirbhūmau sthitistoye saṃhāraḥ pāvake tathā \|\| 6 \|\|*

*tirobhāvo'nile tadvadanugraha ihāmbare \|*  
*sṛjyate dharayā sarvamadbhiḥ sarvaṃ pravarddhate \|\| 7 \|\|*

*ardyate tejasā sarvaṃ vāyunā cāpanīyate \|*  
*vyomnānugṛhyate sarvaṃ jñeyamevaṃ hi sūribhiḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. These activities are observed in the five elements by devotees—Sarga (creation) in the Earth, Sthiti (maintenance) in the waters, Saṃhāra (annihilation) in the fire, Tirobhāva (concealment) in the wind and Anugraha (liberation, the blessed state) in the firmament. Everything is created by the Earth; everything flourishes by virtue of the waters; everything is urged by the fire, everything is removed by the wind and everything is blessed by the firmament. Thus intelligent men must know the same.

## \|\| 1.10.9 \|\|

*paṃcakṛtyamidaṃ voḍhuṃ mamāsti mukhapaṃcakam \|*  
*caturdikṣu caturvaktraṃ tanmadhye paṃcamaṃ mukham \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In order to look after these five-fold activities (pañcakṛtya) I have five faces, four in the four quarters and the fifth in the middle.

## \|\| 1.10.10 \|\|

*yuvābhyāṃ tapasā labdhametatkṛtyadvayaṃ sutau \|*  
*sṛṣṭisthityabhidhaṃ bhāgyaṃ mattaḥ prītādatipriyam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O sons, in view of your austerities you two have received the first two activities:—creation and maintenance. You have gratified me and are blessed therefore.

## \|\| 1.10.11 \|\|

*tathā rudra maheśābhyāmanyatkṛtyadvayaṃ param \|*  
*anugrahākhyaṃ kenāpi labdhuṃ naiva hi śakyate \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Similarly, the other two activities (annihilation and concealment) have been assigned to Rudra and Maheśa. The fifth one of Anugraha (liberation) cannot be taken up by any other.

## \|\| 1.10.12 \|\|

*tatsarvaṃ paurvikaṃ karma yuvābhyāṃ kālavismṛtam \|*  
*na tadrudra maheśābhyāṃ vismṛtaṃ karma tādṛśam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. All this previous arrangement has been forgotten by both of you due to lapse of time, not so by Rudra and Maheśa.

## \|\| 1.10.13 \|\|

*rūpe veśe ca kṛtye ca vāhane cāsane tathā \|*  
*āyudhādau ca matsāmyamasmābhistatkṛte kṛtam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. I have assigned them my equality in form, dress, activity, vehicle, seat, weapons etc.

## \|\| 1.10.14 \|\|

*maddhyānavirahādvatsau mauḍhyaṃ vāmevamāgatam \|*  
*majjñāne sati naivaṃ syānmānaṃ rūpe maheśavat \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O dear sons, your delusion was the result of your not meditating upon me. If you had retained my knowledge you would not have embibed this false pride of being Maheśa yourselves.

## \|\| 1.10.15 \|\|

*tasmānmajjñānasiddhyarthaṃ maṃtramaoṃkāranāmakam \|*  
*itaḥ paraṃ prajapataṃ māmakaṃ mānabhaṃjanam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Hence, hereafter, both of you shall start reciting the mantra Oṃkāra to acquire knowledge of me. It shall quell your false pride as well.

## \|\| 1.10.16 \|\|

*upādiśaṃ nijaṃ maṃtramaoṃkāramurumaṃgalam \|*  
*oṃkāro manmukhājjajñe prathamaṃ matprabodhakaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. I have taught this great auspicious mantra. Oṃkāra came out of my mouth. Originally it indicated me.

## \|\| 1.10.17 \|\|

*vācako'yamahaṃ vācyo maṃtro'yaṃ hi madātmakaḥ \|*  
*tadanusmaraṇaṃ nityaṃ mamānusmaraṇaṃ bhavet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. It is the indicator and I am the indicated. This mantra is identical with me. The repetition of this mantra is verily my repeated remembrance.

## \|\| 1.10.18-19 \|\|

*akāra uttarātpūrvamukāraḥ paścimānanāt \|*  
*makāro dakṣiṇamukhādbiṃduḥ prāṇmukhatastathā \|\| 18 \|\|*

*nādo madhyamukhādevaṃ paṃcadhā'sau vijṛṃbhitaḥ \|*  
*ekībhūtaḥ punastadvadomityekākṣaro bhavet \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. The syllable “A” came first from northern face; the syllable “U” from the western; the syllable “M” from the southern and the Bindu (dot) from the eastern face. The Nāda (mystical sound) came from the middle face. Thus the complete set cropped up in five-fold form. Then all of them united in the syllable of “Om”.

## \|\| 1.10.20 \|\|

*nāmarūpātmakaṃ sarvaṃ vedabhūtakuladvayam \|*  
*vyāptametena maṃtreṇa śivaśaktyośca bodhakaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The two sets of created beings—Nāma (name) and Rūpa (form) are pervaded by this mantra. It indicates Śiva and Śakti.

## \|\| 1.10.21 \|\|

*asmātpaṃcākṣaraṃ jajñe bodhakaṃ sakalasyatat \|*  
*ākārādikrameṇaiva nakārādiyathākramam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. From this also is born the five-syllabled mantra (Namaśśivāya). It indicates all knowledge. The syllables “NA” etc. follow the order of the syllables “A” etc.

## \|\| 1.10.22 \|\|

*asmātpaṃcākṣarājjātā mātṛkāḥ paṃcabhedataḥ \|*  
*tasmācchiraścaturvaktrāttripādgāya trireva hi \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. From the five-syllabled mantra the five mothers were born. The Śiromantra is born of that. The three-footed Gāyatrī also came out of the four faces.

## \|\| 1.10.23 \|\|

*vedaḥ sarvastato jajñe tato vai maṃtrakoṭayaḥ \|*  
*tattanmaṃtreṇa tatsiddhiḥ sarvasiddhirito bhavet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The entire set of Vedas and crores of mantras were born of that. Different things are achieved through different mantras but everything is achieved through Oṃkāra alone.

## \|\| 1.10.24 \|\|

*anena maṃtrakaṃdena bhogo mokṣaśca siddhyati \|*  
*sakalā maṃtrarājānaḥ sākṣādbhogapradāḥ śubhāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. By this root-mantra, the very enjoyment as well as salvation is achieved. All the royal mantras are auspicious and directly accord enjoyment.

## \|\| 1.10.25 \|\|

*naṃdikeśvara uvāca \|*  
*punastayostatra tiraḥ paṭaṃ guruḥ prakalpya maṃtraṃ ca samādiśatparam \|*  
*nidhāya tacchīrṣṇi karāṃbujaṃ śanairudaṇmukhaṃ saṃsthitayoḥ sahāṃbikaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

Nandikeśvara said:—

25\. The lord in the company of his consort Ambikā, assumed the role of the preceptor for both of them. He screened them and placed his lotus-like hand on their heads as they faced the north and slowly taught them the great mantra.

## \|\| 1.10.26-27 \|\|

*triruccāryāgrahīnmaṃtraṃ yaṃtrataṃtroktipūrvakam \|*  
*śiṣyau ca tau dakṣiṇāyāmātmānaṃ ca samarpayat \|\| 26 \|\|*

*prabaddhahastau kila tau tadaṃtike tameva devaṃ jagaturjagadgurum \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The two disciples received the mantra by repeating it thrice, along with the requisite Yantra and Tantra duly expounded. By way of fees, the disciples dedicated themselves. Thereafter standing near him with hands clasped in reverence they addressed the lord, the preceptor of the universe.

## \|\| 1.10.28-31 \|\|

*brahmācyutāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*namo niṣkalarūpāya namo niṣkalatejase \|*  
*namaḥ sakalanāthāya namaste sakalātmane \|\| 28 \|\|*

*namaḥ praṇavavācyāya namaḥ praṇavaliṃgine \|*  
*namaḥ sṛṣṭyādikartre ca namaḥ paṃcamukhāyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

*paṃcabrahmasvarūpāya paṃca kṛtyāyate namaḥ \|*  
*ātmane brahmaṇe tubhyamanaṃtaguṇaśaktaye \|\| 30 \|\|*

*sakalākalarūpāya śaṃbhave gurave namaḥ \|*  
*iti stutvā guruṃ padyairbrahmā viṣṇuśca nematuḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Brahmā and Viṣṇu said:—

28-31. (The prayer):—Obeisance to Thee of the bodiless form. Obeisance to Thee of the formless lustre. Obeisance to Thee the lord of everything. Obeisance to Thee the soul of everything or of the embodied form. Obeisance to Thee stated by the Praṇava. Obeisance to Thee having Praṇava as Thy symbol. Obeisance to Thee the author of creation etc. Obeisance to Thee of five faces. Obeisance to Thee identical with Pañcabrahma form. Obeisance to Thee of five-fold functions. Obeisance to Thee the Ātman, the Brahman, of endless attributes and power. Obeisance to Śiva the preceptor, possessed of both embodied and bodiless forms.”

After eulogising the preceptor in verses Brahmā and Viṣṇu bowed to him.

## \|\| 1.10.32 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*vatsakau sarvatattvaṃ ca kathitaṃ darśitaṃ ca vām \|*  
*japataṃ praṇavaṃ maṃtraṃ devīdiṣṭaṃ madātmakam \|\| 32 \|\|*

Īśvara said:—

32\. O dear sons, the truthful extract of everything has been narrated to you with demonstration. You shall recite as directed by the Goddess this Om mantra which is identical with me.

## \|\| 1.10.33 \|\|

*jñānaṃ ca susthiraṃ bhāgyaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati śāśvatam \|*  
*ādrā ryāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tajjāpyaṃ tvakṣayaṃ bhavet \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Your knowledge shall be stabilised. Permanent fortune shall stand by you. On the Caturdaśī day and on the day with Ārdrā star, the recital of this mantra will give you everlasting efficacy.

## \|\| 1.10.34-35 \|\|

*sūryagatyā mahādrā ryāmekaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet \|*  
*mṛgaśīrṣāṃtimo bhāgaḥ punarvasvādimastathā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*ādrā rsamaḥ sadā jñeyaḥ pūjāhomāditarpaṇe \|*  
*darśanaṃ tu prabhāte ca prātaḥsaṃgavakālayoḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. The recital of this mantra at the time when the transit of the sun is in the Ārdrā star is million-fold efficacious. In the context of worship, Homa and Tarpaṇa, the last quarter of the star Mṛgaśiras and the first quarter of Punarvasu must always be considered on a par with Ārdrā. The Vision is to be had at early dawn and within three muhūrtas (two hours twenty-four minutes) thereafter.

## \|\| 1.10.36 \|\|

*caturdaśī tathā grāhyā niśīthavyāpinī bhavet \|*  
*pradoṣavyāpinī caiva parayuktā praśasyate \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Caturdaśī is to be taken when it continues up to midnight. If it is only upto the early part of the night and joined with another thereafter, it is also recommended.

## \|\| 1.10.37 \|\|

*liṃgaṃ beraṃ ca metulyaṃ yajatāṃ liṃgamuttamam \|*  
*tasmālliṃgaṃ paraṃ pūjyaṃ berādapi mumukṣubhiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Although I consider the phallic and the embodied form to be equal, the phallic form is excellent for those who worship. Hence for those who seek salvation the latter is preferable to the former.

## \|\| 1.10.38-39 \|\|

*liṃgamaoṃkāramaṃtreṇa beraṃ paṃcākṣareṇa tu \|*  
*svayameva hi saddravyaiḥ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ parairapi \|\| 38 \|\|*

*pūjayedupacāraiśca matpadaṃ sulabhaṃ bhavet \|*  
*iti śāsya tathā śiṣyau tatraivāṃ'tarhitaḥ śivaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. The others too shall install the phallic form with Oṃkāra mantra and the embodied form with the five-syllabled mantra, with excellent articles of worship and adore with due homage. It will be easy for them to attain my region.

Having thus instructed His disciples Śiva vanished there itself.

## Chapter 11 - Mode of worshipping the phallic form of Śiva and making gifts

## \|\| 1.11.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*kathaṃ liṃgaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ kathaṃ vātasya lakṣaṇam \|*  
*kathaṃ vā tatsamabhyarcyaṃ deśe kāle ca kena hi \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. How is the phallic form of Śiva to be installed? What are the characteristic features of the form? How is it to be worshipped? What is the appropriate time and place for worship. What sort of performer he must be?

## \|\| 1.11.2-3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*yuṣmadarthaṃ pravakṣyāmi buddhyatāmavadhānataḥ \|*  
*anukūle śubhe kāle puṇye tīrthe taṭe tathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*yatheṣṭaṃ liṃgamāropyaṃ yatra syānnityamarcanam \|*  
*pārthivena tathāpyenaṃ taijasena yathāruci \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2-3. I shall tell you everything for your sake, please listen attentively. The time must be convenient and auspicious. The place must be a holy centre. It can be on the bank of a river or anywhere facilitating a daily worship. It can be of Pārthiva (Earth), Āpya (Watery) or Taijasa (fiery) type.

## \|\| 1.11.4 \|\|

*kalpalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ liṃgaṃ pūjāphalaṃ labhet \|*  
*sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sadyaḥ pūjāphalapradam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. If it has all the characteristics mentioned in the sacred texts, the devotee derives the fruit of worship. If it has all characteristics, it accords the fruit of worship instantaneously.

## \|\| 1.11.5 \|\|

*care viśiṣyate sūkṣmaṃ sthāvare sthūlameva hi \|*  
*salakṣaṇaṃ sapīṭhaṃ ca sthāpayecchivanirmitam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. A subtle one is recommended if it be mobile and a gross one if it is stationary. The phallic emblem of good characteristics shall be set up in the seat of the same sort.

## \|\| 1.11.6 \|\|

*maṃḍalaṃ caturasraṃ vā trikoṇamathavā tathā \|*  
*khaṭvāṃgavanmadhyasūkṣmaṃ liṃgapīṭhaṃ mahāphalaṃ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The seat can be circular, square or triangular in shape. The one shaped like a cot in the middle is of middle efficacy.

## \|\| 1.11.7 \|\|

*prathamaṃ mṛcchilādibhyo ligaṃ lohādibhiḥ kṛtam \|*  
*yena liṃgaṃ tena pīṭhaṃ sthāvare hi viśiṣyate \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. At first, the emblem was made of earth or rock; then it used to be made with the metals. If it is stationary, the emblem and the Pīṭha should be of the one and the same material.

## \|\| 1.11.8 \|\|

*liṃgaṃ pīṭhaṃ care tvekaṃ liṃgaṃ bāṇakṛtaṃ vinā \|*  
*liṃgapramāṇaṃ kartṛṇāṃ dvādaśāṃgulamuttamam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Save the one which the asura Bāṇa worshipped, both the emblem and the seat shall be unitary, if emblem be mobile. The length of the emblem shall be of the measure of twelve fingers of the devotee.

## \|\| 1.11.9 \|\|

*nyūnaṃ cetphalamalpaṃ syādadhikaṃ naiva dūṣyate \|*  
*karturekāṃgulanyūnaṃ carepi ca tathaiva hi \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. If it is shorter it is less efficacious; if it is longer there is no harm. A shortage by the breadth of the finger of the devotee in regard to the mobile one is similarly harmless.

## \|\| 1.11.10-12 \|\|

*ādau vimānaṃ śilpena kāryaṃ devagaṇairyutam \|*  
*tatra garbhagṛhe ramye dṛḍhe darpaṇasaṃnibhe \|\| 10 \|\|*

*bhūṣite navaratnaiśca digdvāre ca pradhānakaiḥ \|*  
*nīlaṃ raktaṃ ca vai dūryaṃ śyāmaṃ mārakataṃ tathā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*muktāpravālagomedavajrāṇi navaratnakam \|*  
*madhye liṃgaṃ mahaddravyaṃ nikṣipetsahavaidike \|\| 12 \|\|*

10-12. A Vimāna (chariot-like structure) of artistic beauty shall be made at first wherein the divine attendants shall be represented. In its firm and beautiful sanctum sanctorum shining like a mirror studded with the nine precious gems—sapphire, lapis lazuli, Emerald, pearl, coral, Gomedaka, diamonds and rubies, the emblem shall be installed on the altar.

## \|\| 1.11.13-17 \|\|

*saṃpūjya liṃgaṃ sadyādyaiḥ paṃcasthāne yathākramam \|*  
*agnau ca hutvā bahudhā haviṣāsa kalaṃ ca mām \|\| 13 \|\|*

*abhyarcya gurumācāryamarthaiḥ kāmaiśca bāṃdhavam \|*  
*dadyādaiśvaryamarthibhyo jaḍamapyajaḍaṃ tathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ jīvaṃ sarvaṃ saṃtoṣya yatnataḥ \|*  
*suvarṇapūrite śvabhre navaratnaiśca pūrite \|\| 15 \|\|*

*sadyādi brahma coccārya dhyātvā devaṃ paraṃ śubham \|*  
*udīrya ca mahāmaṃtramaoṃkāraṃ nādaghoṣitam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*liṃgaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhāpya ligaṃ pīṭhena yojayet \|*  
*liṃgaṃ sapīṭhaṃ nikṣipya nityalepena baṃdhayet \|\| 17 \|\|*

13-17. The emblems hall be worshipped with the mantras beginning with “Sadyo” in five different places in order. Sacrificial offerings shall be made in the fire. Śiva and the gods of His family shall be adored. The preceptor is given monetary gifts. Kinsmen are propitiated with whatever they desire. Money is distributed among the mendicants. All objects sentient or otherwise, and all living beings movable or immovable are duly gratified. The cavity is filled with gems. Mantras “Sadyo” etc. are recited. The auspicious supreme lord is meditated upon. The great mantra Oṃkāra resonant with its mystical sound is repeated. The liṅga is then united with the Pīṭha (pedestal). The two are then welded together.

## \|\| 1.11.18 \|\|

*evaṃ beraṃ ca saṃsthāpyaṃ tatraiva paramaṃ śubham \|*  
*paṃcākṣareṇa beraṃ tu utsavārthaṃ vahistathā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Similarly the embodied image shall also be fixed there auspiciously. For the sake of festivals the embodied image shall be installed outside with the five-syllabled mantra.

## \|\| 1.11.19 \|\|

*beraṃ gurubhyo gṛhṇīyātsādhubhiḥ pūjitaṃ tu vā \|*  
*evaṃ liṃge ca bere ca pūjā śivapadapradā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The embodied image shall be taken from the preceptors or it must be one that has been worshipped by saintly men. Such an adoration of the embodied image and the phallic emblem accords the region of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.11.20 \|\|

*punaśca dvividhaṃ proktaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ tathā \|*  
*sthāvaraṃ liṃgamityāhustarugulmādikaṃ tathā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The phallic emblem is of two varieties: the stationary and the mobile. Trees, hedges etc. represent the stationary.

## \|\| 1.11.21 \|\|

*jaṃgamaṃ liṃgamityāhuḥ kṛmikīṭādikaṃ tathā \|*  
*sthāvarasya ca śuśrūṣā jaṃgamasya ca tarpaṇam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Worms, insects etc. represent the mobile. For the stationary one, tending and similar service is recommended. For the mobile one Tarpaṇa (propitiation) is recommended.

## \|\| 1.11.22 \|\|

*tattatsukhānurāgeṇa śivapūjāṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|*  
*pīṭhamaṃbāmayaṃ sarvaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ ca cinmayam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. With a love for the happiness of different beings Śiva Pūjā shall be performed—so say the wise men. The pedestal represents Śiva’s consort—Pārvatī and his phallic emblem represents the sentient being.

## \|\| 1.11.23 \|\|

*yathā devīmumāmaṃke dhṛtvā tiṣṭhati śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*tathā liṃgamidaṃ pīṭhaṃ dhṛtvā tiṣṭhati saṃtatam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Just as lord Śiva remains ever in close embrace of the Goddess Pārvatī, so also the phallic emblem holds on to the pedestal, for ever.

## \|\| 1.11.24 \|\|

*evaṃ sthāpya mahāliṃgaṃ pūjayedupacārakaiḥ \|*  
*nityapūjā yathā śaktidhvajādikaraṇaṃ tathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Such is the installation of Śiva’s great phallic emblem which shall be worshipped with due homage. The daily worship shall be made in accorance with one’s capacity; so also the fixation of banner etc.

## \|\| 1.11.25-29 \|\|

*iti saṃsthāpayelliṃgaṃ sākṣācchivapadapradam \|*  
*athavā caraliṃgaṃ tu ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*pūjayecca yathānyāyaṃ kramācchivapadapradam \|*  
*āvāhanaṃ cāsanaṃ ca arghyaṃ pādyaṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*tadaṃgācamanaṃ caiva snānamabhyaṃgapūrvakam \|*  
*vastraṃ gaṃdhaṃ tathā puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ nivedanam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*nīrājanaṃ ca tāṃbūlaṃ namaskāro visarjanam \|*  
*athavā'rghyādikaṃ kṛtvā naivedyāṃ taṃ yathāvidhi \|\| 28 \|\|*

*athābhiṣekaṃ naivedyaṃ namaskāraṃ ca tarpaṇam \|*  
*yathāśakti sadākuryātkramācchivapadapradam \|\| 29 \|\|*

25-29. The devotee shall install the phallic emblem and it will accord directly the region of Śiva. Or the devotee shall worship the mobile emblem with the sixteen types of homage and services as prescribed. It accords the region of Śiva gradually. The sixteen types of service are:—invocation (Āvāhana); offering the seat (Āsana); water offering (Arghya); washing of the feet (Pādya); water for rinsing the mouth as a mystical rite (Ācamana); oil bath (Abhyaṅga snāna); offering of cloth (Vastra); Scents (Gandha); flowers (Puṣpa); incense (Dhūpa); lamps (Dīpa); food offering (Nivedana); waving of lights (Nīrājana); betel leaves (Tāmbūla); obeisance (Namaskāra); and mystical discharge and conclusion (Visarjana).

Or the devotee need perform the rites from water-offering to food offering alone duly. Or the devotee shall daily perform, as he can, ablution (Abhiṣeka); food offering (Naivedya); and obeisance (Namaskāra) and propitiation (Tarpaṇa),—all these in order. It will accord him the region of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.11.30 \|\|

*athavā mānuṣe liṃgepyārṣe daive svayaṃbhuvi \|*  
*sthāpite'pūrvake liṃge sopacāraṃ yathā tathā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Or he shall perform all the sixteen rites in the phallic emblem of human, saintly or godly origin, or in one naturally risen up (Svayambhū) or in one of very extraordinary nature installed duly.

## \|\| 1.11.31 \|\|

*pūjopakaraṇe datte yatkiṃcitphalamaśnute \|*  
*pradakṣiṇānamaskāraiḥ kramācchivapadapradam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. If the devotee makes gifts of articles of worship he will get some benefit or other. By circumambulation and obeisance he will attain Śiva’s region gradually.

## \|\| 1.11.32-33 \|\|

*liṃgaṃ darśanamātraṃ vā niyamena śivapradam \|*  
*mṛtpiṣṭagośakṛtpuṣpaiḥ karavīreṇa vā phalaiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*guḍena navanītena bhasmanānnairyathāruci \|*  
*liṃgaṃ yatnena kṛtvāṃte yajettadanusārataḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Regular vision of the phallic emblem accords benefit. Or the devotee can make a phallic emblem out of clay, cow-dung, flowers, Karavīra fruit, jaggery, butter, ashes or cooked rice as he likes and worship it according to the prescribed rules.

## \|\| 1.11.34 \|\|

*aṃguṣṭhādāvapi tathā pūjāmicchaṃti kecana \|*  
*liṃgakarmaṇi sarvatra niṣedhosti na karhicit \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Some authorities have recommended the worship of the phallic emblem on the thumb etc. In these rites of phallic worship, there is no sort of prohibition whatsoever.

## \|\| 1.11.35 \|\|

*sarvatra phaladātā hi prayāsānuguṇaṃ śivaḥ \|*  
*athavā liṃgadānaṃ vā liṃgamaulyamathāpi vā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Everywhere Śiva accords benefit as befitting the endeavour put in. Or he shall make gifts of the phallic emblem or the value of its construction.

## \|\| 1.11.36 \|\|

*śraddhayā śivabhaktāya dattaṃ śivapadapradam \|*  
*athavā praṇavaṃ nityaṃ japeddaśasahasrakam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Whatever is given to a devotee of Śiva with sincere faith accords Śiva’s region. Or the devotee can repeat the Praṇava mantra ten thousand times every day.

## \|\| 1.11.37 \|\|

*saṃdhyayośca sahasraṃ vā jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam \|*  
*japakāle makārāṃtaṃ manaḥśuddhikaraṃ bhajet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Repetition of Om (Praṇava mantra) a thousand times at dawn and at dusk is known to be according Śiva’s region. At the time of the repeated utterance (Japa) of the mantra, (Om) ending with “M” purifies the mind.

## \|\| 1.11.38 \|\|

*samādhau mānasaṃ proktamupāṃśu sārvakālikam \|*  
*samānapraṇavaṃ cemaṃ biṃdunādayutaṃ viduḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. At the time of Samādhi (meditation) the repetition of Oṃkāra must be mental. Muttering of it in low voice can be practised at all times. The same with Bindu (dot) and Nāda (sound) is also of the same efficacy.

## \|\| 1.11.39 \|\|

*atha paṃcākṣaraṃ nityaṃ japedayutamādarāt \|*  
*saṃdhyayośca sahasraṃ vā jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Or the devotee can with due reverence repeat the five-syllabled mantra ten thousand times every day or a thousand times at dawn and at dusk. It accords the region of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.11.40 \|\|

*praṇavenādisaṃyuktaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ viśiṣyate \|*  
*dīkṣāyuktaṃ gurorgrāhyaṃ maṃtraṃ hyatha phalāptaye \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Repetition of the five-syllabled mantra (Namaśśivāya) by a brahmin is specially efficacious with the Om (Praṇava) prefixed. A mantra must be received from a preceptor with proper initiation for the acquisition of the desired fruit.

## \|\| 1.11.41 \|\|

*kuṃbhasnānaṃ maṃtradīkṣāṃ mātṛkānyāsameva ca \|*  
*brāhmaṇaḥ satyapūtātmā gururjñānī viśiṣyate \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The ceremonial ablution when the sun is in transit to the Zodiac Kumbha, initiation for mantras, the Nyāsa of Mātṛkās; a brahmin, a person with soul purified by truth; a preceptor of perfect knowledge—all these are splendid.

## \|\| 1.11.42 \|\|

*dvijānāṃ ca namaḥpūrvamanyeṣāṃ ca namontakam \|*  
*strīṇāṃ ca kvacidicchaṃti namo taṃ ca yathāvidhi \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Brahmins shall begin with Namaḥ and the others shall end with Namaḥ. With regard to some women the mantra shall end with Namaḥ duly.

## \|\| 1.11.43 \|\|

*viprastrīṇāṃ namaḥ pūrvamidamicchaṃti kecana \|*  
*paṃcakoṭijapaṃ kṛtvā sadā śivasamo bhavet \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Some say that Brahmin women begin with Namaḥ. Repetition of this for five crores of times will render a person equal to Sadāśiva.

## \|\| 1.11.44 \|\|

*ekadvitricatuḥkoṭyābrahmādīnāṃ padaṃ vrajet \|*  
*japedakṣaralakṣaṃvā akṣarāṇāṃ pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. By repeating it one, two, three or four crores of times, the devotee shall attain the region of Brahmā and others. One can repeat any of the syllables a hundred thousand times or all of the syllables separately a hundred thousand times.

## \|\| 1.11.45-47 \|\|

*athavākṣaralakṣaṃ vā jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam \|*  
*sahasraṃ tu sahasrāṇāṃ sahasreṇa dinena hi \|\| 45 \|\|*

*japenmaṃtrādiṣṭasiddhirnityaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanāt \|*  
*aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vai gāyatrīṃ prātareva hi \|\| 46 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇastu japennityaṃ kramācchivapadapradān \|*  
*vedamaṃtrāṃstu sūktāni japenniyamamāsthitaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

45-47. Or a hundred thousand times all the syllables together, if repeated, accord Śiva’s region. Or if the devotee repeats it a thousand times every day and completes a million times in a thousand days, he can achieve whatever he desires. He shall feed brahmins every day. A brahmin shall repeat the Gāyatrī a thousand and eight times every day m the morning. He shall attain Śiva’s region gradually. He shall repeat Vedic verses and hymns with the observance of restraints.

## \|\| 1.11.48 \|\|

*ekaṃ daśārṇaṃ maṃtraṃ ca śatonaṃ ca tadūrdhvakam \|*  
*ayutaṃ ca sahasraṃ ca śatamekaṃ vinā bhavet \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The Daśārṇa mantra shall be repeated either 99 times or nine hundred times or nine thousand nine hundred times.

## \|\| 1.11.49 \|\|

*vedapārāyaṇaṃ caiva jñeyaṃ śivapadapradam \|*  
*anyānbahutarānmaṃtrāñjapedakṣaralakṣataḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The regular study of the Vedas accords Śiva’s region. All the other sorts of mantras shall be repeated a hundred thousand times.

## \|\| 1.11.50 \|\|

*ekākṣarāṃstathā maṃtrāñjapedakṣarakoṭitaḥ \|*  
*tataḥ paraṃ japeccaiva sahasraṃ bhaktipūrvakam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. If the mantra consists of only one syllable it shall first be repeated a crore times and thereafter a thousand times with great devotion.

## \|\| 1.11.51 \|\|

*evaṃ kuryādyathāśakti kramācchiva padaṃ labhet \|*  
*nityaṃ rucikaraṃ tvekaṃ maṃtramāmaraṇāṃtikam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Doing thus according to one’s capacity one shall gradually attain Śiva’s region. It is the duty of every one to repeat a mantra pleasing to him every day till his death.

## \|\| 1.11.52-53 \|\|

*japetsahasramomiti sarvābhīṣṭaṃ śivājñayā \|*  
*puṣpārāmādikaṃ vāpi tathā saṃmārjanādikam \|\| 52 \|\|*

*śivāya śivakāryāthe kṛtvā śivapadaṃ labhet \|*  
*śivakṣetre tathā vāsaṃ nityaṃ kuryācca bhaktitaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. If a man repeats “Om” a thousand times he shall get all his desires fulfilled at the bidding of Śiva. If he plants a flower-garden for the sake of Śiva or even renders service by sweeping and cleaning Śiva’s temple and precincts he shall attain Śiva’s region. The devotee shall reside for ever in Śiva’s temple with great devotion.

## \|\| 1.11.54 \|\|

*jaḍānāmajaḍānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ bhuktimuktidam \|*  
*tasmādvāsaṃ śivakṣetre kuryadāmaraṇaṃ budhaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. It yields worldly enjoyment and salvation to every one sentient or insentient. Hence an intelligent man shall reside in a temple of Śiva till death.

## \|\| 1.11.55 \|\|

*liṃgāddhastaśataṃ puṇyaṃ kṣetre mānuṣake viduḥ \|*  
*sahasrāratnimātraṃ tu puṇyakṣetre tathārṣake \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. In a temple built by ordinary man, the space upto a hundred hastas (1 hasta = 30 cms) from the phallic image is holy. In a temple dedicated to sages, the space upto a thousand Aratnis (1 Aratni = 45 cms) from the phallic image is holy. In a temple dedicated to sages, the space upto a thousand Aratnis (1 Aratni = 45 cms) from the emblem is holy.

## \|\| 1.11.56 \|\|

*daivaliṃge tathā jñeyaṃ sahasrāratnimānataḥ \|*  
*dhanuṣpramāṇasāhasraṃ puṇyaṃ kṣetre svayaṃ bhuvi \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. If the phallic emblem had been installed by gods the space upto a thousand Aratnis is holy. In a temple where phallic emblem is self-risen, the space upto a thousand Dhanuḥ. Pramāṇas (a dhanuḥ. pramāṇa = 4 hastas) is holy.

## \|\| 1.11.57 \|\|

*puṇyakṣetre sthitā vāpī kūpādyaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca \|*  
*śivagaṃgeti vijñeyaṃ śivasya vacanaṃ yathā \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. The tank, well, pond etc. in a holy centre shall be considered Śiva-Gaṅgā in accordance with Śiva’s statement.

## \|\| 1.11.58 \|\|

*tatra snātvā tathā dattvā japitvā hi śivaṃ vrajet \|*  
*śivakṣetraṃ samāśritya vasedāmaraṇaṃ tathā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. By taking bath or making gifts or muttering mantras in that centre one will attain Śiva. One shall seek shelter in a temple of Śiva and stay there till death.

## \|\| 1.11.59-61 \|\|

*dāhaṃ daśāhaṃ māsyaṃ vā sapiṃḍīkaraṇaṃ tu vā \|*  
*ābdikaṃ vā śivakṣetre kṣetre piṃḍamathāpi vā \|\| 59 \|\|*

*sarvapāpa vinirmuktaḥ sadyaḥ śivapadaṃ labhet \|*  
*athavā saptarātraṃ vā vasedvā paṃcarātrakam \|\| 60 \|\|*

*trirātramekarātraṃ vā kramācchivapadaṃ labhet \|*  
*svavarṇānuguṇaṃ loke svācārātprāpnute naraḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

59-61. The rites of obsequies of the second day or the tenth day, the offerings of monthly Piṇḍas, the rite of Sapiṇḍīkaraṇa or the annual Śrāddha shall be performed in a holy centre. He will instantly attain Śiva’s region. By staying there for seven, five or three nights or a single night he will attain Śiva’s region gradually. He will obtain results according to his conduct and befitting his caste.

## \|\| 1.11.62 \|\|

*varṇoddhāreṇa bhaktyā ca tatphalātiśayaṃ naraḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ kāmanayā sadyaḥ phalamavāpnuyāt \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. By the uplift in the caste and devotion the fruit gains more efficacy. Anything done with a desire in view yields results immediately.

## \|\| 1.11.63-64 \|\|

*sarvaṃ kṛtamakāmena sākṣācchivapadapradam \|*  
*prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhnamahastriṣvekataḥ kramāt \|\| 63 \|\|*

*prātarvidhikaraṃ jñeyaṃ madhyāhnaṃ kāmikaṃ tathā \|*  
*sāyāhnaṃ śāṃtikaṃ jñeyaṃ rātrāvapi tathaiva hi \|\| 64 \|\|*

63-64. Anything done with no specific desire m view yields the region of Śiva directly. Of the three periods of time, ordained rites shall be performed in the morning, rites for the fulfilment of desires in the midday and rites for the suppression of the evil in the evening. The same thing holds good for nights too.

## \|\| 1.11.65 \|\|

*kālo niśītho vai proktomadhyayāmadvayaṃ niśi \|*  
*śivapūjā viśeṣeṇa tatkāle'bhīṣṭasiddhidā \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The two middle Yāmas (1 Yāma is equal to 3 hours) at night are called Niśītha. The worship of Śiva at that time accords desired results.

## \|\| 1.11.66 \|\|

*evaṃ jñātvā naraḥ kurvanyathoktaphalabhāgbhavet \|*  
*kalau yuge viśeṣeṇa phalasiddhistu karmaṇā \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. If a man performs rites after realising this, he shall achieve the due results. Especially in the Kali age the achievement of fruit is only due to the precise performance of actions.

## \|\| 1.11.67 \|\|

*uktena kenacidvāpi adhikāravibhedataḥ \|*  
*sadvṛttiḥ pāpabhīruścettatatphalamavāpnuyāt \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. If the man is well behaved, afraid of sins and the observer of good actions at other man’s suggestion or at his own he shall attain due results.

## \|\| 1.11.68-69 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*atha kṣetrāṇi puṇyāni samāsātkathayasva naḥ \|*  
*sarvāḥ striyaśca puruṣā yānyāśritya padaṃ labhet \|\| 68 \|\|*

*sūta yogivaraśreṣṭha śivakṣetrāgamāṃstathā \|*  
*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇuta śraddhayā sarvakṣetrāṇi ca tadāgamān \|\| 69 \|\|*

The sages said:—

68-69. O Sūta, foremost among excellent yogins, please tell us briefly about the various holy centres by resorting to which women and men shall attain the region (of Śiva). Please tell us about the traditions of Śiva temples also.

## Chapter 12 - The narrative of Śiva’s holy centres and temples

## \|\| 1.12.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ prājñāḥ śivakṣetraṃ vimuktidam \|*  
*tadāgamāṃstato vakṣye lokarakṣārthameva hi \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O wise sages, please listen to the narrative of holy centres with Śiva’s temples all of which accord salvation. Thereafter I shall tell you their traditions for the welfare of the people.

## \|\| 1.12.2 \|\|

*paṃcāśatkoṭivistīrṇā saśailavanakānanā \|*  
*śivājñayā hi pṛthivī lokaṃ dhṛtvā ca tiṣṭhati \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The Earth, fifty crores of yojanas in extent, abounding in mountains and forests, supports the people at the bidding of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.12.3 \|\|

*tatra tatra śivakṣetraṃ tatra tatra nivāsinām \|*  
*mokṣārthaṃ kṛpayā devaḥ kṣetraṃ kalpitavānprabhuḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The lord has Himself raised up these temples and holy centres in different places for the liberation of the residents of these localities.

## \|\| 1.12.4 \|\|

*parigrahādṛṣīṇāṃ ca devānāṃ parigrahāt \|*  
*svayaṃbhūtānyathānyāni lokarakṣārthameva hi \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. These temples whether self-risen or not, in view of their being accepted (as their frequent resort) by the sages and Devas are intended for the redemption of the people.

## \|\| 1.12.5 \|\|

*tīrthe kṣetre sadākāryaṃ snānadānajapādikam \|*  
*anyathā rogadāridra ymūkatvādyāpnuyānnaraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. In these holy centres and temples, ablutions, charitable gifts, Japas etc must be regularly performed. Otherwise men are sure to be affected by ailments, penury, dumbness etc.

## \|\| 1.12.6 \|\|

*athāsminbhārate varṣe prāpnoti maraṇaṃ naraḥ \|*  
*svayaṃbhūsthānavāsena punarmānuṣyamāpnuyāt \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. If a man dies anywhere in the Bhāratavarṣa he shall be reborn again as a man if he has resided in a holy centre where there is a self-risen phallic emblem of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.12.7 \|\|

*kṣetre pāpasya karaṇaṃ dṛḍhaṃ bhavati bhūsurāḥ \|*  
*puṇyakṣetre nivāse hi pāpamaṇvapi nācaret \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O brahmins, committing sins in a holy centre is of ineffable character. When a man stays in a holy centre he must not commit even the smallest sin.

## \|\| 1.12.8 \|\|

*yena kenāpyupāyena puṇyakṣetre vasennaraḥ \|*  
*siṃdhoḥ śatanadītīre saṃti kṣetrāṇyanekaśaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Somehow men must strive to find a residence in a holy centre. On the shores of the ocean in the confluence of hundreds of rivers there are many such holy centres and temples.

## \|\| 1.12.9 \|\|

*sarasvatī nadī puṇyā proktā ṣaṣṭimukhā tathā \|*  
*tattattīre vasetprājñaḥ kramādbrahmapadaṃ labhet \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The holy river Sarasvatī is said to have sixty mouths or holy centres on its banks. Hence an intelligent man must stay on its banks. He shall attain Brahma’s region gradually.

## \|\| 1.12.10-11 \|\|

*himavadgirijā gaṃgā puṇyā śatamukhā nadī \|*  
*tattīre caiva kāśyādipuṇyakṣetrāṇyanekaśaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*tatra tīraṃ praśastaṃ hi mṛge mṛgabṛhaspatau \|*  
*śoṇabhadro daśamukhaḥ puṇyobhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. The river Gaṅgā flowing from the Himālaya mountains is very holy with its hundred mouths. There are many holy centres on its banks such as Kāśī etc. Its banks are highly sacred in the month of Mārgaśīrṣa or when Bṛhaspati (Jupiter) is in the zodiac ‘Capricornus. The river Śoṇabhadra of ten mouths is holy and yields all cherished desires.

## \|\| 1.12.12-13 \|\|

*tatra snānopavāsena padaṃ vaināyakaṃ labhet \|*  
*caturvīṃśamukhā puṇyā narmadā ca mahānadī \|\| 12 \|\|*

*tasyāṃ snānena vāsena padaṃ vaiṣṇavamāpnuyāt \|*  
*tamasā dvādaśamukhā revā daśamukhā nadī \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. By ablutions therein and observing fast the devotee shall attain the region of the god Gaṇeśa. The holy Narmadā\[3\] is a great river of twenty-four mouths. By a dip therein and residing on its banks the devotee shall attain the region of Viṣṇu. The river Tamasā \[4\] is of twelve mouths and Revā has ten mouths.

## \|\| 1.12.14 \|\|

*godāvarī mahāpuṇyā brahmagovadhanāśinī \|*  
*ekaviṃśamukhā proktā rudra lokapradāyinī \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Godāvarī is very holy and it quells the sins of murdering a brahmin or slaughtering a cow. It is said to have twentyone mouths and accords Rudraloka.

## \|\| 1.12.15 \|\|

*kṛṣṇaveṇī puṇyanadī sarvapāpakṣayāvahā \|*  
*sāṣṭādaśamukhāproktā viṣṇulokapradāyinī \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Kṛṣṇāveṇī is a sacred river destroying all sins. It is said to have eighteen mouths and it accords Viṣṇuloka.

## \|\| 1.12.16 \|\|

*tuṃgabhadrā daśamukhā brahmalokapradāyinī \|*  
*suvarṇamukharī puṇyā proktā navamukhā tathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Tuṅgabhadrā \[8\] has ten mouths and it accords Brahmaloka. The holy Suvarṇamukharī is said to have nine mouths.

## \|\| 1.12.17-19 \|\|

*tatraiva suprajāyaṃte brahmalokacyutāstathā \|*  
*sarasvatī ca paṃpā ca kanyāśvetanadī śubhā \|\| 17 \|\|*

*etāsāṃ tīravāsena iṃdra lokamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*sahyādri jā mahāpuṇyā kāverīti mahānadī \|\| 18 \|\|*

*saptaviṃśamukhā proktā sarvābhīṣṭaṃ pradāyinī \|*  
*tattīrāḥ svargadāścaiva brahmaviṣṇupadapradāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

17-19. Those who fall from Brahmaloka are born there. By residing on the banks of the auspicious rivers Sarasvatī,\[10\] Pampā,\[11\] Kanyā\[12\] and Śvetanadī\[13\] one shall attain Indraloka. The great river Kāverī flowing from the mountain Sahya is very holy and is said to have twenty-seven mouths. It accords all cherished desires. Its banks are the bestowers of heaven and the regions of Brahmā and Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 1.12.20-28 \|\|

*śivalokapradā śaivāstathā'bhīṣṭaphalapradāḥ \|*  
*naimiṣe badare snāyānmeṣage ca gurau ravau \|\| 20 \|\|*

*brahmalokapradaṃ vidyāttataḥ pūjādikaṃ tathā \|*  
*siṃdhunadyāṃ tathā snānaṃ siṃhe karkaṭage ravau \|\| 21 \|\|*

*kedārodakapānaṃ ca snānaṃ ca jñānadaṃ viduḥ \|*  
*godāvaryāṃ siṃhamāse snāyātsiṃhabṛhaspatau \|\| 22 \|\|*

*śivalokapradamiti śivenoktaṃ tathā purā \|*  
*yamunāśoṇayoḥ snāyādgurau kanyāgate ravau \|\| 23 \|\|*

*dharmaloke daṃtiloke mahābhogapradaṃ viduḥ \|*  
*kāveryāṃ ca tathāsnāyāttulāge tu ravau gurau \|\| 24 \|\|*

*viṣṇorvacanamāhātmyātsarvābhīṣṭapradaṃ viduḥ \|*  
*vṛścike māsi saṃprāpte tathārke guruvṛścike \|\| 25 \|\|*

*narmadāyāṃ nadīsnānādviṣṇulokamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*suvarṇamukharīsnānaṃ cāpage ca gurau ravau \|\| 26 \|\|*

*śivalokapradamiti brāhmaṇo vacanaṃ yathā \|*  
*mṛgamāsi tathā snāyājjāhnavyāṃ mṛgage gurau \|\| 27 \|\|*

*śivalokapradamiti brahmaṇo vacanaṃ yathā \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇvoḥ pade bhuktvā tadaṃte jñānamāpnuyāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

20-28. The devotees of Śiva are the bestowers of Śivaloka and accord cherished desires. When the Jupiter and the sun are in the zodiac of Meṣa, the devotee shall take the holy bath in Naimiṣa\[15\] and Badara.\[16\] Worship etc. thereafter accords Brahmaloka. When the sun is in Karkaṭaka or Siṃha one shall take bath in the Sindhu (Indus)\[17\]. On that occasion the drinking of the sacred water of Kedāra\[18\] and ablution therein accords perfect knowledge. Śiva Himself has mentioned before that the bath in the Godāvarī in the month of Siṃha when Jupiter is also in the zodiac of Siṃha accords Śiva region. When Jupiter and the sun are in the zodiac of Kanya, ablution shall be performed in the rivers—Yamunā and Śoṇa, the fruit of which is great enjoyment in the worlds of Dharma and Dantin (Gaṇeśa). When the sun and the Jupiter are in Tulā, the devotee shall take bath in the Kāverī the fruit whereof is the attainment of all cherished desires as stated by Viṣṇu Himself. The devotee who takes bath in the river Narmadā in the month of Vṛścika, when the Jupiter is in the zodiac of Vṛścika, attains Viṣṇuloka. Brahmā has stated that the bath in the Suvarṇamukharī when the sun and the Jupiter are in the zodiac of Dhanus accords Śivaloka. The devotee shall take bath in the Jāhnavī (Ganges) in the month of Mārgaśīrṣa when Jupiter is in the zodiac of Capricornus. After enjoying pleasures in the regions of Brahmā and Viṣṇu he will gain perfect knowledge in the end.

## \|\| 1.12.29-30 \|\|

*gaṃgāyāṃ māghamāse tu tathākuṃbhagate ravau \|*  
*śrāddhaṃ vā piṃḍadānaṃ vā tilodakamathāpivā \|\| 29 \|\|*

*vaṃśadvayapitṛṇāṃ ca kulakoṭyuddharaṃ viduḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇaveṇyāṃ praśaṃsaṃti mīnage ca gurau ravau \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. In the month of Māgha when the sun is in the zodiac of Kumbha, Śrāddha, offerings of Piṇḍa and water libations with gingelly seeds raise the crores of manes on both the sides (Paternal and maternal) of the family. When the sun and the Jupiter are in the zodiac of Mīna, ablution shall be performed in Kṛṣṇāveṇī.

## \|\| 1.12.31-32 \|\|

*tattattīrthe ca tanmāsi snānamiṃdra padapradam \|*  
*gaṃgāṃ vā sahyajāṃ vāpi samāśritya vasedbudhaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*tatkālakṛtapāpasya kṣayo bhavati niścitam \|*  
*rudra lokapradānyeva saṃti kṣetrāṇyanekaśaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The ceremonial ablutions taken in the different sacred waters in the respective months accord the region of Indra. An intelligent man shall resort to Gaṅgā or the Kāverī river. Certainly his sin will be quelled thereby. There are many holy centres yielding Rudraloka.

## \|\| 1.12.33 \|\|

*tāmraparṇī vegavatī brahmalokaphalaprade \|*  
*tayostīre hi saṃtyeva kṣetrāṇi svargadāni ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

3 3. The rivers Tāmraparnī\[20\] and Vegavatī accord Brahmaloka. There are holy centres on their banks bestowing heaven on the worshipper.

## \|\| 1.12.34 \|\|

*saṃti kṣetrāṇi tanmadhye puṇyadāni ca bhūriśaḥ \|*  
*tatra tatra vasanprājñastādṛśaṃ ca phalaṃ labhet \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. In between these rivers there are meritorious holy centres. Intelligent men residing there will reap the respective fruits thereof.

## \|\| 1.12.35 \|\|

*sadācāreṇa sadvṛttyā sadā bhāvanayāpi ca \|*  
*vaseddayāluḥ prājño vai nānyathā tatphalaṃ labhet \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Only by good conduct, good predilections and good concepts as well as by being sympathetic can the devotee derive the benefit, not otherwise.

## \|\| 1.12.36 \|\|

*puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ bahudhā ṛddhimṛcchati \|*  
*puṇyakṣetre kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ mahadaṇvapi jāyate \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Meritorious actions performed in a holy centre flourish in many ways. Sinful acts committed in a holy centre, though slight, become manifold.

## \|\| 1.12.37-38 \|\|

*tatkālaṃ jīvanārthaścetpuṇyena kṣayameṣyati \|*  
*puṇyamaiśvaryadaṃ prāhuḥ kāyikaṃ vācikaṃ tathā \|\| 37 \|\|*

*mānasaṃ ca tathā pāpaṃ tādṛśaṃ nāśayeddvijāḥ \|*  
*mānasaṃ vajralepaṃ tu kalpakalpānugaṃ tathā \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. If the sin committed in a holy centre is only for livelihood, the merit will destroy that sin. Merit accords prosperity and quells physical, verbal and mental sins. O brahmins, the mental sin is adamantine in sticking to the sinner and it continues for many Kalpas.

## \|\| 1.12.39-40 \|\|

*dhyānādeva hi tannaśyennānyathā nāśamṛcchati \|*  
*vācikaṃ japajālena kāyikaṃ kāyaśoṣaṇāt \|\| 39 \|\|*

*dānāddhanakṛtaṃ naśyennā'nyathākalpakoṭibhiḥ \|*  
*kvacitpāpena puṇyaṃ ca vṛddhipūrveṇa naśyati \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. The mental sin can be wiped off only by meditation and not otherwise. The verbal sin is wiped off by Japas and the physical sin by forcefully causing the emaciation of the body. Sins committed by means of wealth can be wiped off by making charitable gifts and not otherwise, though crores of Kalpas (Aeons) may elapse. In some places the increasing sin destroys the merit.

## \|\| 1.12.41-43 \|\|

*bījāṃśaścaiva vṛddhyaṃśo bhogāṃśaḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ \|*  
*jñānanāśyo hi bījāṃśo vṛddhiruktaprakārataḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*bhogāṃśo bhoganāśyastu nānyathā puṇyakoṭibhiḥ \|*  
*bījaprarohe naṣṭe tu śeṣo bhogāya kalpate \|\| 42 \|\|*

*devānāṃ pūjayā caiva brahmaṇānāṃ ca dānataḥ \|*  
*tapodhikyācca kālena bhogaḥ sahyo bhavennṛṇām \|*  
*tasmātpāpamakṛtvaiva vastavyaṃ sukhamicchatā \|\| 43 \|\|*

41-43. Both Merit and Demerit have three aspects:—the seed stage, flourishing stage and the enjoyment stage. If they are in the seed stage they can be quelled by perfect knowledge. If they are in the flourishing stage they can be quelled in the manner described before. If they are in the enjoyment stage they get destroyed only by enjoying and experiencing their fruits and not otherwise though one might have performed crores of meritorious deeds. If the seed or the flourishing seedlings are destroyed what remains must be experienced and wiped off. If one regularly performs worship of gods, makes gifts to brahmins and performs sufficient penance, the enjoyment becomes bearable. Hence those who wish for happiness must refrain from committing sins.

## Chapter 13 - Description of good conduct (sadācāra)

## \|\| 1.13.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sadācāraṃ śrāvayāśu yena lokāñjayedbudhaḥ \|*  
*dharmādharmamayānbrūhi svarganārakadāṃstathā \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. Kindly tell us the mode of good conduct (sadācāra) whereby the sensible man quickly attains higher worlds. Please tell us about virtue and evil that cause attainment of heaven or hell.

## \|\| 1.13.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sadācārayuto vidvānbrāhmaṇo nāma nāmataḥ \|*  
*vedācārayuto vipro hyetairekaikavāndvijaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. A brahmin endowed with strict adherence to good conduct is perfectly wise. A brahmin learned in Vedas and of good conduct is called a Vipra. A brahmin endowed with only one of these two is a mere Dvija.

## \|\| 1.13.3 \|\|

*alpācārolpavedaśca kṣatriyo rājasevakaḥ \|*  
*kiṃcidācāravānvaiśyaḥ kṛṣivāṇijyakṛttayā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. A brahmin following some of the prescribed rules of conduct and with a smattering of the Vedas is a Kṣatriya brahmin, at best a royal servant. Very careless in following the rules of conduct the brahmin is really a Vaiśya brahmin. One engaged in agriculture and trading activities is also likewise.

## \|\| 1.13.4 \|\|

*śūdra brāhmaṇa ityuktaḥ svayameva hi karṣakaḥ \|*  
*asūyāluḥ paradro hī caṃḍāladvija ucyate \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. A brahmin ploughing the field himself is a Śūdra brahmin. One of envious and spiteful temperament is a degraded Dvija.

## \|\| 1.13.5 \|\|

*pṛthivīpālako rājā itarekṣatriyā matāḥ \|*  
*dhānyādikrayavānvaiśya itaro vaṇigucyate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. A Kṣatriya who rules over a kingdom is a “King”; others are mere Kṣatriyas. A merchant dealing in grains etc. is a Vaiśya and others of his caste are mere “Vaṇiks”

## \|\| 1.13.6 \|\|

*brahmakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ śuśrūṣuḥ śūdra ucyate \|*  
*karṣako vṛṣalo jñeya itare caiva dasyavaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. A person rendering service to Brahmins, Kṣatriyas and Vaiśyas is called a Śūdra. A working agriculturist is a Vṛṣala and the others are Dasyus.

## \|\| 1.13.7 \|\|

*sarvo hyuṣaḥprācīmukhaścintayeddevapūrvakān \|*  
*dharmānarthāṃśca tatkleśānāyaṃ ca vyayameva ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. It is the duty of everyone of the four castes to get up early in the morning and sit facing the east and meditate on gods. He shall then think about the various acts of virtue, of matters regarding monetary dealings, the problems connected with them, the sources of income and the items of expenditure.

## \|\| 1.13.8 \|\|

*āyurdveṣaśca maraṇaṃ pāpaṃ bhāgyaṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*vyādhiḥ puṣṭistathā śaktiḥ prātarutthānadikphalam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The direction in which one casts one’s first glance on waking up indicates the good or bad that is likely to attend one on that day—the eight effects in order are—longevity, hatred, death, sin, fortune, sickness, nourishment and strength.

## \|\| 1.13.9 \|\|

*niśāṃtyāyāmoṣā jñeyā yāmārdhaṃ saṃdhirucyate \|*  
*tatkāle tu samutthāya viṇmūtre visṛjeddvijaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The last yāma (3 hours) of the night is called Uṣā and the latter half of it is sandhi (period of conjunction). A brahmin shall get up at that hour and answer the calls of nature.

## \|\| 1.13.10 \|\|

*gṛhāddūraṃ tato gatvā bāhyataḥ pravṛtastathā \|*  
*udaṇmukhaḥ samāviśya pratibaṃdhe'nyadiṇmukhaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It must be in a place far off from the house. It must be a covered place. He shall sit facing the north. If it is not possible due to any obstacle he can sit facing other directions.

## \|\| 1.13.11 \|\|

*jalāgnibrāhmaṇādīnāṃ devānāṃ nābhimukhyataḥ \|*  
*liṃgaṃ pidhāya vāmena mukhamanyena pāṇinā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He must never sit in front of water, fire, a brahmin or the idol of any god. He must screen the penis with the left hand and the mouth with the right.

## \|\| 1.13.12 \|\|

*malamutsṛjya cotthāya na paśyeccaiva tanmalam \|*  
*uddhṛtena jalenaiva śaucaṃ kuryājjalādbahiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After evacuating the bowels, the faeces should not be looked at. Water drawn out in a vessel should be used for cleaning (i.e. no one should sit inside the tank or river-water for cleaning purpose).

## \|\| 1.13.13 \|\|

*athavā devapitrārṣatīrthāvataraṇaṃ vinā \|*  
*sapta vā paṃca vā trīnvā gudaṃ saṃśodhayenmṛdā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Any way no one shall enter the holy tanks and rivers dedicated to deities, manes etc. and frequented by the sages. The rectum must be cleaned with mud seven, five or three times.

## \|\| 1.13.14 \|\|

*liṃge karkoṭamātraṃ tu gude prasṛtiriṣyate \|*  
*tata utthāya paddhastaśaucaṃ gaṇḍūṣamaṣṭakam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The penis must be cleaned with mud as large as a cucumber fruit and the quantity of mud for the purification of the rectum shall be Prasṛti (half a handful). After the purification of the excretory organs, hands and feet must be washed and gargling shall be done for eight times.

## \|\| 1.13.15 \|\|

*yena kena ca patreṇa kāṣṭhena ca jalādbahiḥ \|*  
*kāryaṃ saṃtyajya tarjjanīṃ daṃtadhāvanamīritam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. For gargling, the water can be taken in any vessel or a wooden cup; but water shall be spit outside (not in the river or tank). Washing of the teeth with any leaf or twig must be without using the index finger and outside the water.

## \|\| 1.13.16 \|\|

*jaladevānnamaskṛtya maṃtreṇa snānamācaret \|*  
*aśaktaḥ kaṃṭhadaghnaṃ vā kaṭidaghnamathāpi vā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After making obeisance to the gods of water, the twice-born shall perform the ablution with mantras. Sick or weak persons shall take bath upto the neck or hips.

## \|\| 1.13.17 \|\|

*ājānu jalamāviśya maṃtrasnānaṃ samācaret \|*  
*devādīṃstarpayedvidvāṃstatra tīrthajalena ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Sprinkling water upto the knees he shall perform the Mantrasnāna. He shall propitiate deities etc. sensibly with the water from the holy tank or river.

## \|\| 1.13.18 \|\|

*dhautavastraṃ samādāya paṃcakacchena dhārayet \|*  
*uttarīyaṃ ca kiṃ caiva dhāryaṃ sarveṣu karmasu \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. A washed dry cloth should be taken and worn in the form of paūcakaccha (wearing of the lower garment in a special way). In all sacred rites the upper cloth should also be used.

## \|\| 1.13.19-20 \|\|

*nadyāditīrthasnāne tu snānavastraṃ na śodhayet \|*  
*vāpīkūpagṛhādau tu snānādūrdhvaṃ nayedbudhaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*śilādārvādike vāpi jale vāpi sthalepi vā \|*  
*saṃśodhya pīḍayedvastraṃ pitṛṇāṃ tṛptaye dvijāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. While taking bath in the holy river or tank, the cloth worn shall not be rinsed or beaten. The sensible man shall take it to a separate tank or well or to the house itself and beat it on a rock or on a plank to the gratification of the manes, O brahmins.

## \|\| 1.13.21-23 \|\|

*jābālakoktamaṃtreṇa bhasmanā ca tripuṃḍrakam \|*  
*anyathā cejjale pāta itastannarakamṛcchati \|\| 21 \|\|*

*āpohiṣṭheti śirasi prokṣayetpāpaśāṃtaye \|*  
*yasyeti maṃtraṃ pāde tu saṃdhiprokṣaṇamucyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

*pāde mūrdhni hṛdi caiva mūrdhni hṛtpāda eva ca \|*  
*hṛtpādamūrdhni saṃprokṣya maṃtrasnānaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

21-23. The Tripuṇḍraka\[1\] shall be drawn on the forehead with the Jābālaka mantra. If anyone enters water otherwise, he will surely go to hell. According to scholarly authorities the mantrasnāna is as follows: Repeating the mantra “Āpo hi ṣṭhā”\[2\] etc. water shall be sprinkled over the head for suppressing sins. Repeating the mantra “Yasya Kṣayāya” etc. water shall be sprinkled over the joints in the legs. The order is as follows:—feet, head, chest; head, chest, feet and chest, feet, head for sprinkling with water thrice.

## \|\| 1.13.24 \|\|

*īṣatsparśe ca dauḥ svāsthye rājarāṣṭrabhaye'pi ca \|*  
*atyāgatikāle ca maṃtrasnānaṃ samācaret \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. It is enough if one performs mantra snāna when one is slightly indisposed, or when there is danger from the king or when there is civil commotion, or when there is no other way or when one is about to undertake a journey.

## \|\| 1.13.25 \|\|

*prātaḥ sūryānuvākena sāyamagnyanuvākataḥ \|*  
*apaḥ pītvā tathāmadhye punaḥ prokṣaṇamācaret \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He shall drink by way of Ācamana reciting the mantras from Sūryānuvāka in the morning or from Agni-Anuvāka in the evening and perform the ceremonial sprinkling in the middle.

## \|\| 1.13.26 \|\|

*gāyatr yā japamaṃtrāṃte trirūrdhvaṃ prāgvinikṣipet \|*  
*maṃtreṇa saha caikaṃ vai madhye'rghyaṃ tu raverdvijā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O brahmins, at the end of the Japa of Gāyatrī-mantra Arghya shall be offered thrice to the sun towards east and once also thereafter.

## \|\| 1.13.27 \|\|

*atha jāte ca sāyāhne bhuvi paścimadiṇmukhaḥ \|*  
*uddhṛtya dadyātprātastu madhyāhneṃgulibhistathā \|\| 27 \|\|*

2 7. The offering of Arghya in the morning is by lifting both the hands high up; that in the midday by letting off the water through the fingers and that in the evening by letting the water over the ground facing the west.

## \|\| 1.13.28 \|\|

*aṃgulīnāṃ ca raṃdhreṇa laṃbaṃ paśyeddivākaram \|*  
*ātmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā śuddhācamanamācaret \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the midday the sun is to be viewed through the fingers reciting the mantra prescribed for that. The circumambulation of oneself is performed (in the prescribed manner) and the pure Ācamana (without mantras) is performed.

## \|\| 1.13.29-30 \|\|

*sāyaṃ muhūrtādarvāktu kṛtā saṃdhyā vṛthā bhavet \|*  
*akālātkāla ityukto dine'tīte yathākramam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*divā'tīte ca gāyatrīṃ śataṃ nitye kramājjapet \|*  
*ādarśāhātparā'tīte gāyatrīṃ lakṣamabhyaset \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Sandhyā prayer performed before the prescribed time is ineffective. Hence Sandhyā shall be performed at the prescribed time. The expiatory rite for the omission of Sandhyā prayer for a day is the repetition of Gāyatrī a hundred times more than the usual number of times for ten days. If the omission is for ten days or more, Gāyatrī must be repeated for a hundred thousand times as atonement.

## \|\| 1.13.31-32 \|\|

*māsātīte tu nitye hi punaścopanayaṃ caret \|*  
*īśo gaurīguho viṣṇurbrahmā ceṃdra śca vai yamaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*evaṃ rūpāṃśca vai devāṃstarpayedarthasiddhaye \|*  
*brahmārpaṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śuddhācamanamācaret \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. If one omits Sandhyā for a month one has to be re-invested with the sacred thread.\[5\] For the sake of prosperity deities shall be propitiated such as Īśa, Gaurī, Guha. Viṣṇu. Brahmā, Candra (the moon) and Yama. Thereafter the entire rite shall be dedicated to the supreme Brahman and pure Ācamana shall be performed.

## \|\| 1.13.33-34 \|\|

*tīrthadakṣiṇataḥ śaste maṭhe maṃtrālaye budhaḥ \|*  
*tatra devālaye vāpi gṛhe vā niyatasthale \|\| 33 \|\|*

*sarvāndevānnamaskṛtya sthirabuddhiḥ sthirāsanaḥ \|*  
*praṇavaṃ pūrvamabhyasya gāyatrīmabhyasettataḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. Towards the right of the holy water, in a splendid prayer hall, temple or a common Maṭha, or in a stipulated place in one’s own house, one shall sit firmly with the mind in concentration and perform the Gāyatrī Japa after due obeisance to all gods. He shall not omit the practice of the Praṇava mantra.

## \|\| 1.13.35-37 \|\|

*jīvabrahmaikyaviṣayaṃ buddhvā praṇavamabhyaset \|*  
*trailokyasṛṣṭikarttāraṃ sthitikartāramacyutam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*saṃhartāraṃ tathā rudraṃ svaprakāśamupāsmahe \|*  
*jñānakarmeṃdri yāṇāṃ ca manovṛttīrdhiyastathā \|\| 36 \|\|*

*bhogamokṣaprade dharme jñāne ca prerayetsadā \|*  
*itthamarthaṃ dhiyādhyāyanbrahmaprāpnoti niścayaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

35-37. While practising the Praṇava he shall realise fully the identity of Jīva (the individual soul) with the supreme Brahman. The full implication of the Gāyatrī must be borne in the mind when the Japa is performed. “We pray to Brahmā, the creator of the three worlds, to Acyuta the sustainer and Rudra the Annihilator. We meditate on the Self-luminary that prompts us in the activities of virtue and wisdom bestowing enjoyment and salvation, the Self-luminary that is the driving force behind the sense-organs, mind, intellect and acts of volition.” The devotee who dwells thus on the meaning constantly attains the Brahman.

## \|\| 1.13.38 \|\|

*kevalaṃ vā japennityaṃ brāhmaṇyasya ca pūrtaye \|*  
*sahasramabhyasennityaṃ prātarbrāhmaṇapuṃgavaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Or if incompetent to dwell on the meaning let him at least continue the recitation of the mere mantra to keep his Brahminhood in tact. An excellent brahmin must repeat the mantra a thousand times in the morning every day.

## \|\| 1.13.39 \|\|

*anyeṣāṃ ca yathā śaktimadhyāhne ca śataṃ japet \|*  
*sāyaṃ dvidaśakaṃ jñeyaṃ śikhāṣṭakasamanvitam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Others shall repeat as many times as they can. In the midday Gāyatrī shall be repeated a hundred times; in the evening at least twenty times along with Śikhāṣṭaka \[A set of eight as the tuft i.e. eight times more than stipulated.\]

## \|\| 1.13.40-41 \|\|

*mūlādhāraṃ samārabhya dvādaśāṃtasthitāṃstathā \|*  
*vidyeśabrahmaviṣṇvīśajīvātmaparameśvarān \|\| 40 \|\|*

*brahmabuddhyā tadaikyaṃ ca sohaṃ bhāvanayā japet \|*  
*tāneva brahmaraṃdhrādau kāyādbāhye ca bhāvayet \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. He shall meditate on Vidyeśa, Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Īśa, Jīvātman and Parameśvara stationed in the twelve esoteric centre of the body from Mūlādhāra (basic support) to the Brahmarandhra (the mystical aperture at the crown of the head), as identical with Brahman with the conception of Soham (I am He) and continue the Japa. He shall then meditate on them as stationed outside the body as well.

## \|\| 1.13.42-43 \|\|

*mahattattvaṃ samārabhya śarīraṃ tu sahasrakam \|*  
*ekaikasmājjapādekamatikramya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*parasminyojayejjīvaṃ japatattvamudāhṛtam \|*  
*śatadvidaśakaṃ dehaṃ śikhāṣṭakasamanvitam \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. From Mahat tattva (the cosmic principle) there are a thousand extraneous bodies each of which is to be passed by each mantra slowly and the Jīva must be unified with the Supreme. This is the principle on which the Japa is based. This Japa for the sake of the extraneous bodies is for two thousand times with the Śikhāṣṭaka.

## \|\| 1.13.44 \|\|

*maṃtrāṇāṃ japa evaṃ hi japamādikramādviduḥ \|*  
*sahasraṃ brāhmadaṃ vidyācchatamaiṃdra pradaṃ viduḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. This is the tradition regarding the Japas. Repetition for a thousand times accords Brahmahood and that for a hundred times accords the region of Indra.

## \|\| 1.13.45 \|\|

*itarattvātmarakṣārthaṃ brahmayoniṣu jāyate \|*  
*divākaramupasthāya nityamitthaṃ samācaret \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Repetition for less number of times may guard the soul to a certain extent and bring about rebirth in the family of a brahmin. After the worship of the sun, the brahmin shall practise thus every day.

## \|\| 1.13.46 \|\|

*lakṣadvādaśayuktastu pūrṇabrāhmaṇa īritaḥ \|*  
*gāyatr yā lakṣahīnaṃ tu vedakāryena yojayet \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. A brahmin who has completed one million two hundred thousand repetitions becomes a full-fledged brahmin. A brahmin who has not completed at least a hundred thousand repetitions of Gāyatrī is not authorised in Vedic rites.

## \|\| 1.13.47 \|\|

*āsaptatestu niyamaṃ paścātpravrājanaṃ caret \|*  
*prātardvādaśasāhasraṃ pravrājīpraṇavaṃ japet \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Till he completes his seventieth year he shall follow these rules. Afterwards he can take to renunciation. After renunciation he shall repeat the Praṇava twelve thousand times in the morning every day.

## \|\| 1.13.48 \|\|

*dine dine tvatikrāṃte nityamevaṃ kramājjapet \|*  
*māsādau kramaśo'tīte sārdhalakṣajapena hi \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Omissions and deficiencies of one day must be made good the next day. If the omission is continued for a month, the atonement is repetition for one hundred and fifty thousand times.

## \|\| 1.13.49 \|\|

*ata ūrdhvamatikrāṃte punaḥ praiṣaṃ samācaret \|*  
*evaṃ kṛtvā doṣaśāṃtiranyathā rauravaṃ vrajet \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. If the omission extends beyond this, he shall take the order of Sanyāsa afresh. Then only can the defect be fully effaced. Otherwise he is sure to fall into Raurava, the terrible hell.

## \|\| 1.13.50 \|\|

*dharmārthayostato yatnaṃ kuryātkāmī na cetaraḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇo muktikāmaḥ syādbrahmajñānaṃ sadābhyaset \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Only the person who has a cherished desire shall endeavour for virtue and wealth and not others. A brahmin shall seek salvation and practise the ways of realising Brahman for ever.

## \|\| 1.13.51 \|\|

*dharmādartho'rthato bhogo bhogādvairāgyasaṃbhavaḥ \|*  
*dharmārjitārthabhogena vairāgyamupajāyate \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. From virtue wealth is derived and from wealth enjoyment. Vairāgya (non-attachment) is the outcome of enjoyment. That is to say, when one fully enjoys the pleasures by means of wealth acquired by virtuous means one comes to the stage of Vairāgya (Detached State).

## \|\| 1.13.52-53 \|\|

*viparītārthabhogena rāga eva prajāyate \|*  
*dharmaśca dvividhaḥ prokto dra vyadehadvayena ca \|\| 52 \|\|*

*dra vyamijyādirūpaṃ syāttīrthasnānādi daihikam \|*  
*dhanena dhanamāpnoti tapasā divyarūpatām \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. If the enjoyment is through the wealth acquired by other means, the result is the increase of passion alone. Dharma is twofold: one through the sacrificial offering and the other through the body by performing ceremonial ablutions in a sacred river etc. One can earn wealth through virtue and divine form through penance.

## \|\| 1.13.54 \|\|

*niṣkāmaḥ śuddhimāpnoti śuddhyā jñānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*kṛtādau hi tapaḥśloghyaṃ dra vyadharmaḥ kalau yuge \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. A person freed from desire gains purity and by purity he acquires knowledge. There is no doubt about it. In the ages of Kṛtā, Tretā and Dvāpara penance was recommended for attaining Dharma; but in the age of Kali it is the sacrificial offering that secures Dharma for us.

## \|\| 1.13.55 \|\|

*kṛtedhyānājjñānasiddhistretāyāṃ tapasā tathā \|*  
*dvāpare yajanājjñānaṃ pratimāpūjayā kalau \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. In the Kṛta age knowledge was acquired through meditation; in the Tretā through penance; in the Dvāpara through sacrifice and now in the Kali age it is through the worship of idols.

## \|\| 1.13.56 \|\|

*yādṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ vā tādṛśaṃ phalameva hi \|*  
*dra vyadehāṃgabhedena nyūnavṛddhikṣayādikam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. The fruit is in accordance with the nature of merit and sin. Deficiency, increase, decrease etc are due to the difference in the articles employed and the part of the body and items of rites.

## \|\| 1.13.57 \|\|

*adharmo hiṃsikārūpo dharmastu sukharūpakaḥ \|*  
*adharmādduḥkhamāpnoti dharmādvai sukhamedhate \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Evil is of violent character and virtue is of pleasant nature. A person becomes miserable due to evil and secures happiness on account of virtue.

## \|\| 1.13.58 \|\|

*vidyādurvṛttito duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ vidyātsuvṛttitaḥ \|*  
*dharmārjanamataḥ kuryādbhogamokṣaprasiddhaye \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. It must be known that bad conduct leads to misery and good conduct to happiness. Hence it is the duty of everyone to acquire virtue for the sake of worldly enjoyment as well as salvation.

## \|\| 1.13.59 \|\|

*sakuṭuṃbasya viprasya caturjanayutasya ca \|*  
*śatavarṣasya vṛttiṃ tu dadyāttadbrahmalokadam \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. If any one regularly offers sufficient material means to a brahmin with four members in his family, for a hundred years he will remain in Brahmaloka.

## \|\| 1.13.60 \|\|

*cāṃdrā yaṇasahasraṃ tu brahmalokapradaṃ viduḥ \|*  
*sahasrasya kuṭuṃbasya pratiṣṭhāṃ kṣatriyaścaret \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The rite of Cāndrāyaṇa performed a thousand times yields Brahmaloka. It is the duty of a Kṣatriya to establish and sustain a thousand families.

## \|\| 1.13.61-63 \|\|

*iṃdra lokapradaṃ vidyādayutaṃ brahmalokadam \|*  
*yāṃ devatāṃ puraskṛtya dānamācarate naraḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

*tattallokamavāpnoti iti vedavido viduḥ \|*  
*arthahīnaḥ sadā kuryāttapasā mārjanaṃ tathā \|\| 62 \|\|*

*tīrthācca tapasā prāpyaṃ sukhamakṣayyamaśnute \|*  
*arthārjanamatho vakṣye nyāyataḥ susamāhitaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

61-63. It yields Indraloka to him. If he maintains ten thousand families he attains Brahmaloka. According to scholars in the Vedas, a man attains the region of that deity in meditation of whom he makes charitable gifts. A man devoid of wealth shall endeavour to accumulate penance and austerities. Everlasting happiness is achieved by pilgrimages to holy centres and penances. Now I shall expound the mode of acquiring wealth through pure and lawful means.

## \|\| 1.13.64 \|\|

*kṛtātpratigrahāccaiva yājanācca viśuddhitaḥ \|*  
*adainyādanatikleśādbrāhmaṇo dhanamarjayet \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. A brahmin shall earn wealth without cringing or exerting himself too much. He can accept monetary gifts and fees for presiding over sacrifices duly performed.

## \|\| 1.13.65 \|\|

*kṣatriyo bāhuvīryeṇa kṛṣigorakṣaṇādviśaḥ \|*  
*nyāyārjitasya vittasya dānātsiddhiṃ samaśnute \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. A Kṣatriya shall earn wealth by valorous exploits and a Vaiśya by means of agriculture and cattle-breading. The charitable gifts of wealth acquired by lawful means alone, are attended with good results.

## \|\| 1.13.66 \|\|

*jñānasiddhyā mokṣasiddhiḥ sarveṣāṃ gurvanugrahāt \|*  
*mokṣātsvarūpasiddhiḥ syātparānandaṃ samaśnute \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Salvation is achieved by the acquisition of Perfect knowledge by every one with the blessings of the preceptor. Salvation is realisation of one’s own real form and the perfect bliss.

## \|\| 1.13.67 \|\|

*satsaṃgātsarvametadvai narāṇāṃ jāyate dvijāḥ \|*  
*dhanadhānyādikaṃ sarvaṃ deyaṃ vai gṛhamedhinā \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. O brahmins, men realise all these things only if they cultivate the association of good people. A householder shall make charitable gifts of everything like money, grain etc.

## \|\| 1.13.68 \|\|

*yadyatkāle vastujātaṃ phalaṃ vā dhānyameva ca \|*  
*tattatsarvaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo deyaṃ vai hitamicchatā \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. A person who desires permanent welfare for himself shall give to Brahmins fruit, grain or other articles especially when the need for the same arises.

## \|\| 1.13.69 \|\|

*jalaṃ caiva sadā deyamannaṃ kṣudvyādhiśāṃtaye \|*  
*kṣetraṃ dhānyaṃ tathā''mānnamannamevaṃ caturvidham \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Water shall always be given to the thirsty. Food shall be given to the hungry and the sick. Gift of food is of four types—field, unhusked grain (or seed), uncooked food and cooked food.

## \|\| 1.13.70 \|\|

*yāvatkālaṃ yadannaṃ vai bhuktvā śravaṇamedhate \|*  
*tāvatkṛtasya puṇyasya tvardhaṃ dāturna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*
