*yathāyogyaṃ prakurvīta kriyādīñchivabhaktimān \|\| 153 \|\|*

153\. The true devotee of Śiva shall observe the sacred rites etc. in accordance with the place, time, physical ability, possession of wealth as befitting his state.

## \|\| 1.18.154 \|\|

*nyāyārjitasuvittena vasetprājñaḥ śivasthale \|*  
*jīvahiṃsādirahitamatikleśavivarjitam \|\| 154 \|\|*

154\. The intelligent devotee shall take up his residence in a holy centre of Śiva, desist from violence to living beings, without exposing himself to undue strain, and spending only such wealth as he earns by legitimate means.

## \|\| 1.18.155 \|\|

*paṃcākṣareṇa japtaṃ ca toyamannaṃ viduḥ sukham \|*  
*athavā'hurdaridra sya bhikṣānnaṃjñānadaṃ bhavet \|\| 155 \|\|*

155\. Even water sanctified by the five-syllabled mantra is conducive to happiness like cooked food. Even the alms begged and acquired by an indigent devotee is conducive to perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 1.18.156 \|\|

*śivabhaktasya bhikṣānnaṃśivabhaktivivardhanam \|*  
*śaṃbhusatramiti prāhurbhikṣānnaṃśivayoginaḥ \|\| 156 \|\|*

156\. Charitable food of a devotee of Śiva increases devotion to Śiva. Śivayogins call such charitable food sacrificial offerings to Siva.

## \|\| 1.18.157 \|\|

*yena kenāpyupāyena yatra kutrāpi bhūtale \|*  
*śuddhānnabhuksadā maunīrahasyaṃ na prakāśayet \|\| 157 \|\|*

157\. The devotee of Śiva shall always be scrupulous about the purity of his food, wherever he stays and whatever means of sustenance he has. He shall observe silence and shall not disclose the secret.

## \|\| 1.18.158 \|\|

*prakāśayettu bhaktānāṃ śivamāhātmyameva hi \|*  
*rahasyaṃ śivamaṃtrasya śivo jānāti nāparaḥ \|\| 158 \|\|*

158\. To the devotees he shall expound the greatness of Śiva. Only Śiva can know the secret of Śivamantra. No one else.

## \|\| 1.18.159 \|\|

*śivabhakto vasennityaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ samāśritaḥ \|*  
*sthāṇuliṃgāśrayeṇaiva sthāṇurbhavati bhūsurāḥ \|\| 159 \|\|*

159\. The devotee of Śiva shall always resort to the phallic emblem of Śiva. O brahmins, one becomes Śiva by resorting to stationary phallic emblem.

## \|\| 1.18.160 \|\|

*pūjayā caraliṃgasya kramānmukto bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*sarvamuktaṃ samāsena sādhyasādhanamuttamam \|\| 160 \|\|*

160\. By worshipping the mobile phallic image the liberation is certainly gradual. Thus I have mentioned the achievable and the excellent means of achievement.

## \|\| 1.18.161 \|\|

*vyāsena yatpurāproktaṃ yacchrutaṃ hi mayā purā \|*  
*bhadra mastu hi vo'smākaṃ śivabhaktirdṛḍhā'stusā \|\| 161 \|\|*

161\. What has been mentioned formerly by Vyāsa and what has been heard by me before, has been mentioned to you. Welfare attend ye all. May our devotion to Śiva be stable and firm.

## \|\| 1.18.162 \|\|

*ya imaṃ paṭhate'dhyāyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti naraḥ sadā \|*  
*śivajñānaṃ sa labhateśivasya kṛpayā budhāḥ \|\| 162 \|\|*

162\. O scholars, whoever reads this chapter by Siva’s grace and whoever listens to this always shall acquire the knowledge of Siva.

## Chapter 19 - The worship of Śiva’s Earthen phallic image (pārthiva-liṅga)

## \|\| 1.19.1-2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta sūta ciraṃjīva dhanyastvaṃ śivabhaktimān \|*  
*samyaguktastvayā liṃgamahimā satphalapradaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*yatra pārthivamāheśaliṃgasya mahimādhunā \|*  
*sarvotkṛṣṭaśca kathito vyāsato brūhi taṃ punaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1-2. O Sūta, Sūta, be long-lived. Thou art a blessed devotee of Śiva. The greatness of Siva’s phallic image in according excellent benefit has been well explained by you.

Now speak about the greatness of Earthen phallic image of Śiva which is far superior to all others.

## \|\| 1.19.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ sarve sadbhaktyā harato khilāḥ \|*  
*śivapārthivaliṃgasya mahimā procyate mayā \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O sages, please listen all of you with great devotion and respect. Now I am going to speak on the greatness of earthly phallic image of Siva.

## \|\| 1.19.4 \|\|

*ukteṣveteṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ liṃgamuttamam \|*  
*tasya pūjanato viprā bahavaḥ siddhimāgatāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The Earthly phallic image of Śiva is the most excellent of all such images of Śiva. Many brahmins have achieved great things by worshipping it.

## \|\| 1.19.5 \|\|

*harirbrahmā ca ṛṣayaḥ saprajāpatayastathā \|*  
*saṃpūjya pārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ prāpuḥsarvepsitaṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O brahmins, Hari, Brahmā, Prajāpati and other sages have attained all they desired by worshipping this Earthly phallic image.

## \|\| 1.19.6 \|\|

*devāsuramanuṣyāśca gaṃdharvoragarākṣasāḥ \|*  
*anyepi bahavastaṃ saṃpūjya siddhiṃ gatāḥ param \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Devas, Asuras, men, Gandharvas, serpents, Rākṣasas and many others have attained greatness after worshipping it.

## \|\| 1.19.7 \|\|

*kṛte ratnamayaṃ liṃgaṃ tretāyāṃ hemasaṃbhavam \|*  
*dvāpare pāradaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pārthivaṃ tu kalau yuge \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The phallic emblem of Śiva made of precious gems was considered the best in the Kṛta age; of pure gold in the Dvāpara; of mercury in the Tretā and of earth in the Kali age.

## \|\| 1.19.8 \|\|

*aṣṭamūrtiṣu sarvāsu mūrtirvai pārthivī varā \|*  
*ananyapūjitā viprāstapastasmānmahatphalam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Among the eight cosmic bodies of Śiva, the Earthen body is the best. Since it is not worshipped by any one else O Brāhmaṇas! it yields great benefit.

## \|\| 1.19.9 \|\|

*yathā sarveṣu deveṣu jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭho maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*evaṃ sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣṭamucyate \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Just as Śiva is the oldest and the most excellent of all deities, so also his earthly phallic image is the most excellent of all.

## \|\| 1.19.10 \|\|

*yathā nadīṣu sarvāsu jyeṣṭhā śreṣṭhā surāpagā \|*  
*tathā sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Just as the celestial river Gaṅgā is the oldest and the most excellent of all rivers, so also is the earthen phallic image of Śiva the most excellent of all.

## \|\| 1.19.11 \|\|

*yathā sarveṣu maṃtreṣu praṇavo hi mahānsmṛtaḥ \|*  
*tathedaṃ pārthivaṃ śreṣṭhamārādhyaṃ pūjyameva hi \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Just as the Praṇava is considered the greatest of all mantras, so also the earthen phallic image of Śiva that is worthy to be worshipped, is the most excellent of all.

## \|\| 1.19.12 \|\|

*yathā sarveṣu varṇeṣu brāhmaṇaḥśreṣṭha ucyate \|*  
*tathā sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Just as the brahmin is spoken of as the most excellent of all Varṇas so also is the earthen phallic image of Śiva the most excellent of all other phallic images.

## \|\| 1.19.13 \|\|

*yathā purīṣu sarvāsu kāśīśreṣṭhatamā smṛtā \|*  
*tathā sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Just as Kāśī is considered the most excellent of all holy cities, so also the earthly phallic image of Śiva is spoken of as the most excellent of all other phallic images.

## \|\| 1.19.14 \|\|

*yathā vrateṣu sarveṣu śivarātrivrataṃ param \|*  
*tathā sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣthamucyate \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Just as the rite of Śivarātri is the greatest of all sacred rites so also the earthly phallic image of Śiva is the most excellent of all other phallic images.

## \|\| 1.19.15 \|\|

*yathā devīṣu sarvāsu śaivīśaktiḥ parāsmṛtā \|*  
*tathā sarveṣu liṃgeṣu pārthivaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Just as Śiva’s energy is considered the greatest of all goddesses so also the earthen phallic emblem of Śiva is spoken of as the most excellent of all.

## \|\| 1.19.16 \|\|

*prakṛtyapārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ yonyadevaṃ prapūjayet \|*  
*vṛthā bhavati sā pūjā snānadānādikaṃ vṛthā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Discarding the worship of the earthen phallic image if any one were to worship another deity, that worship becomes fruitless. Ceremonial ablutions, charitable gifts etc. are of no avail.

## \|\| 1.19.17 \|\|

*pārthivārādhanaṃ puṇyaṃ dhanyamāyurvivardhanam \|*  
*tuṣṭidaṃ puṣṭidaṃśrīdaṃ kāryaṃ sādhakasattamaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The propitiation of the earthen phallic image is sanctifying, bestower of bliss, longevity, satiety, nourishment and fortune. It must be observed by all good aspirants.

## \|\| 1.19.18 \|\|

*yathā labdhopacāraiśca bhaktyā śraddhāsamanvitaḥ \|*  
*pūjayetpārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ sarvakāmārthasiddhidam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. A devotee endowed with unflinching faith shall worship the earthen phallic image with such modes of service as are easily available. It accords the achievement of all desired objects.

## \|\| 1.19.19 \|\|

*yaḥ kṛtvā pārthivaṃ liṃge pūjayecchubhavedikam \|*  
*ihaiva dhanavāñchrīmānaṃte rudro bhijāyate \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. He who worships the earthen phallic image after constructing an auspicious altar becomes affluent and glorious here itself and becomes Rudra in the end.

## \|\| 1.19.20 \|\|

*trisaṃdhyaṃ yorcayaṃlliṃgaṃ kṛtvā bilvena pārthivam \|*  
*daśaikādaśakaṃyāvattasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He who worships the earthen phallic image in the three junctures of the threefold division of the day every day gains the bliss for twenty-one future births.

## \|\| 1.19.21 \|\|

*anenaiva svadehena rudra loke mahīyate \|*  
*pāpahaṃ sarvamartyānāṃ darśanātsparśanādapi \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He is honoured in Rudraloka with this body alone. His body dispels the sins of every man by mere sight or touch.

## \|\| 1.19.22 \|\|

*jīvanmuktaḥ sa vaijñānī śiva eva na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tasya darśanamātreṇa bhuktirmuktiśca jāyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He is a living liberated soul, he is wise, he is Śiva, there is no doubt. A mere sight of him accords enjoyment of worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 1.19.23-24 \|\|

*śivaṃ yaḥ pūjayennityaṃ kṛtvā liṃgaṃ tu pārthivam \|*  
*yāvajjīvanaparyaṃtaṃ sa yāti śivamandiram \|\| 23 \|\|*

*mṛḍenāpramitānvarṣāñchivalokehi tiṣṭhati \|*  
*sakāmaḥ punarāgatya rājendro bhārate bhavet \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. He who worships the earthen phallic emblem of Śiva every day stays in Śivaloka for so many years of Śiva, as he had been visiting Śiva’s temple in his life. If he had any wish he would be reborn in the land of Bharata as a sovereign monarch.

## \|\| 1.19.25 \|\|

*niṣkāmaḥ pūjayennityaṃ pārthivaṃliṃgamuttamam \|*  
*śivaloke sadā tiṣṭhettataḥ sāyujyamāpnuyāt \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. If a man without any desire worships every day the excellent earthen phallic image, he shall stay in Śiva’s region for ever. He shall attain the Sāyujya type of salvation.

## \|\| 1.19.26 \|\|

*pārthivaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ ca vipro yadi na pūjayet \|*  
*sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ śūlaprotaṃ sudāruṇam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. If a brahmin does not worship the earthen phallic image he shall fall in the terrible hell with a terrible trident pierced through his body.

## \|\| 1.19.27 \|\|

*yathākathaṃcidvidhinā ramyaṃ liṃgaṃ prakārayet \|*  
*paṃcasūtravidhānāṃ ca pārthivena vicārayet \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. By any means the phallic image shall be made beautiful. The Pañcasatra rite shall be performed with the earthen phallic image.

## \|\| 1.19.28 \|\|

*akhaṇḍaṃ taddhi kartavyaṃ na vikhaṇḍaṃ prakārayet \|*  
*dvikhaṇḍaṃ tu prakurvāṇo naiva pūjāphalaṃ labhet \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The earthen phallic image shall be made as a single whole. Making it piecemeal i.e if the image is made joining two or more pieces, he will never derive the merit of worship.

## \|\| 1.19.29 \|\|

*ratnajaṃ hemajaṃ liṃgaṃ pāradaṃ sphāṭikaṃ tathā \|*  
*pārthivaṃ puṣparāgotthamakhaṃḍaṃ tu prakārayet \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Whether it is made of gems, gold, mercury, crystals or Pusparāga it shall be a single whole.

## \|\| 1.19.30 \|\|

*akhaṃḍaṃ tu caraṃ liṃgaṃ dvikhaṃḍamacaraṃ smṛtam \|*  
*khaṃḍākhaṃḍavicāroyaṃ sacarācarayoḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. All mobile phallic emblems shall be a single whole. Stationary phallic images shall be made of two pieces. This is the rule about broken and unbroken phallic images both immobile or mobile.

## \|\| 1.19.31 \|\|

*vedikā tu mahāvidyā liṃgaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*ato hi sthāvare liṃge smṛtā śreṣṭhādikhaṃḍitā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The pedestal is the great Māyā; the phallic image is lord Śiva. Hence in immobile image two-piece construction is recommended.

## \|\| 1.19.32 \|\|

*dvikhaṃḍaṃ sthāvaraṃ liṃgaṃ kartavyaṃ hi vidhānataḥ \|*  
*akhaṃḍaṃ jaṃgamaṃ proktaṃś aivasiddhāntavedibhiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. This has been mentioned by those who know the principles of Śaiva cult that a stationary phallic image shall be made of two pieces.

## \|\| 1.19.33 \|\|

*dvikhaṃḍaṃ tu carāṃ liṃgaṃ kurvantyajñānamohitāḥ \|*  
*naiva siddhāntavettāro munayaḥ śāstrakovidāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Only those who are deluded by ignorance make the mobile phallic image of two pieces. The sages who know the Śaiva cult; and are well versed in Śaiva Sacred texts do not enjoin that.

## \|\| 1.19.34 \|\|

*akhaṃḍaṃ sthāvaraṃ liṃgaṃ dvikhaṃḍaṃ carameva ca \|*  
*yekurvantinarāmūḍhānapūjāphalabhāginaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Those who make a stationary phallic image as a single whole and a mobile one pieced are fools. They never reap the benefit of worship.

## \|\| 1.19.35 \|\|

*tasmācchāstroktavidhinā akhaṃḍaṃ carasaṃjñakam \|*  
*dvikhaṃḍaṃ sthāvaraṃ liṃgaṃ kartavyaṃ parayā mudā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Hence, one shall make with very great pleasure the mobile one as a single whole and the stationary one as two-pieced according to rules laid down in the sacred texts.

## \|\| 1.19.36 \|\|

*akhaṃḍe tu care pūjā sampūrṇaphaladāyinī \|*  
*dvikhaṃḍe tu care pūjāmahāhānipradā smṛtā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The worship of an unbroken mobile image yields full benefit while the worship of two-pieced mobile image brings about great harm.

## \|\| 1.19.37 \|\|

*akhaṃḍe sthāvare pūjā na kāmaphaladāyinī \|*  
*pratyavāyakarī nityamityuktaṃ śāstravedibhiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. This has been stated by those who know the lore that the worship of a stationary image of a single piece not only withholds the cherished desire but is also full of hazards.

## Chapter 20 - Worshipping an earthen phallic image by chanting Vedic mantras

## \|\| 1.20.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*atha vaidikabhaktānāṃ pārthivārcāṃ nigadyate \|*  
*vaidikenaiva mārgeṇa bhuktimuktipradāyinī \|\| 1 \|\|*

Summary: The mode of worshipping an earthen phallic image by chanting Vedic mantras.

Sūta said:—

1\. Now, the mode of worshipping an earthen phallic image according to the Vedic rites is being explained. It yields worldly pleasures and salvation to the Vedic worshippers.

## \|\| 1.20.2 \|\|

*sūtroktavidhinā snātvā saṃdhyāṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi \|*  
*brahmayajñaṃ vidhāyādau tatastarppaṇamācaret \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The devotee shall take bath in accordance with the rules prescribed in the sacred code. He shall duly perform his Sandhyā prayers. After performing the Brahma Yajña, one of the five daily sacrifices, he shall perform Tarpaṇa (a rite of offering water oblation to the manes).

## \|\| 1.20.3-4 \|\|

*naityikaṃ sakalaṃ kāmaṃ vidhāyānaṃtaraṃ pumān \|*  
*śivasmaraṇapūrvaṃ hi bhasmarudrā kṣadhārakaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*vedoktāvidhinā samyaksaṃpūrṇaphalasiddhaye \|*  
*pūjayetparayā bhaktyā pārthivaṃ liṃgamuttamam \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. After finishing the daily rites he shall apply ashes and wear Rudrākṣa, all along remembering Lord Śiva. With great devotion he shall then worship the excellent earthen phallic image according to Vedic injunctions in order to realise the full benefit.

## \|\| 1.20.5 \|\|

*nadītīre taḍāge ca parvate kānane'pi ca \|*  
*śivālaye śucau deśe pārthivārcā vidhīyate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The worship of the earthen phallic image shall be performed on the bank of a river or a tank or on the top of a mountain or in a forest, or in a Śiva temple. It must be in a clean place.

## \|\| 1.20.6 \|\|

*śuddhapradeśasaṃbhūtāṃ mṛdamāhṛtya yatnataḥ \|*  
*śivaliṃgaṃ prakalpeta sāvadhānatayā dvijāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O brahmins, he shall bring clay from a clean place and carefully make the phallic image.

## \|\| 1.20.7 \|\|

*vipre gaurā smṛtā śoṇā bāhuje pītavarṇakā \|*  
*vaiśye kṛṣṇā pādajāte hyathavā yatra yā bhavet \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. White clay is to be used by a brahmin; red clay by a Kṣatriya; yellow clay by a Vaiśya and black clay by a Sūdra. Anything available shall be used if the specified clay is not found.

## \|\| 1.20.8 \|\|

*saṃgṛhya mṛttikāṃ liṃganirmāṇārthaṃ prayatnataḥ \|*  
*atīva śubhadeśe ca sthāpayettāṃ mṛdaṃ śubhām \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. After taking the clay he shall place it in an auspicious place for making the image.

## \|\| 1.20.9 \|\|

*saṃśodhya ca jalenāpi piṃḍīkṛtya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ \|*  
*vidhīyeta śubhaṃ liṃgaṃ pārthivaṃ vedamārgataḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. After washing the clay clean with water and kneading it slowly he shall prepare a good earthen phallic image according to the Vedic direction.

## \|\| 1.20.10 \|\|

*tataḥ saṃpūjayedbhaktyā bhuktimuktiphalāptaye \|*  
*tatprakāramahaṃ vacmi śṛṇudhvaṃ saṃvidhānataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Then he shall worship it with devotion for the sake of enjoying worldly pleasures here and salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 1.20.11 \|\|

*namaḥ śivāya maṃtreṇārcanadra vyaṃ ca prokṣayet \|*  
*bhūrasīti ca maṃtreṇa kṣetrasiddhiṃ prakārayet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The material of worship shall be sprinkled with water, chanting the formula “Namaḥ Śivāy\[1\]” With the mantra “Bhūrasi” etc. the achievement of the sanctity of a holy centre (Kṣetra Siddhi) shall be effected.

## \|\| 1.20.12 \|\|

*āposmāniti maṃtreṇa jalasaṃskāramācaret \|*  
*namaste rudra maṃtreṇa phāṭikābaṃdhamucyate \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Water shall be sanctified with the mantra “Āposmān\[3\]” etc. The rite of “Phāṭikābandha” shall be performed with “Namaste Rudra” mantra

## \|\| 1.20.13 \|\|

*śaṃbhavāyeti maṃtreṇa kṣetraśuddhiṃ prakārayet \|*  
*namaḥ pūrveṇa kuryātpaṃcāmṛtasyāpi prokṣaṇam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The purity of the place of worship shall be heightened with the mantra “Śambhavāya\[5\]” etc. The sprinkling of water over Pañcāmṛta shall be performed with the word Namaḥ prefixed.

## \|\| 1.20.14 \|\|

*nīlagrīvāya maṃtreṇa namaḥpūrveṇa bhaktimān \|*  
*carecchaṃkaraliṃgasya pratiṣṭhāpanamuttamam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The excellent installation of the phallic image of Śiva shall be made devoutly with the mantra “Namaḥ Nīla-grīvāya” (obeisance to the blue-necked).

## \|\| 1.20.15 \|\|

*bhaktitastata etatte rudrā yeti ca maṃtrataḥ \|*  
*āsanaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ vai dadyādvaidikamārgakṛt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The worshipper following the Vedic path shall make devoutly the offer of a beautiful seat with the mantra “Etatte rudrāya” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.16 \|\|

*māno mahantamiti ca maṃtreṇāvāhanaṃ caret \|*  
*yāte rudre ṇa maṃtreṇa saṃcaredupaveśanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The invocation (Āvāhana) shall be performed with the mantra “Mā no mahāntam\[9\]” etc. The seating (Upaveśana) shall be performed with the mantra “Yā te rudreṇa”.

## \|\| 1.20.17 \|\|

*maṃtreṇa yāmiṣumiti nyāsaṃ kuryyācchivasya ca \|*  
*adhyavocaditi premṇādhivāsaṃ manunācaret \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. With the mantra “Yāmiṣum\[11\]” etc. the Nyāsa (ritualistic touching of the body in various parts) shall be performed. The offering of fragrance shall be performed endearingly with the mantra ‘Adhyavocat’ etc.

## \|\| 1.20.18 \|\|

*manunā saujīva iti devatānyāsamācaret \|*  
*asau yovasarpatīti cācaredapasarpaṇam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The Nyāsa of the deity shall be performed with the mantra “Asau Jīva\[13\]” etc. The rite of approaching the deity (upasarpaṇa) shall be performed with the mantra “Asau Yovasarpati” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.19 \|\|

*namostu nīlagrīvāyeti pādyaṃ manunāharet \|*  
*arghyaṃ ca rudra gāyatr yā'camanaṃ tr yaṃbakeṇa ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The water used for washing the feet (Pādya) shall be offered with the mantra. “Namostu Nīlagrīvāya”\[15\] (obeisance to the blue-necked). The water for the respectful reception (Arghya) shall be offered with the Rudragāyatrī\[16\] mantra and the sipping water (Ācamana) with the Tryambaka mantra.

## \|\| 1.20.20 \|\|

*payaḥ pṛthivyāmiti ca payasā snānamācaret \|*  
*dadhikrāvṇetimaṃtreṇa dadhisnānaṃ ca kārayet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The ceremonial ablution with milk shall be performed with the mantra “Payaḥ Pṛthivyām”\[18\] etc. The ceremonial ablution with curd shall be performed with the mantra “Dadhi Krāvṇaḥ” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.21-22 \|\|

*ghṛṭaṃ snāne khalu ghṛtaṃ ghṛtaṃ yāveti maṃtrataḥ \|*  
*madhuvātā madhunaktaṃ madhumānna iti tr yṛcā \|\| 21 \|\|*

*madhukhaṃḍasnapanaṃ proktamiti paṃcāmṛtaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*athavā pādyamaṃtreṇa snānaṃ paṃcāmṛtena ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. The ceremonial ablution with ghee shall be performed with the mantra “Ghṛtam Ghṛtayāvā”\[20\] etc. The ceremonial ablution with honey and Sugar candy shall be performed with three hymns beginning with “Madhuvātā”\[21\], Madhu Naktam\[22\], Madhumānnaḥ”\[23\]. Thus the Pañcāmṛta ablution is explained. Or the ablution with Pañcāmṛta can be performed with the Pādya mantra Namostu Nīlagrīvāya.

## \|\| 1.20.23 \|\|

*mānastoke iti premṇā maṃtreṇa kaṭibaṃdhanam \|*  
*namo dhṛṣṇave iti vā uttarīyaṃ ca dhāpayet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The tying of the waistband (Kaṭibandhana) shall be performed devoutly with the mantra “Mā nastoke”\[25\] etc. The piece of cloth to be worn on the upper part of the body shall be offered with the mantra “Namo Dhṛṣṇave” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.24 \|\|

*yā te hetiriti premṇā ṛkcatuṣkeṇa vaidikaḥ \|*  
*śivāya vidhinā bhaktaścaredvastrasamarpaṇam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The pious follower of Vedic rites shall make an offering of cloth (vastrasamarpaṇa) duly to Śiva with the four hymns beginning with “Yā te heti” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.25 \|\|

*namaḥ śvabhya iti premṇā gaṃdhaṃ dadyādṛcā sudhīḥ \|*  
*namastakṣabhya iti cākṣatānmaṃtreṇa cārpayet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The intelligent devotee shall offer scents devoutly with the mantra “Namaḥ Śvabhyaḥ”\[28\] etc. He shall offer Akṣatas (raw rice grains) with the mantra “Namastakṣabhyaḥ” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.26 \|\|

*namaḥ pāryāya iti vā puṣpa maṃtreṇa cārpayet \|*  
*namaḥ parṇyāya iti vā bilbapatrasamarpaṇam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Flower offerings shall be made with the mantra “Namaḥ Pāryāya”\[30\] etc. Bilva leaves shall be offered with the mantra “Namaḥ Parṇāya” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.27 \|\|

*namaḥ kapardine ceti dhūpaṃ dadyādyathāvidhi \|*  
*dīpaṃ dadyādyathoktaṃ tu nama āśava ityṛcā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The incense shall be offered with the mantra “Namaḥ Kapardine ca”\[32\] etc. in accordance with the rules. The lamp shall be offered in the prescribed manner with the mantra “Namaḥ Āśave” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.28 \|\|

*namo jyeṣṭhāya maṃtreṇa dadyānnaivedyamuttamam \|*  
*manunā tr yambakamiti punarācamanaṃ smṛtam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The excellent Naivedya shall be offered with the mantra “Namo Jyeṣṭhāya”\[34\] etc. Ācamana shall be offered again with the mantra “Tryambakam” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.29 \|\|

*imā rudrā yeti ṛcā kuryātphalasamarpaṇam \|*  
*namo vrajyāyeti ṛcā sakalaṃ śaṃbhaverpayet \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Fruit shall be offered with the mantra “Imā Rudrāya”\[36\]. Everything shall be dedicated to Śiva with the mantra “Namo Vrajyāya” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.30 \|\|

*māno mahāṃtamiti ca mānastoke iti tataḥ \|*  
*maṃtradvayenaikadaśākṣatai rudrā nprapūjayet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. We shall make an offering of eleven raw rice grains to the eleven Rudras\[38\] with the two mantras”Mā No Mahāntam”\[39\] etc. and “Mā Nastoke” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.31 \|\|

*hiraṇyagarbha iti tr yṛcā dakṣiṇāṃ hi samarpayet \|*  
*devasya tveti maṃtreṇa hyabhiṣekaṃ caredbudhaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The scholarly devotee shall offer sacrificial fee (Dakṣiṇā)\[41\] with the three mantras beginning with “Hiraṇya-garbha” etc. and shall perform ablution (Abhiṣeka) with the mantra “Devasya tvā” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.32 \|\|

*dīpamaṃtreṇa vā śaṃbhornīrājanavidhiṃ caret \|*  
*puṣpāṃjaliṃ caredbhaktyā imā rudrā ya ca tr yṛcā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The rite of waving lights Nīrājana for Śiva shall be performed with the mantra for the lamp (Namaḥ Āśave)\[43\]. Puṣpāñjali (offering of handful of flowers) shall be performed with devotion with the hymn Imā rudrāya” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.33 \|\|

*māno mahāntamiti ca caretprājñaḥ pradakṣiṇām \|*  
*mānastoketi maṃtreṇa sāṣṭāṇgaṃ praṇametsudhīḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The wise devotee shall then perform the Pradakṣiṇā (circumambulation) with the mantra “Mā No Mahāntam”\[45\] and the intelligent one shall perform Sāṣṭāṅga (eight limbs touching the ground) prostration with the mantra “Mā Nastoke” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.34 \|\|

*eṣate iti maṃtreṇa śivamudrā ṃ pradarśayet \|*  
*yatoyata ityabhayāṃ jñānākhyāṃ tr yaṃbakeṇa ca \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He shall show the “Śiva Mudrā” with the mantra “Eṣa te”\[47\]; the Abhayamudrā with the mantra “Yato Yataḥ”\[48\] etc. and the Jñāna Mudrā with the Tryambakamantra.

## \|\| 1.20.35 \|\|

*namaḥseneti maṃtreṇa mahāmudrā ṃ pradarśayet \|*  
*darśayeddhenumudrā ṃ ca namo gobhya ṛcānayā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The Mahāmudrā shall be shown with the mantra “Namaḥ Senā-” etc. He shall then show the Dhenumudrā with the mantra “Namo Gobhyaḥ”. etc.

## \|\| 1.20.36 \|\|

*paṃcamudrā ḥ pradarśyātha śivamaṃtrajapaṃ caret \|*  
*śatarudri yamaṃtreṇa japedvedavicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. After showing all these five Mudrās he shall perform the “Śiva Mantra Japa”. The devotee well versed in the Vedas shall recite the “Śatarudriya” mantra.

## \|\| 1.20.37 \|\|

*tataḥ paṃcāṇgapāṭhaṃ ca kuryyādvedavicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*devāgātviti maṃtreṇa kuryācchaṃbhorvisarjanam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Pañcāṅgapāṭha shall then be performed by the Vedic scholar. Then Visarjana (Ritualistic farewell) shall be performed with the mantra “Devā gātu” etc.

## \|\| 1.20.38 \|\|

*ityuktaḥ śivapūjāyā vyāsato vaidikovidhiḥ \|*  
*samāsataśca śṛṇuta vaidikaṃ vidhimuttamam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thus the Vedic rite of the worship of Śiva has been explained in detail. Now listen to the excellent Vedic rite in brief.

## \|\| 1.20.39 \|\|

*ṛcā sadyojātamiti mṛdāharaṇamācaret \|*  
*vāmadevāya iti ca jalaprakṣepamācaret \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The clay shall be brought with the mantra “Sadyo Jātam”\[53\]. The sprinkling of water shall be performed with the mantra “Vāmadevāya”.

## \|\| 1.20.40 \|\|

*aghoreṇa ca maṃtreṇa liṃganirmāṇamācaret \|*  
*tatpuruṣāya maṃtreṇāhvānaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The phallic image shall be prepared with the Aghora\[55\] mantra. The Āhvāna (invocation) shall be performed with the mantra “Tatpuruṣāya”.

## \|\| 1.20.41 \|\|

*saṃyojayedvedikāyāmīśānamanunā haram \|*  
*anyatsarvaṃ vidhānaṃ ca kuryyātsaṃkṣepataḥ sudhīḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

4 l. The phallic image of Hara shall be fixed to the pedestal with the Īśāna mantra. The intelligent devotee shall perform all other rites in brief.

## \|\| 1.20.42 \|\|

*paṃcākṣareṇa maṃtreṇa gurudattena vā tathā \|*  
*kuryātpūjāṃ ṣoḍaśopacāreṇa vidhivatsudhīḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. With the five-syllabled mantra or any other mantra taught by the preceptor the intelligent devotee shall perform, as prescribed by the rule, the adoration with due observance of the sixteen Upacāras (and the following prayer).

## \|\| 1.20.43 \|\|

*bhavāya bhavanāśāya mahādevāya dhīmahi \|*  
*ugrāya ugranāśāya śarvāya śaśimauline \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. “We meditate upon Bhava, the destroyer of worldly existence, on the great lord, on Ugra, the annihilator of terrible sins, on Śarva the moon-crested”.

## \|\| 1.20.44 \|\|

*anena manunā vāpi pūjayecchaṃkaraṃ sudhīḥ \|*  
*subhaktyā ca bhramaṃ tyaktvā bhaktyaiva phaladaḥ śivaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The intelligent devotee shall perform the worship of Śiva with this mantra or with the Vedic mantra with great devotion and abandoning errors. Śiva accords benefits when with devotion he is propitiated.

## \|\| 1.20.45 \|\|

*ityapi proktamādṛtya vaidikakramapūjanam \|*  
*procyatenyavidhiḥ samyaksādhāraṇatayā dvijaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Notwithstanding the Vedic mode of worship as stated above, O brahmins, we now proceed to explain the common procedure of Śiva’s worship.

## \|\| 1.20.46 \|\|

*pūjā pārthivaliṃgasya saṃproktā śivanāmabhiḥ \|*  
*tāṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhāḥ sarvakāmapradāyinīm \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. This mode of worship of Śiva’s earthen phallic image is the muttering of the names of Śiva. O excellent sages, it yields all cherished desires. Please listen to me. I shall explain it.

## \|\| 1.20.47-48 \|\|

*haro maheśvaraḥ śaṃbhuḥ śūlapāṇiḥ pinākadhṛk \|*  
*śivaḥ paśupatiścaiva mahādeva iti kramāt \|\| 47 \|\|*

*mṛdāharaṇasaṃghaṭṭapratiṣṭhāhvānameva ca \|*  
*snapanaṃ pūjanaṃ caiva kṣamasveti visarjanam \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. The eight names of Śiva viz:—Hara, Maheśvara, Śambhu, Śūlapāṇi, Pinākadhṛk, Śiva, Paśupati and Mahādeva shall be used respectively for the rites of bringing the clay, kneading, installation, invocation, ceremonial ablution, worship, craving the forbearance and ritualistic farewell.

## \|\| 1.20.49 \|\|

*oṃkārādicaturthyaṃtairnamontairnāmabhiḥ kramāt \|*  
*kartavyā ca kriyā sarvā bhaktyā paramayā mudā \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Each of the names shall be prefixed with Oṃkāra. The name shall be used in the dative case and Namaḥ shall be added to them. The rites shall be performed respectively with great devotion and joy.

## \|\| 1.20.50 \|\|

*kṛtvā nyāsavidhiṃ samyakṣaḍaṇgakarayostathā \|*  
*ṣaḍakṣareṇa maṃtreṇa tato dhyānaṃ samācaret \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The Nyāsa rite shall be duly performed and the Aṅganyāsa of the two hands shall also be performed. The devotee shall perform meditation with the six-syllabled mantra—Om namaśśivāya.

## \|\| 1.20.51 \|\|

*kailāsapīṭhāsanamadhyasaṃsthaṃ bhaktaiḥ sanaṃdādibhirarcyamānam \|*  
*bhaktārtidāvānalamaprameyaṃ dhyāyedumāliṃgitaviśvabhūṣaṇam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The devotee shall meditate on Śiva seated in the middle of his seat on the pedestal in Kailāsa, worshipped by Sananda and others. Śiva is a forest fire, as it were, for the dry wood in the form of the distress of the devotees. He is immeasurable. He is the Ornament of the universe being closely embraced by his consort, Umā.

## \|\| 1.20.52 \|\|

*dhyāyennityaṃ maheśaṃ rajatagirinibhaṃ cārucaṃdrā vataṃsaṃ ratnākalpojjvalāṃgaṃ paraśumṛgavarābhītihastaṃ prasannam \|*  
*padmāsīnaṃ samaṃtātsthitamamaragaṇairvyāghrakṛttiṃ vasānaṃ viśvādyaṃ viśvabījaṃ nikhilabhayaharaṃ paṃcavaktraṃ trinetram \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. He shall meditate on Śiva always in the following way:—He is like a silver mountain. He wears the beautiful moon, on his forehead. His limbs are resplendent with ornaments of gems. He holds the axe, the deer, the Mudrā of boon and the Mudrā of freedom from fear in His four hands. He is joyful. He is seated in the lotus pose. The assembled Devas stand around Him and offer prayers. He wears the hide of the tiger. He is the primordial Being, the seed of the universe. He dispels all fears. He is the three-eyed\[59\] lord with five faces.

## \|\| 1.20.53 \|\|

*iti dhyātvā ca saṃpūjya pārthivaṃ liṃgamuttamam \|*  
*japetpaṃcākṣaraṃ maṃtraṃ gurudattaṃ yathāvidhi \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. After the meditation and worship of the excellent earthly image he shall duly perform the Japa of the five-syllabled mantra taught by the preceptor.

## \|\| 1.20.54 \|\|

*stutibhiścaiva deveśaṃ stuvīta praṇamansudhīḥ \|*  
*nānābhidhābhirviprendrā ḥ paṭhedvai śatarudri yam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O foremost among brahmins, the intelligent devotee shall adore the lord of Devas with different sorts of hymns and recite the Śatarudriya mantra.

## \|\| 1.20.55 \|\|

*tataḥ sākṣatapuṣpāṇi gṛhītvāṃjalinā mudā \|*  
*prārthayecchaṃkaraṃ bhaktyā maṃtrairebhiḥ subhaktitaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He shall take raw rice grains and flowers by means of palms joined together in the form of a bowl and pray to Śiva by means of the following mantras.

## \|\| 1.20.56-60 \|\|

*tāvakastvadguṇaprāṇastvaccittohaṃ sadā mṛḍa \|*  
*kṛpānidha iti jñātvā bhūtanātha prasīda me \|\| 56 \|\|*

*ajñānādyadi vā jñānājjapa pūjādikaṃ mayā \|*  
*kṛtaṃ tadastu saphalaṃ kṛpayā tava śaṃkara \|\| 57 \|\|*

*ahaṃ pāpī mahānadya pāvanaśca bhavānmahān \|*  
*iti vijñāya gaurīśa yadicchasi tathā kuru \|\| 58 \|\|*

*vedaiḥ purāṇaiḥ siddhāntairṛṣibhirvividhairapi \|*  
*na jñātosi mahādeva kutohaṃ tvaṃ mahāśiva \|\| 59 \|\|*

*yathā tathā tvadīyosmi sarvabhāvairmaheśvara \|*  
*rakṣaṇīyastvayāhaṃ vai prasīda parameśvara \|\| 60 \|\|*

56-60. The hymn—“O Śiva, the merciful, I am Thine. Thy attributes are my vital breath. My mind is ever fixed in Thee. Knowing this, O lord of goblins, be pleased with me. Consciously or unconsciously, whatever I have performed by way of Japa or adoration may that O Śiva, with Thy favour, be fruitful. I am the greatest sinner and Thou art the greatest sanctifier. O Lord of Gaurī, knowing this, do thou whatever thou dost wish. O great lord, Thou art not known by Vedas, Purāṇas, systems of Philosophy or the different sages. O Sadāśiva, how can I know Thee? In whatever manner, I belong to Thee, O Śiva, by all my thoughtforms. I am to be saved by Thee. Be pleased with me O Siva”.

## \|\| 1.20.61 \|\|

*ityevaṃ cākṣatānpuṣpānāropya ca śivopari \|*  
*praṇamedbhaktitaśśaṃbhuṃ sāṣṭāṃgaṃ vidhivanmune \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. After repeating the hymn, the devotee shall place the flowers and the rice-grains over the phallic image of Śiva. O sages, he shall then prostrate before Śiva with devotion (his eight limbs touching the ground).

## \|\| 1.20.62 \|\|

*tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kuryādyathoktavidhinā sudhīḥ \|*  
*punaḥ stuvīta deveśaṃ stutibhiḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The intelligent devotee shall perform circumambulation in the manner prescribed. He shall pray to the lord of Devas with great faith.

## \|\| 1.20.63 \|\|

*tato galaravaṃ kṛtvā praṇamecchucinamradhīḥ \|*  
*kuryādvijñaptimādṛtya visarjanamathācaret \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Then he shall make a full-throated sound. He shall humbly bow down his head. He shall then make a formal request and perform the rite of ritualistic farewell.

## \|\| 1.20.64 \|\|

*ityuktā muniśārdūlāḥ pārthivārcā vidhānataḥ \|*  
*bhuktidā muktidā caiva śivabhaktivivardhinī \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. O foremost among sages, thus have I explained to you the procedure for the worship of the phallic image that accords worldly pleasures, salvation and increases devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 1.20.65-66 \|\|

*ityadhyāyaṃ sucittena yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi \|*  
*sarvapāpaviśuddhātmāsarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 65 \|\|*

*āyurāyogyadaṃ caiva yaśasyaṃ svargyameva ca \|*  
*putrapautrādisukhadamākhyānamidamuttamam \|\| 66 \|\|*

65-66. Whoever reads or listens to this chapter with a pure mind shall be purified of all sins and shall attain all wishes. This excellent narration bestows longevity, health, fame, heaven and happiness by way of sons and grandsons.

## Chapter 21 - Number of phallic images of Śiva used in worship

## \|\| 1.21.1-2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta sūta mahābhāga vyāsaśiṣya namostu te \|*  
*samyaguktaṃ tvayā tāta pārthivārcāvidhānakam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kāmanābhedamāśritya saṃkhyāṃ brūhi vidhānataḥ \|*  
*śivapārthivaliṃgānāṃ kṛpayā dīnavatsala \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1-2. O Sūta, O Sūta the fortunate, disciple of Vyāsa, obeisance be to Thee. Thou hast clearly explained the procedure of the worship of the earthen phallic images. Now kindly explain the number of phallic images as based on the wishes one may have. Thou art favourably disposed to the distressed and the miserable.

## \|\| 1.21.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ sarve pārthivārcāvidhānakam \|*  
*yasyānuṣṭhānamātreṇa kṛtakṛtyo bhavennaraḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O sages, you listen to the rules of procedure in the worship of earthen phallic image, by following which a man reaps full satisfaction.

## \|\| 1.21.4 \|\|

*akṛtvā pārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ yonyadevaṃ prapūjayet \|*  
*vṛthā bhavati sā pūjā damadānādikaṃ vṛthā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. If any one worships another deity without making the earthen phallic image, his worship shall be fruitless. His restraint and charitable gifts go in vain.

## \|\| 1.21.5 \|\|

*saṃkhyā pārthivaliṃgānāṃ yathākāmaṃ nigadyate \|*  
*saṃkhyā sadyo muniśreṣṭha niścayena phalapradā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The number of earthen phallic images in regard to different desires is being stipulated which will, O foremost among sages, certainly yield the benefit.

## \|\| 1.21.6 \|\|

*prathamāvāhanaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhā pūjanaṃ pṛthak \|*  
*liṃgākāraṃ samaṃ tatra sarvaṃ jñeyaṃ pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The first invocation, installation and worship are all separate. Only the shape of the phallic image is the same. Everything else is different.

## \|\| 1.21.7 \|\|

*vidyārthī puruṣaḥ prītyā sahasramitapārthivam \|*  
*pūjayecchivaliṃgaṃ hi niścayāttatphalapradam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. A person who seeks learning shall with pleasure make a thousand earthen phallic images and offer worship. Certainly he will get that benefit.

## \|\| 1.21.8 \|\|

*naraḥ pārthivaliṃgānāṃ dhanārthī ca tadarddhakam \|*  
*putrārthī sārddhasāhasraṃ vastrārthī śatapaṃcakram \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. A person desirous of wealth shall make five hundred earthen phallic images; wishing for a son—a thousand five hundred; wishing for garments—five hundred.

## \|\| 1.21.9 \|\|

*mokṣārthī koṭiguṇitaṃ bhūkāmaśca sahasrakam \|*  
*dayārthī ca trisāhasraṃ tīrthārthī dvisahasrakam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. A person desirous of salvation—a crore; desirous of lands—a thousand; craving for mercy—three thousand; desirous of a holy centre—two thousand.

## \|\| 1.21.10 \|\|

*suhṛtkāmī trisāhasraṃ vaśyārthī śatamaṣṭakam \|*  
*māraṇārthī saptaśataṃ mohanārthī śatāṣṭakam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. A person desirous of friends—three thousand; desirous of the power of controlling—eight hundred; desirous of bringing about the death of a person—seven hundred; desirous of enchanting—eight hundred.

## \|\| 1.21.11 \|\|

*uccāṭanaparaścaiva sahasraṃ ca yathoktataḥ \|*  
*staṃbhanārthī sahasraṃ tu dveṣaṇārthī tadarddhakam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. A person desirous of sweeping off his foes—a thousand; desirous of numbifying—a thousand; desirous of kindling hatred—five hundred.

I 2. A person desirous of freeing himself from fetters—a thousand five hundred. If there is fear from a great king—five hundred.

## \|\| 1.21.13 \|\|

*caurādisaṃkaṭe jñeyaṃ pārthivānāṃ śatadvayam \|*  
*ḍākinyādibhaye paṃcaśatamuktaṃ japārthivam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. If there is danger from thieves, robbers etc.—two hundred; if there is the evil influence of Ḍākinī and other foul spirits—five hundred.

## \|\| 1.21.14 \|\|

*dāridra ye paṃcasāhasramayutaṃ sarvakāmadam \|*  
*atha nityavidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇudhvaṃ munisattamāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. In poverty—five thousand. If ten thousand such are made, all wishes will be fulfilled. O great sages, I shall now mention the daily procedure. Please listen.

## \|\| 1.21.15 \|\|

*ekaṃ pāpaharaṃ proktaṃ dviliṃgaṃ cārthasiddhidam \|*  
*triliṃgaṃ sarvakāmānāṃ kāraṇaṃ paramīritam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. One such is said to remove sins. Two confer wealth. Three are mentioned as the cause for the fulfilment of all desires.

## \|\| 1.21.16 \|\|

*uttarottaramevaṃ syātpūrvoktagaṇanāvidhi \|*  
*matāṃtaramatho vakṣye saṃkhyāyāṃ munibhedataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Above this, more and more benefits accrue until the stipulated number is reached. I shall now mention another opinion coming from a different sage.

## \|\| 1.21.17 \|\|

*liṃgānāmayutaṃ kṛtvā pārthivānāṃ subuddhimān \|*  
*nirbhayo hi bhavennūnaṃ mahārājabhayaṃ haret \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. An intelligent person can certainly remain fearless after making such ten thousand images. It removes the fear from great kings.

## \|\| 1.21.18 \|\|

*kārāgṛhādimuktyarthamayutaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ \|*  
*ḍākinyādibhaye saptasahasraṃ kārayettathā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. A sensible man shall cause ten thousand such to be made for freedom from imprisonment. When there is the fear of the evil influence of Ḍākinī and other evil spirits he shall cause seven thousand such to be made.

## \|\| 1.21.19 \|\|

*sahasrāṇi paṃcapaṃcāśadaputraḥ prakārayet \|*  
*liṃgānāmayutenaiva kanyakāsaṃtatiṃ labhet \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. A person having no sons shall cause fifty-five thousand such to be made. One shall get daughters by causing ten thousand such to be made.

## \|\| 1.21.20 \|\|

*liṃgānāmayutenaiva viṣṇvādaiśvaryamāpnuyāt \|*  
*liṃgānāṃ prayutenaiva hyatulāṃ śriyamāpnuyāt \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. A devotee shall achieve the prosperity and splendour of Viṣṇu and others by making ten thousand images. He shall derive unrivalled glory and wealth by making one million images.

## \|\| 1.21.21 \|\|

*koṭimekāṃ tu liṃgānāṃ yaḥ karoti naro bhuvi \|*  
*śiva eva bhavetsopi nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Surely if a man makes a crore he shall become Śiva Himself.

## \|\| 1.21.22 \|\|

*arcā pārthivaliṃgānāṃ koṭiyajñaphalapradā \|*  
*bhuktidā muktidā nityaṃ tataḥ kāmarthināṃ nṛṇām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The worship of earthen phallic images accords the the benefit of a crore sacrifices. It gives all worldly pleasures and salvation to those who desire them.

## \|\| 1.21.23 \|\|

*vinā liṃgārcanaṃ yasya kālo gacchati nityaśaḥ \|*  
*mahāhānirbhavettasya durvṛttasya durātmanaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He who spends his time in vain without worship of such images will incur great loss. He is no better than a wicked, evil-souled man.

## \|\| 1.21.24 \|\|

*ekataḥ sarvadānāni vratāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*tīrthāni niyamā yajñā liṃgārcā caikataḥ smṛtā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. If the worship of such images is weighed against all the charitable gifts, sacred rites, holy centres, restraints and sacrifices, both will be found equal.

## \|\| 1.21.25 \|\|

*kalau liṃgārcanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tathā loke pradṛśyate \|*  
*tathā nāstīti śāstrāṇāmeṣa siddhāntaniścayaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. In the age of Kali the worship of the phallic image is excellent as is evident from what we see in the world. There is nothing else. This is the conclusion of all sacred texts and religious cults.

## \|\| 1.21.26 \|\|

*bhuktimuktipradaṃ liṃgaṃ vividhāpannivāraṇam \|*  
*pūjayitvā naro nityaṃ śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The phallic image yields worldly pleasures and salvation. It wards off different sorts of mishaps. By worshipping it, man attains identity with Śiva.

## \|\| 1.21.27 \|\|

*śivānāmamayaṃ liṃgaṃ nityaṃ pūjyaṃ maharṣibhiḥ \|*  
*yataśca sarvaliṃgeṣu tasmātpūjyaṃ vidhānataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Since the phallic image is enjoined to be worshipped even by the sages, it shall be worshipped by every one in the manner stipulated.

## \|\| 1.21.28 \|\|

*uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ nīcaṃ trividhaṃ liṃgamīritam \|*  
*mānato muniśārdūlāstacchṛṇudhvaṃ vadāmyaham \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Based on sizes the images are of three types—Excellent (Uttama), normal (Madhyama) and inferior (Nīca). O foremost of sages, I shall explain them, please listen.

## \|\| 1.21.29 \|\|

*caturaṃgulamucchrāyaṃ ramyaṃ vedikayā yutam \|*  
*uttamaṃ liṃgamākhyātaṃ munibhiḥ śāstrakovidaiḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. A phallic image, four angulas (inches) in height, with a splendid pedestal is mentioned as the most excellent by sages who are well-versed in sacred lore.

## \|\| 1.21.30 \|\|

*tadarddhaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ tadarddhamaghamaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*itthaṃ trividhamākhyātamuttarottarataḥ param \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Half of that is middling. Half of this latter is inferior. Thus I have mentioned three types of phallic images.

## \|\| 1.21.31 \|\|

*anekaliṃgaṃ yo nityaṃ bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ \|*  
*pūjayetsa labhetkāmānmanasā mānasepsitān \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He who worships many such images every day with great devotion and faith can achieve the fulfilment of any desire conceived in his heart.

## \|\| 1.21.32 \|\|

*na liṃgārādhanādanyatpuṇyaṃ vedacatuṣṭaye \|*  
*vidyate sarvaśāstrāṇāmeṣa eva viniścayaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. In the four Vedas, nothing else is mentioned so holy as the worship of the phallic image. This is the conclusion arrived at in all sacred lores.

## \|\| 1.21.33 \|\|

*sarvametatparityajya karmajālamaśeṣataḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā paramayā vidvāṃ lliṃgamekaṃ prapūjayet \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. All other rites can entirely be abandoned. A really learned man shall worship only the phallic image with great devotion.

## \|\| 1.21.34 \|\|

*liṃgercitercitaṃ sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṃgamam \|*  
*saṃsārāṃbudhimagnānāṃ nānyattāraṇasādhanam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. If the phallic image is worshipped, it means that the entire universe consisting of the mobile and the immobile has been worshipped. There is no other means to save persons submerged in the ocean of worldly existence.

## \|\| 1.21.35 \|\|

*ajñānatimirāṃdhānāṃ viṣayāsaktacetasām \|*  
*plavo nānyosti jagati liṃgārādhanamaṃtarā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Men of the world are blind due to ignorance. Their minds are sullied by worldly desires. Except for the worship of the phallic image there is no other raft to save them from destruction.

## \|\| 1.21.36-38 \|\|

*haribrahmādayo devā munayo yakṣarākṣasāḥ \|*  
*gaṃdharvāścaraṇāssiddhā daiteyā dānavāstathā \|\| 36 \|\|*

*nāgāḥ śeṣaprabhṛtayo garuḍādyāḥkhagāstathā \|*  
*saprajāpatayaścānye manavaḥ kinnarā narāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*pūjayitvā mahābhaktyā liṃgaṃ sarvārthasiddhidam \|*  
*prāptāḥ kāmānabhīṣṭāṃśca tāṃstānsarvānhṛdi sthitān \|\| 38 \|\|*

36-38. Hari, Brahmā and other devas, sages, Yakṣas, Rākṣasas, Gandharvas, Cāraṇas, Siddhas, Daityas, Dānavas, Śeṣa and other serpents, Garuḍa and other birds, all the Manus, Prajāpati, Kinnaras, men etc. have worshipped the wealth-yielding phallic image with great devotion and have achieved their desires surging in their heart of hearts.

## \|\| 1.21.39 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā pratilomajaḥ \|*  
*pūjayetsatataṃ liṃgaṃ tattanmaṃtreṇa sādaram \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Brahmins, Kṣatriyas, Vaiśyas, Śūdras, persons born of inter-caste marriages and others shall worship the phallic icon with great devotion with the respective mantras.

## \|\| 1.21.40 \|\|

*kiṃ bahūktena munayaḥ strīṇāmapi tathānyataḥ \|*  
*adhikārosti sarveṣāṃ śivaliṃgārcane dvijāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. O brahmin sages, why shall I tell much? Even women and others are authorized in the worship of the phallic image.

## \|\| 1.21.41 \|\|

*dvijānāṃ vaidikenāpi mārgeṇārādhanaṃ varam \|*  
*anyeṣāmapi jaṃtūnāṃ vaidikena na saṃmatam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The twice-born can very well worship according to the Vedic rites but not so the others who are not authorized.

## \|\| 1.21.42 \|\|

*vaidikānāṃ dvijānāṃ ca pūjā vaidikamārgataḥ \|*  
*kartavyānānyamārgeṇa ityāha bhagavāñchivaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Lord Śiva Himself has enjoined that the twice-born shall perform the worship according to the Vedic rites and not by any other means.

## \|\| 1.21.43 \|\|

*dadhīcigautamādīnāṃ śāpenādagdhacetasām \|*  
*dvijānāṃ jāyate śraddhānaiva vaidikakarmaṇi \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. But those Dvijas who have been cursed by Dadhīci, Gautama and others do not follow the Vedic rites faithfully.

## \|\| 1.21.44 \|\|

*yo vaidikamanādṛtya karma smārtamathāpi vā \|*  
*anyatsamācarenmartyo na saṃkalpaphalaṃ labhet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The man who rejects the Vedic rites and follows those laid down in Smṛtis or any other rite will not derive the conceived fruit.

## \|\| 1.21.45 \|\|

*itthaṃ kṛtvārcanaṃ śaṃbhornaivedyāṃtaṃ vidhānataḥ \|*  
*pūjayedaṣṭamūrtīśca tatraiva trijaganmayīḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The true devotee after performing worship in the prescribed manner shall worship the eight cosmic bodies (of Śiva) consisting of the three worlds.

## \|\| 1.21.46 \|\|

*kṣitirāponalo vāyurākāśaḥ sūryyasomakau \|*  
*yajamāna iti tvaṣṭau mūrtayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The Earth, the waters, the fire, the wind, the Ether, the sun, the moon and the sacrificer—these are the eight cosmic bodies.

## \|\| 1.21.47 \|\|

*śarvo bhavaśca rudra śca ugrobhīma itīśvaraḥ \|*  
*mahādevaḥ paśupatiretānmūrtibhirarcayet \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Śarva, Bhava, Rudra, Ugra, Bhīma, Īśvara, Mahādeva and Paśupati are the manifestations of Śiva who shall be worshipped with these cosmic bodies respectively.

## \|\| 1.21.48 \|\|

*pūjayetparivāraṃ ca tataḥ śaṃbhoḥ subhaktitaḥ \|*  
*īśānādikramāttatra caṃdanākṣatapatrakaiḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Then he shall worship retinue of Śiva with great devotion with sandal paste, raw rice and holy leaves in the quarters beginning with North-east.

## \|\| 1.21.49 \|\|

*īśānaṃ naṃdinaṃ caṃḍaṃ mahākālaṃ ca bhṛṃgiṇam \|*  
*vṛṣaṃ skaṃdaṃ kapardīśaṃ somaṃ śukraṃ ca tatkramāt \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. They are Īśāna, Nandī, Caṇḍa, Mahākāla, Bhṛṅgin, Vṛṣa, Skanda, Kapardīśa, Soma and Śukra.

## \|\| 1.21.50 \|\|

*agrato vīrabhadraṃ ca pṛṣṭhe kīrtimukhaṃ tathā \|*  
*tata ekādaśānrudrā npūjayedvidhinā tataḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Vīrabhadra in front and Kīrtimukha at the back. Then he shall worship eleven Rudras.

## \|\| 1.21.51-52 \|\|

*tataḥ paṃcākṣaraṃ japtvā śatarudri yameva ca \|*  
*stutīrnānāvidhāḥ kṛtvā paṃcāṃgapaṭhanaṃ tathā \|\| 51 \|\|*

*tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā natvā liṃgaṃ visarjayet \|*  
*iti proktamaśeṣaṃ ca śivapūjanamādarāt \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. Then he shall repeat the five-syllabled mantra, Śatarudriya, many Saiva hymns and read Pañcāṅga and perform circumambulation. After obeisance he shall bid farewell to the phallic image. Thus have I mentioned the worship of Śiva with due devotion.

## \|\| 1.21.53-54 \|\|

*rātrāvudaṇmukhaḥ kuryāddevakāryaṃ sadaiva hi \|*  
*śivārcanaṃ sadāpyevaṃ śuciḥ kuryādudaṇmukhaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

*na prācīmagrataḥ śaṃbhornodīcīṃ śaktisaṃhitān \|*  
*na pratīcīṃ yataḥ pṛṣṭhamato grāhyaṃ samāśrayet \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. Divine rites shall always be performed facing the north in the night. Similarly Śiva’s worship shall always be performed facing the north, not the east. Śaktisaṃhitā shall not be recited facing the north or the west since it is the back.

## \|\| 1.21.55-56 \|\|

*vinā bhasmatripuṃḍreṇa vinā rudrā kṣamālayā \|*  
*bilvapatraṃ vinā naiva pūjayecchaṃkaraṃ budhaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

*bhasmāprāptau muniśreṣṭhāḥ pravṛtte śivapūjane \|*  
*tasmānmṛdāpi kartavyaṃ lalāṭe ca tripuṃḍrakam \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. Śiva shall not be worshipped without Tripuṇḍra, Rudrākṣa and Bilvapatra. O best of sages! when the worship is on, if the ash is not available, Tripuṇḍra, (three lines on the forehead) shall be drawn with the white clay.

## Chapter 22 - On the partaking of the Naivedya of Śiva and the greatness of Bilva

## \|\| 1.22.1 \|\|

*ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ \|*  
*agrāhyaṃ śivanaivedyamiti pūrvaṃ śrutaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*brūhi tannirṇayaṃ bilvamāhātmyamapi sanmune \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O good sage, we have heard before, that the offering of eatables (Naivedya) made to Śiva should not be taken by others. Please tell us decisively about this and also about the greatness of Bilva.

## \|\| 1.22.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇudhvaṃ munayaḥ sarve sāvadhānatayādhunā \|*  
*sarvaṃ vadāmi saṃprītyā dhanyā yūyaṃ śivavratāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O sages, all of you please hear now attentively. With pleasure I shall explain everything. All of you who take up Śiva’s sacred rites are really blessed.

## \|\| 1.22.3 \|\|

*śivabhaktaḥ śuciḥ śuddhaḥ sadvratīdṛḍhaniścayaḥ \|*  
*bhakṣayecchivanaivedyaṃ tyajedagrāhyabhāvanām \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. A devotee of Śiva who is pure and clean, devoutly performing good rites and of fixed resolve shall partake of Śiva’s Naivedya. He shall abandon all thoughts which are not worthy of being entertained.

## \|\| 1.22.4 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvāpi śivanaivedye yāṃti pāpāni dūrataḥ \|*  
*bhakte tu śivanaivedye puṇyānyā yāṃti koṭiśaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Even at the sight of the Naivedya of Śiva, all sins disappear. When it is taken in, crores of merits flock in, in no moment.

## \|\| 1.22.5 \|\|

*alaṃ yāgasahasreṇāpyalaṃ yāgārbudairapi \|*  
*bhakṣite śivanaivedye śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. A thousand sacrifices are of no avail. Hundred millions of sacrifices are useless. When Śiva’s Naivedya is eaten one will attain identity with Siva.

## \|\| 1.22.6 \|\|

*yadgṛhe śivanaivedyapracāropi prajāyate \|*  
*tadgṛhaṃ pāvanaṃ sarvamanyapāvanakāraṇam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. If in a family Śiva’s Naivedya becomes popular with the members, that house becomes sacred and it can make others also sacred.

## \|\| 1.22.7 \|\|

*āgataṃ śivanaivedyaṃ gṛhītvā śirasā mudā \|*  
*bhakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnena śivasmaraṇapūrvakam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When Śiva’s Naivedya is offered it shall be accepted with pleasure and humility. It shall be eaten eagerly while remembering Siva.

## \|\| 1.22.8 \|\|

*āgataṃ śivanaivedyamanyadā grāhyamityapi \|*  
*vilaṃbe pāpasaṃbaṃdho bhavatyeva hi mānave \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. If any one who is offered Śiva’s Naivedya delays taking it immediately, thinking that it can be taken afterwards, he will incur sins.

## \|\| 1.22.9 \|\|

*na yasya śivanaivedyagrahaṇecchā prajāyate \|*  
*sa pāpiṣṭho gariṣṭhaḥ syānnarakaṃ yātyapi dhruvam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. If anyone has no inclination to take Śiva’s Naivedya he becomes a sinner of sinners and is sure to fall into hell.

## \|\| 1.22.10 \|\|

*hṛdaye candra kānte ca svarṇarūpyādinirmite \|*  
*śivadīkṣāvatā bhaktenedaṃ bhakṣyamitīryyate \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After initiation in Saiva cult, the devotee shall partake of the offerings of eatables made to the phallic image whether conceived in the heart or made of moon-slab, silver, gold etc.

## \|\| 1.22.11 \|\|

*śivadīkṣānvito bhakto mahāprasādasaṃjñakam \|*  
*sarveṣāmapi liṃgānāṃ naivedyaṃ bhakṣayecchubham \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The Naivedya of all phallic icons is called a great favour and is auspicious. A devotee after initiation into Śaiva cult shall eat it.

## \|\| 1.22.12 \|\|

*anyadīkṣāyujāṃ nṝṇāṃ śivabhaktiratātmanām \|*  
*śṛṇudhvaṃ nirṇayaṃ prītyā śivanaivedyabhakṣaṇe \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Please listen to the decision with pleasure on partaking of Śiva’s Naivedya by persons who take initiation in other cults but maintain their devotion to Siva.

## \|\| 1.22.13-15 \|\|

*śālagrāmodbhave liṃge rasaliṃge tathā dvijāḥ \|*  
*pāṣāṇe rājate svarṇe surasiddhapratiṣṭhite \|\| 13 \|\|*

*kāśmīre sphāṭike rātne jyotirliṃgeṣu sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*cāndrāyaṇasamaṃ proktaṃ śaṃbhornaivedyabhakṣaṇam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*brahmahāpi śucirbhūtvā nirmālyaṃ yastu dhārayet \|*  
*bhakṣayitvā drutaṃ tasya sarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati \|\| 15 \|\|*

13-15. With regard to the following phallic images viz:—that which is obtained from Śālagrāma stone, Rasaliṅga, liṅgas made of rock, silver, gold, crystals and gems, liṅgas installed by devas and siddhas, Kāśmīra liṅgas and Jyotirliṅgas\[2\], the partaking of the Naivedya of Śiva is on a par with the rite of Cāndrāyaṇa. Even the slayer of a brahmin if he partakes of the remains of the food offered to the God quells all his sins immediately.

## \|\| 1.22.16-17 \|\|

*caṃḍādhikāro yatrāsti tadbhoktavyaṃ na mānavaiḥ \|*  
*caṃḍādhikāro no yatra bhoktavyaṃ tacca bhaktitaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*bāṇaliṃge ca lauhe ca siddhe liṃge svayaṃbhuvi \|*  
*pratimāsu ca sarvāsu na caṃḍodhikṛto bhavet \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. In regard to Bāṇaliṅga, metallic liṅga, Siddha-liṅga and Svayambhū-liṅga and in all other idols, Caṇḍa, one of the attendants of Śiva, is not authorised. Where Caṇḍa is not authorised, the food-offering can be partaken of by men with devotion. But no man shall partake of the food-offering where Caṇḍa is authorised.

## \|\| 1.22.18 \|\|

*snāpayitvā vidhānena yo liṃgasnāpanodakam \|*  
*triḥpibettrividhaṃ pāpaṃ tasyehāśu vinaśyati \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. After performing the ceremonial ablution duly if any one drinks the water three times, all the three types of sins committed by him are quickly destroyed.

## \|\| 1.22.19-20 \|\|

*agrāhyaṃ śivanaivedyaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam \|*  
*śālagrāmaśilāsaṃgātsarvaṃ yāti pavitritām \|\| 19 \|\|*

*liṃgopari ca yaddravyaṃ tadagrāhyaṃ munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*supavitraṃ ca tajjñeyaṃ yalliṃgasparśabāhyataḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. If at all anything from Śivanaivedya is not to be taken it is that article which is actually put on the liṅga. O great sages, that what is not in contact with the liṅga is pure and as such, it can be partaken of. When it is in contact with Śālagrāma Śilā, it is pure and can be taken whether it is food-offering, leaf, flower, fruit or water.

## \|\| 1.22.21 \|\|

*naivedyanirṇayaḥ proktaṃ itthaṃ vo munisattamāḥ \|*  
*śṛṇudhvaṃ bilvamāhātmyaṃ sāvadhānatayā'darāt \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O great sages, thus I have told you the decision about food-offering, Now, hear me attentively, with devotion. I shall explain the greatness of Bilva.

## \|\| 1.22.22 \|\|

*mahādevasvarūpoyaṃ bilvo devairapi stutiḥ \|*  
*yathākathaṃcidetasya mahimā jñāyate katham \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. This Bilva is the symbol of Śiva. It is adored even by the Gods. It is difficult to understand its greatness. It can only be known to a certain extent.

## \|\| 1.22.23 \|\|

*puṇyatīrthāni yāvaṃti lokeṣu prathitānyapi \|*  
*tāni sarvāṇi tīrthānibilvamūleva saṃti hi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Whatever holy centre there is in the world finds a place under the root of Bilva.

## \|\| 1.22.24 \|\|

*bilvamūle mahādevaṃ liṃgarūpiṇamavyayam \|*  
*yaḥ pūjayati puṇyātmā sa śivaṃ prāpnuyāddhruvam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He who worships Mahādeva in the form of Liṅga at the root of Bilva becomes a purified soul; he shall certainly attain śiva.

## \|\| 1.22.25 \|\|

*bilvamūle jalairyastu mūrddhānamabhiṣiṃcati \|*  
*sa sarvatīrthasnātaḥ syātsa eva bhuvi pāvanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He who pours water over his head at the root of a Bilva can be considered to have taken his bath in all sacred waters in the earth. Verily he is holy.

## \|\| 1.22.26 \|\|

*etasya bilvamūlasyāthālavālamanuttamam \|*  
*jalākulaṃ mahādevo dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭobhavatyalam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Seeing the water basin round the foot of the Bilva tree full of water, Śiva becomes greatly pleased.

## \|\| 1.22.27 \|\|

*pūjayedbilvamūlaṃ yo gaṃdhapuṣpādibhirnaraḥ \|*  
*śivalokamavāpnoti saṃtatirvarddhate sukham \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The man who worships the root of a Bilva tree offering scents and flowers attains the region of Śiva. His happiness increases; his family flourishes.

## \|\| 1.22.28 \|\|

*bilvamūle dīpamālāṃ yaḥ kalpayati sādaram \|*  
*sa tattvajñānasaṃpanno maheśāṃtargato bhavet \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He who places a row of lighted lamps at the root of Bilva tree with reverence becomes endowed with the knowledge of truth and merges into Śiva.

## \|\| 1.22.29 \|\|

*bilvaśākhāṃ samādāya hastena navapallavam \|*  
*gṛhītvā pūjayedbilvaṃ sa ca pāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. He who worships the Bilva tree abounding in fresh tender sprouts becomes free from sins.

## \|\| 1.22.30 \|\|

*bilvamūle śivarataṃ bhojayedyastu bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*ekaṃ vā koṭiguṇitaṃ tasya puṇyaṃ prajāyate \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. If a man piously feeds a devotee of Śiva at the root of a Bilva tree he reaps the fruit thereof, ten million times more than in the usual course.

## \|\| 1.22.31 \|\|

*bilvamūle kṣīramuktamannamājyena saṃyutam \|*  
*yo dadyācchivabhaktāya sa daridro na jāyate \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He who makes a gift of rice cooked in milk and ghee to a devotee of Śiva, at the root of a Bilva tree will never become poor.

## \|\| 1.22.32 \|\|

*sāṃgopāṃgamiti proktaṃ śivaliṃgaprapūjanam \|*  
*pravṛttānāṃ nivṛttānāṃ bhedato dvividhaṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O brahmins, thus I have explained to you the mode of worship of Śiva’s phallic image with all its divisions and sub-divisions. It is of two types: one is enjoined for those who are actively engaged in worldly pursuits and the other is meant for those who have actually renounced them.

## \|\| 1.22.33 \|\|

*pravṛttānāṃ pīṭhapūjāṃ sarvapūjāṃ samācaret \|*  
*abhiṣekānte naivedyaṃ śālyannena samācaret \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The worship of the pedestal yields all cherished desires to those who are engaged in worldly pursuits. They shall perform the complete worship in a vessel.

## \|\| 1.22.34 \|\|

*pūjānte sthāpayelliṃgaṃ puṭe śuddhe pṛthaggṛhe \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. At the end of consecration, he shall offer cooked rice Śāli as food-offering. At the conclusion of worship, the phallic image shall be kept in a pure casket separately in the house.

## \|\| 1.22.35 \|\|

*karapūjānivṛttānāṃ svabhojyaṃ tu nivedayet \|*  
*nivṛttānāṃ paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ liṃgameva viśiṣyate \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. He who has renounced the world (the Nivṛtta) shall perform Karapūjā (worship in the palm of the hand). He shall offer that food to the deity which he is accustomed to take himself. The subtle phallic image is specially recommended for the Nivṛttas.

## \|\| 1.22.36 \|\|

*vibhūtyabhyarcanaṃ kuryādvibhūtiṃ ca nivedayet \|*  
*pūjāṃ kṛtvā tathā liṃgaṃ śirasādhārayetsadā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He shall offer holy ashes both for worship and food offering. At the end of worship he shall always keep the phallic image on his head.

## Chapter 23 - The glorification of the Rudrākṣa and of the names of Śiva

## \|\| 1.23.1-2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta sūta mahābhāga vyāsaśiṣya namostu te \|*  
*tadeva vyāsato brūhi bhasmamāhātmyamuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*tathā rudrā kṣamāhātmyaṃ nāma māhātmyamuttamam \|*  
*tritayaṃ brūhi suprītyā mamānaṃdayacetasam \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1-2. O Sūta, Sūta the fortunate disciple of Vyāsa, obeisance to thee. Please explain again the glorification of the holy ashes, of the Rudrākṣa and of Śiva’s names. Lovingly explaining the three, please delight our minds.

## \|\| 1.23.3-4 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sādhupṛṣṭaṃ bhavadbhiśca lokānāṃ hitakārakam \|*  
*bhavaṃto vai mahādhanyāḥ pavitrāḥ kulabhūṣaṇāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*yeṣāṃ caiva śivaḥ sākṣāddaivataṃ paramaṃ śubham \|*  
*sadā śivakathā loke vallabhā bhavatāṃ sadā \|\| 4 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3-4. It is good that you have referred to this matter that is highly beneficent to the world. You are blessed, holy and ornaments to your families since you own Śiva as your sole great favourite deity. The anecdotes of Śiva are dear to you all for ever.

## \|\| 1.23.5 \|\|

*te dhanyāśca kṛtārthāśca saphalaṃ dehadhāraṇam \|*  
*uddhṛtañca kulaṃ teṣāṃ ye śivaṃ samupāsate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Those who adore Śiva are blessed and content. Their birth is fruitful and their family is elevated.

## \|\| 1.23.6 \|\|

*mukhe yasya śivanāma sadāśivaśiveti ca \|*  
*pāpāni na spṛśaṃtyeva khadirāṃgāraṃkayathā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Sins never touch those from whose mouth the names Sadāśiva, Śiva etc. come out for ever, as they do not touch the burning charcoal of the khadira wood.

## \|\| 1.23.7 \|\|

*śrīśivāya namastubhyaṃ mukhaṃ vyāharate yadā \|*  
*tanmukhaṃ pāvanaṃ tīrthaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When a mouth utters “Obeisance to Thee, holy Śiva” that mouth (face) is on a par with holy centres destroying all sins.

## \|\| 1.23.8 \|\|

*tanmukhañca tathā yo vai paśyatiprītimānnaraḥ \|*  
*tīrthajanyaṃ phalaṃ tasya bhavatīti suniścitam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. It is certain that the benefit of making pilgrimages to holy centres accrues to one who lovingly looks at His holy face.

## \|\| 1.23.9 \|\|

*yatra trayaṃ sadā tiṣṭhedetacchubhataraṃ dvijā \|*  
*tasya darśanamātreṇa veṇīsnānaphalaṃlabhet \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O brahmins, the place where these three are found is the most auspicious one. A mere contact of the place accords the benefit of taking a holy dip in the sacred Triveṇī.

## \|\| 1.23.10 \|\|

*śivanāmavibhūtiśca tathā rudrā kṣa eva ca \|*  
*etattrayaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ triveṇīsadṛśaṃ smṛtam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Śiva’s name, the ashes and the Rudrākṣa beads—the three are very holy and are on a par with Triveṇī (the confluence of the three holy rivers).

## \|\| 1.23.11 \|\|

*etattrayaṃ śarīre ca yasya tiṣṭhati nityaśaḥ \|*  
*tasyaiva darśanaṃ loke durlabhaṃ pāpahārakam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The sight of the persons who have these three in their bodies is a rare occurrence. But when obtained it removes all sins.

## \|\| 1.23.12 \|\|

*taddarśanaṃ yathā veṇī nobhayoraṃtaraṃ manāk \|*  
*evaṃ yonavijānāti sapāpiṣṭho na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. There is no difference at all between these two—a sight of the holy man and a bath in the Triveṇī. He who does not realise this is undoubtedly a sinner.

## \|\| 1.23.13 \|\|

*vibhūtiryasya no bhāle nāṃge rudrā kṣadhāraṇam \|*  
*nāsye śivamayī vāṇī taṃ tyajedadhamaṃ yathā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The man who has no ashes on his forehead, has not worn Rudrākṣa on his body and does not utter names of Śiva shall be shunned as one does a base man.

## \|\| 1.23.14 \|\|

*śaivaṃ nāma yathā gaṃgā vibhūtiryamunā matā \|*  
*rudrā kṣaṃ vidhinā proktā sarvapāpāvināśinī \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. As said by Brahmā, Śiva’s name is on a par with Gaṅgā, the ash is equal to Yamunā and Rudrākṣa destroys all sins (and is equal to Sarasvatī).

## \|\| 1.23.15-16 \|\|

*śarīre ca trayaṃ yasya tatphalaṃ caikataḥ sthitam \|*  
*ekato veṇikāyāśca snānajaṃtuphalaṃ budhaiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*tadevaṃ tulitaṃ pūrvaṃ brahmaṇāhitakāriṇā \|*  
*samānaṃ caiva tajjātaṃ tasmāddhāryaṃ sadā budhaiḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Brahmā wishing to bestow beneficence weighed one against the other. He put on one side the benefit achieved by a person in whose body the three things were present. On the other side he put the blessedness achieved by those who took their bath in the holy Triveṇī. Both were found equal. Hence scholars shall wear these always.

## \|\| 1.23.17 \|\|

*taddinaṃ hi samārabhya brahmaviṣṇvādibhiḥ saraiḥ \|*  
*dhāryate tritayaṃ tacca darśanātpāpahārakam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. From that time onwards Brahmā, Viṣṇu and other Devas wear these three. Their very sight dispels sins.

## \|\| 1.23.18 \|\|

*ṛṣya ūcuḥ \|*  
*īdṛśaṃ hi phalaṃ proktaṃ nāmāditritayodbhavam \|*  
*tanmāhātmyaṃ viśeṣeṇa vaktumarhasi suvrata \|\| 18 \|\|*

The sages said:—

18\. O righteous one, you have explained the benefit of the three things: Śiva’s name etc. Please explain it vividly.

## \|\| 1.23.19 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ṛṣayo hi mahāprājñāḥ sacchaivā jñānināṃ varāḥ \|*  
*tanmāhātmyaṃ hi sadbhaktyā śṛṇutādarato dvijāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

19\. O brahmanical sages, you are all good devotees of Śiva, gifted with knowledge and great intellect. You are the foremost among the wise. Please listen with reverence to their greatness.

## \|\| 1.23.20 \|\|

*sugūḍhamapi śāstreṣu purāṇeṣu śrutiṣvapi \|*  
*bhavatsnehānmayā viprāḥ prakāśaḥ kriyate'dhunā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O brahmins, it is mysteriously hidden in sacred texts, Vedas and Purāṇas. Out of love for you I reveal the same to you now.

## \|\| 1.23.21 \|\|

*kastattritayamāhātmyaṃ saṃjānāti dvijottamāḥ \|*  
*maheśvaraṃ vinā sarvaṃ brahmāṇḍe sadasatparam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O foremost among the brahmins! Who ever does know the real greatness of the three except Śiva who is beyond all in the whole universe?

## \|\| 1.23.22 \|\|

*vacmyahaṃ nāma māhātmyaṃ yathābhakti samāsataḥ \|*  
*śṛṇuta prītito viprāḥ sarvapāpaharaṃ param \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Briefly I shall explain the greatness of the names as prompted by my devotion. O brahmins, do you lovingly listen to his greatness: the destroyer of all sins.

## \|\| 1.23.23 \|\|

*śiveti nāmadāvāgnermahāpātakaparvatāḥ \|*  
*bhasmībhavaṃtyanāyāsātsatyaṃsatyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Mountainous heaps of great sins are destroyed as in a blazing forest fire when the names of Śiva are repeated. They are reduced to ashes without any difficulty. It is true, undoubtedly true.

## \|\| 1.23.24 \|\|

*pāpamūlāni duḥkhāni vividhānyapi śaunaka \|*  
*śivanāmaikanaśyāni nānyanaśyāni sarvathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O Śaunaka, different sorts of miseries with sins as their roots can be quelled only by muttering Śiva’s names, and not by anything else entirely.

## \|\| 1.23.25 \|\|

*sa vaidikaḥ sa puṇyātmā sa dhanyassa budho mataḥ \|*  
*śivanāmajapāsakto yo nityaṃ bhuvi mānava \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The man who is devotedly attached to the Japas of Śiva’s names in the world, is really a follower of the Vedas, a meritorious soul and a blessed scholar.

## \|\| 1.23.26 \|\|

*bhavaṃti vividhā dharmāsteṣāṃ sadyaḥ phalonmukhāḥ \|*  
*yeṣāṃ bhavati viśvāsaḥ śivanāmajape mune \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O sage, instantaneously fruitful are the different sacred rites of those who have full faith in the efficacy of the Japas of Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.27 \|\|

*pātakāni vinaśyaṃti yāvaṃti śivanāmataḥ \|*  
*bhuvi tāvaṃti pāpāni kriyaṃte na narairmune \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O sage, so many sins are not committed by men in the world as are and can be destroyed by Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.28 \|\|

*brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ rāśīnapramitānmune \|*  
*śivanāma drutaṃ proktaṃ nāśayatyakhilānnaraiḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O sage, Śiva’s names repeated by men, immediately destroy the countless heaps of sins such as the slaughter of a brahmin.

## \|\| 1.23.29 \|\|

*śivanāmatarīṃ prāpya saṃsārābdhiṃ taraṃti ye \|*  
*saṃsāramūlapāpāni tāni naśyaṃtyasaṃśayam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Those who cross the ocean of worldly existence by resorting to the raft of the names of Śiva do definitely destroy those sins that are the root-cause of worldly existence.

## \|\| 1.23.30 \|\|

*saṃsāramūlabhūtānāṃ pātakānāṃ mahāmune \|*  
*śivanāmakuṭhāreṇa vināśo jāyate dhruvam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O great sage, the destruction of sins that are the roots of worldly existence is certainly effected by the axe of Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.31 \|\|

*śivanāmāmṛtaṃ peyaṃ pāpadāvānalārditaiḥ \|*  
*pāpadāvāgnitaptānāṃ śāṃtistena vinā na hi \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The nectar of Śiva’s names shall be drunk by those who are distressed and scorched by the conflagration of sins. Without it, the people who are scorched by the conflagration cannot have any peace.

## \|\| 1.23.32 \|\|

*śiveti nāmapīyūṣavarṣadhārāpariplutāḥ \|*  
*saṃsāradavamadhyepi na śocaṃti kadācana \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Those who are drenched by the nectarine downpour of Śiva’s names never feel ill at ease even in the middle of the conflagration of worldly existence.

## \|\| 1.23.33 \|\|

*śivanāmni mahadbhaktirjātā yeṣāṃ mahātmanām \|*  
*tadvidhānāṃ tu sahasā muktirbhavati sarvathā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The noble souls who have acquired great devotion to the names of Śiva, and those like them, attain perfect liberation instantaneously.

## \|\| 1.23.34 \|\|

*anekajanmabhiryena tapastaptaṃ munīśvara \|*  
*śivanāmni bhavedbhaktiḥ sarvapāpāpahāriṇī \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O lord of sages, devotion to the names of Śiva, that destroys all sins can be acquired only by him who has performed penances in the course of many births.

## \|\| 1.23.35 \|\|

*yasyā sādhāraṇaṃ śaṃbhunāmni bhaktirakhaṃḍitā \|*  
*tasyaiva mokṣaḥ sulabho nānyasyeti matirmama \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Salvation is easy of access only to him who has extraordinary and unbroken devotion for the names of Śiva. I believe in this.

## \|\| 1.23.36 \|\|

*kṛtvāpyanekapāpāni śivanāmajapādaraḥ \|*  
*sarvapāpavinirmukto bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Even if he has committed many sins, a person who has reverence for the Japa of Śiva’s names, becomes certainly free from all sins.

## \|\| 1.23.37 \|\|

*bhavaṃti bhasmasādvṛkṣā davadagdhā yathā vane \|*  
*tathā tāvaṃti dagdhāni pāpāni śivanāmataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Just as the trees in a forest are burnt and reduced to ashes by the forest fire, so also are the sins destroyed by Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.38 \|\|

*yo nityaṃ bhasmapūtāṃgaḥ śivanāmajapādaraḥ \|*  
*saṃtaratyeva saṃsāraṃ saghoramapi śaunaka \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O Saunaka, he who regularly sanctifies his body by the holy ashes and who performs the Japa of Śiva’s names crosses even the terrible ocean of worldly existence.

## \|\| 1.23.39 \|\|

*brahmasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā hatvāpi brāhmaṇānbahūn \|*  
*na lipyate naraḥ pāpaiḥ śivanāmajapādaraḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. A person who undertakes the Japa of Śiva’s names is not sullied by sins even after misappropriating a brahmin’s wealth and killing many brahmins.

## \|\| 1.23.40 \|\|

*vilokya vedānakhilāñchivanāmajapaḥ param \|*  
*saṃsāratāraṇopāya iti pūrvairviniścitaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. After going through all the Vedas it has been decided by our ancestors that the noblest means of crossing the ocean of worldly existence is the performance of the Japa of Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.41 \|\|

*kiṃ bahūktyā muniśreṣṭhāḥ ślokenaikena vacmyaham \|*  
*śivanāmno mahimānaṃ sarvapāpāpahāriṇam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O excellent sages, why should I say much? By means of a single verse I shall mention the greatness and efficacy of the names of Śiva or the destruction of all sins.

## \|\| 1.23.42 \|\|

*pāpānāṃ haraṇe śaṃbhornāmaḥ śaktirhi pāvanī \|*  
*śaknoti pātakaṃ tāvatkartuṃ nāpi naraḥ kvacit \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The power of the names of Śiva in destroying sins is more than the ability of men to commit them.

## \|\| 1.23.43 \|\|

*śivanāmaprabhāveṇa lebhe sadgatimuttamām \|*  
*indra dyumnanṛpaḥ pūrvaṃ mahāpāpaḥ purāmune \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O sage, formerly the king Indradyumna who was a great sinner, attained the excellent goal of the good through the influence of Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.44 \|\|

*tathā kāciddvijāyoṣā sau mune bahupāpinī \|*  
*śivanāmaprabhāveṇa lebhe sadgatimuttamām \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. O sage, similarly a brahmin woman too of very sinful activities attained the excellent goal of the good through the influence of Śiva’s names.

## \|\| 1.23.45 \|\|

*ityuktaṃ vo dvijaśreṣṭhā nāmamāhātmyamuttamam \|*  
*śṛṇudhvaṃ bhasmamāhātmyaṃ sarvapāvanapāvanam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. O excellent brahmins, thus I have told you about the surpassing excellence of the names. Now please listen to the greatness of holy ashes, the most sacred of all.

## Chapter 24 - The greatness of the holy ashes (bhasma)

## \|\| 1.24.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dvividhaṃ bhasma saṃproktaṃ sarvamaṃgaladaṃ param \|*  
*tatprakāramahaṃ vakṣye sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. The ashes (bhasma) of auspicious nature are of two types. I shall explain their characteristics. Please listen attentively.

## \|\| 1.24.2 \|\|

*ekaṃ jñeyaṃ mahābhasma dvitīyaṃ svalpasaṃjñakam \|*  
*mahābhasma iti proktaṃ bhasma nānāvidhaṃ param \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. One is known as Mahābhasma (Great ashes) and the second is known as Svalpa (the little). The Mahābhasma is of various types.

## \|\| 1.24.3 \|\|

*tadbhasma trividhaṃ proktaṃ śrotaṃ smārtaṃ ca laukikam \|*  
*bhasmaiva svalpasaṃjñaṃ hi bahudhā parikīrtitam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. It is of three types: Śrauta, (Vedic), Smārta (resulting from Smṛti rites) and Laukika (prepared from ordinary fire). The Svalpa is the ordinary ash which is of various forms.

## \|\| 1.24.4 \|\|

*śrautaṃ bhasma tathā smārtaṃ dvijānāmeva kīrtitam \|*  
*anyeṣāmapi sarveṣāmaparaṃ bhasma laukikam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The Śrauta and the Smārta ashes are to be used only by the twice-born. The Laukika can be used by every one.

## \|\| 1.24.5 \|\|

*dhāraṇaṃ maṃtrataḥ proktaṃ dvijānāṃ munipuṃgavaiḥ \|*  
*kevalaṃ dhāraṇaṃ jñeyamanyeṣāṃ maṃtravarjitam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Sages have said that the twice-born should apply the holy ashes repeating mantras. The others can simply apply without any mantra.

## \|\| 1.24.6 \|\|

*āgneyamucyate bhasma dagdhagomayasaṃbhavam \|*  
*tadāpi dra vyamityuktaṃ tripuṃḍrasya mahāmune \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. When dry cow-dung is reduced to ashes it is called Agneya (fiery). O great sage, for the sake of Tripuṇḍra this ash can be used.

## \|\| 1.24.7 \|\|

*agnihotrotthitaṃ bhasmasaṃgrāhyaṃ vā manīṣibhiḥ \|*  
*anyayajñotthitaṃ vāpi tripuṇḍrasya ca dhāraṇe \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The ashes resulting from Agnihotra and other sacrificial rites shall be used for the Tripuṇḍra by men seeking intellect.

## \|\| 1.24.8 \|\|

*agnirityādibhirmaṃtrairjābālopaniṣadgateḥ \|*  
*saptabhidhūlanaṃ kāryaṃ bhasmanā sajalena ca \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. When the ashes are put on the forehead or smeared with water, the seven mantras “Agni” etc. mentioned in the Jābālopaniṣad, shall be recited.

## \|\| 1.24.9 \|\|

*varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca maṃtrato maṃtratopi ca \|*  
*tripuṃḍroddhūlanaṃ proktajābālairādareṇa ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. People of all varṇas and Āśramas shall put on Tripuṇḍra on the forehead or dust their bodies with the mantras mentioned in the Jābāla-Upaniṣad or if no mantra is used they shall do the same with reverence.

## \|\| 1.24.10 \|\|

*bhasmanoddhūlanaṃ caiva yathā tiryaktripuṃḍrakam \|*  
*pramādādapi mokṣārthī na tyajediti viśrutiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Dusting with the holy ashes and smearing the Tripuṇḍra in horizontal parallel lines shall not be abandoned by those who seek salvation. Śruti lays down that they shall not get negligent.

## \|\| 1.24.11-12 \|\|

*śivena viṣṇunā caiva tathā tiryaktripuṃḍrakam \|*  
*umādevī ca lakṣmīṃśca vācānyābhiśca nityaśaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdrai rapi ca saṃskaraiḥ \|*  
*apabhraṃśairdhṛtaṃ bhasmatripuṃḍroddhūlanātmanā \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. Śiva, Viṣṇu, Umā, Lakṣmī, goddess of speech and other gods and goddesses, brahmins, kṣatriyas, vaiśyas and persons of mixed castes and hill tribes have observed Tripuṇḍra and dusting always.

## \|\| 1.24.13 \|\|

*uddhūlanaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ ca śraddhayā nācaraṃti ye \|*  
*teṣāṃ nāsti samācāro varṇāśramasamanvitaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Those who do not observe Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana cannot practise well the various rites of the different Varṇas and Āśramas.

## \|\| 1.24.14 \|\|

*uddhūlanaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ ca śraddhayā nācaraṃti ye \|*  
*teṣāṃ nāsti vinirmuktissaṃsārājjanmakoṭibhiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Those who do not observe with faith Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana cannot be liberated from the world even if they take ten million births.

## \|\| 1.24.15 \|\|

*uddhūlanaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ ca śraddhayā nācaranti ye \|*  
*teṣāṃ nāsti śivajñānaṃ kalpakoṭiśatairapi \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Even after hundreds of crores of Kalpas, Śiva-knowledge will not dawn upon those who do not observe with faith Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana.

## \|\| 1.24.16 \|\|

*uddhūlanaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ ca śraddhayā nācaranti ye \|*  
*te mahāpātakairyuktā iti śāstrīyanirṇayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. This is the final conclusion of all sacred texts that those who do not observe with faith Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana are tarnished by great sins.

## \|\| 1.24.17 \|\|

*uddhūlanaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ ca śraddhayā nācaranti ye \|*  
*teṣāmācaritaṃ sarvaṃ viparītaphalāya hi \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Any action performed by those who do not observe Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana with faith will give adverse results.

## \|\| 1.24.18 \|\|

*mahāpātakayuktānāṃ jaṃtūnāṃ śarvavidviṣām \|*  
*tripuṃḍroddhūlanadveṣo jāyate sudṛḍhaṃ mune \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O sage, the hatred towards Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana is kindled in the hearts of only those great sinners who hate everyone.

## \|\| 1.24.19 \|\|

*śivāgnikāryaṃ yaḥ kṛtvā kuryāttriyāyuṣātmavit \|*  
*mucyate sarvapāpaistu spṛṣṭena bhasmanā naraḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After performing the sacred rites of Śiva in the fire, the devotee who has realised the Self shall smear the forehead with the ashes repeating the mantra beginning with “Tryāyuṣā”. The moment the ashes (bhasma) come in contact with his body he will be freed from sins of his impious acts.

## \|\| 1.24.20 \|\|

*sitena bhasmanā kuryyāttrisandhyaṃ yastripuṇḍrakam \|*  
*sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivena saha modate \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He who observes Tripuṇḍra with white ashes during the three Sandhyās every day becomes free from all sins and rejoices with Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.21 \|\|

*sitena bhasmanā kuryāllāṭe tu tripuṇḍrakam \|*  
*yo sāvanādibhūtānhi lokānāpto mṛto bhavet \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He who makes the Tripuṇḍra on the forehead with white ashes shall attain, on death, the primordial worlds.

## \|\| 1.24.22 \|\|

*akṛtvā bhasmanā snānaṃ na japedvai ṣaḍakṣaram \|*  
*tripuṃḍraṃ ca racitvā tu vidhinā bhasmanā japet \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. No one shall repeat the six-syllabled mantra without applying ashes on the body. After making the Tripuṇḍra with the ashes he shall perform the Japa.

## \|\| 1.24.23-24 \|\|

*adayo vādhamo vāpi sarvapāpānvitopi vā \|*  
*uṣaḥpāpānvito vāpi mūrkho vā patitopi vā \|\| 23 \|\|*

*yasmindeśeva sennityaṃ bhūtiśāsanasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*sarvatīrthaiśca kratubhiḥ sāṃnidhyaṃ kriyate sadā \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. All holy centres and all sacrifices will be present for ever in the place where a man after having put ashes on his body stays permanently, no matter whether he is ruthless, base, sinful or commits morning sins, or is a fool or a fallen man.

## \|\| 1.24.25 \|\|
