*tripuṃḍrasahito jīvaḥ pūjyaḥ sarvaiḥ surāsuraiḥ \|*  
*pāpānvitopi śuddhātmā kiṃ punaḥ śraddhayā yutaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Even a sinful person is worthy of being honoured by Devas and Asuras if he has Tripuṇḍra on his forehead. What then of a faithful man endowed with a pure soul?

## \|\| 1.24.26 \|\|

*yasmindeśe śivajñānī bhūtiśāsanasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*gato yadṛcchayādyāpi tasmistīrthāḥ samāgatāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. All the holy centres and sacred rivers go ever to the place which a person who is endowed with Śiva Jñāna (knowledge of Śiva) and has put on ashes casually visits.

## \|\| 1.24.27 \|\|

*bahunātra kimuktena dhāryaṃ bhasma sadā budhaiḥ \|*  
*liṃgārcanaṃ sadā kāryaṃ japyo maṃtraḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Why should I say more? The sensible person shall always apply the ash, shall always worship the phallic image and shall always repeat the six-syllabled mantra of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.28 \|\|

*brahmaṇā viṣṇunā vāpi rudre ṇa munibhiḥ suraiḥ \|*  
*bhasmadhāraṇamāhātmyaṃ na śakyaṃ paribhāṣitum \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Neither Brahmā, nor Viṣṇu, nor Rudra, nor sages, nor the devas can explain adequately the greatness of the application of the ashes.

## \|\| 1.24.29 \|\|

*iti varṇāśramācāro luptavarṇakriyopi ca \|*  
*pāpātsakṛttripuṃḍrasya dhāraṇātsopi mucyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Even if a person has eschewed the duties of the different Varṇas and Āśramas, even if a person has omitted the holy rites of the Varṇas, he shall be freed from the sin if he wears Tripuṇḍra once.

## \|\| 1.24.30 \|\|

*ye bhasmadhāriṇaṃ tyaktvā karma kurvaṃti mānavāḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ nāsti vinirmokṣaḥ saṃsārājjanmakoṭibhiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Those men who exclude a man wearing Tripuṇḍra and perform holy rites are not liberated from worldly bondage even after crores of births.

## \|\| 1.24.31 \|\|

*te nādhītaṃ guroḥ sarvaṃ te na sarvamanuṣṭhitam \|*  
*yena vipreṇa śirasi tripuṃḍraṃ bhasmanā kṛtam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. If a brahmin wears the Tripuṇḍra with the ash on his forehead he must be considered as having learnt everything from the preceptor and as having performed every sacred rite.

## \|\| 1.24.32 \|\|

*ye bhasmadhāriṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā narāḥ kurvaṃti tāḍanam \|*  
*teṣāṃ caṃḍālato janma brahmannūhyaṃ vipaścitā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Those who begin to strike on seeing a person who has applied the ash are reborn of Cāṇḍāla parents. O holy one, this can be guessed by the wise.

## \|\| 1.24.33 \|\|

*mānastokena maṃtreṇa maṃtritaṃ bhasma dhārayet \|*  
*brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaścaiva prokteṣvaṃgeṣu bhaktimān \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. With great devotion Brahmins and Kṣatriyas shall apply the holy ashes over such parts of the body as are prescribed by the rule repeating the mantra “Mā nastoke” etc.

## \|\| 1.24.34 \|\|

*vaiśyastriyaṃ bakenaiva śūdra ḥ paṃcākṣareṇa tu \|*  
*anyāsāṃ vidhavāstrīṇāṃ vidhiḥ proktaśca śūdra vat \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. A Vaiśya shall apply the ashes repeating the Tryambaka\[4\] mantra and a Śūdra with the five-syllabled mantra. Widows and other women shall do like the Śūdras.

## \|\| 1.24.35 \|\|

*paṃcabrahmādimanubhirgṛhasthasya vidhīyate \|*  
*triyaṃbakena manunā vidhirvai brahmacāriṇaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. A house-holder shall repeat the Pañcabrahma\[6\] mantra etc. and a Brahmacārin shall repeat the Tryambaka-mantra at the time.

## \|\| 1.24.36 \|\|

*aghoreṇātha manunā vipinasthavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*yatistu praṇavenaiva tripuṃḍrādīni kārayet \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The Vānaprastha shall repeat the Aghora mantra and an ascetic shall observe with the Praṇava alone.

## \|\| 1.24.37 \|\|

*ativarṇāśramī nityaṃ śivohaṃ bhāvanātparāt \|*  
*śivayogī ca niyatamīśānenāpi dhārayet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. A Śivayogin being outside the pale of Varṇa and Āśrama rites because of his conception “I am Śiva” shall wear ashes with the Īśāna mantra.

## \|\| 1.24.38 \|\|

*na tyājyaṃ sarvavarṇaiśca bhasmadhāraṇamuttamam \|*  
*anyairapi yathājīvaissadeti śivaśāsanam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Śiva has ordained that the rite of wearing ashes shall not be eschewed by the people of any caste and outside the bounds of caste by other living beings.

## \|\| 1.24.39 \|\|

*bhasmasnānena yāvaṃtaḥ kaṇāḥ svāṇge pratiṣṭhitāḥ \|*  
*tāvaṃti śivaliṃgāni tanau dhatte hi dhārakaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. A person who has applied ashes on his body actually wears as many liṅgas as there are particles of the ash that remain on his body.

## \|\| 1.24.40-41 \|\|

*brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā ścāpi ca saṃkarāḥ \|*  
*striyotha vidhavā bālāḥ prāptāḥ pākhaṃḍikāstathā \|\| 40 \|\|*

*brahmacārī gṛhī vanyaḥ saṃnyāsī vā vratī tathā \|*  
*nāryo bhasma tripuṃḍrāṃkā muktā eva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. Brahmins, Kṣatriyas, Vaiśyas, Śūdras, people of mixed castes, women, widows, girls, heretics, a brahmacārin, a householder, a forest-dweller, an ascetic, performer of sacred rites and women who have Tripuṇḍra marks are undoubtedly liberated souls.

## \|\| 1.24.42 \|\|

*jñānājñānadhṛto vāpi vahnidāhasamo yathā \|*  
*jñānājñānadhṛtaṃ bhasma pāvayetsakalaṃ naram \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Just as the fire when touched with or without knowledge burns the body so does the ash worn consciously or unconsciously sanctify the man.

## \|\| 1.24.43 \|\|

*nāśnīyājjalamannamalpamapi vā bhasmākṣadhṛtyā vinā \|*  
*bhuktvāvātha gṛhī vanīpatiyatirvarṇī tathā saṃkaraḥ \|*  
*enobhuṇnarakaṃ prayāti sata dāgāyatrijāpena tadvarṇānāṃ tu yatestu mukhyapraṇavājapena muktaṃbhavet \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. No man shall drink or eat even a bit without applying Bhasma or wearing Rudrākṣa. If he eats or drinks, whether he is a householder or Vānaprastha or an ascetic, a man of the four castes or of mixed caste, he becomes a sinner and goes to hell. If a man of the four castes repeats Gāyatrī or if an ascetic repeats the Praṇava he shall be liberated.

## \|\| 1.24.44 \|\|

*tripuṃḍraṃ ye viniṃdaṃti nindanti śivameva te \|*  
*dhārayaṃti ca ye bhaktyā dhārayanti tameva te \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Those who censure Tripuṇḍra actually censure Śiva. Those who wear it with devotion actually wear Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.45 \|\|

*dhigbhasmarahitaṃ bhālaṃ dhiggrāmamaśivālayam \|*  
*dhiganīśārcanaṃ janma dhigvidyāmaśivāśrayām \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Fie upon the forehead that is devoid of ash. Fie upon the village that has no Śiva temple. Fie upon that life that does not worship Śiva. Fie upon the lore that does not refer to Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.46 \|\|

*ye niṃdaṃti maheśvaraṃ trijagatāmādhārabhūtaṃ haraṃ ye nindaṃti tripuṃḍradhāraṇakaraṃ doṣastu taddarśane \|*  
*te vai saṃkarasūkarāsurakharaśvakroṣṭukīṭopamā jātā eva bhavaṃti pāpaparamāstenārakāḥ kevalam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Great indeed is the sin accruing even from the sight of those who censure Śiva who is the support of three worlds and those who censure the man wearing Tripuṇḍra on his forehead. They are on a par with pigs of rubbish heap, demons, donkeys, dogs, jackals and worms. Such sinful persons are hellish fiends even from their very birth.

## \|\| 1.24.47 \|\|

*te dṛṣṭvā śaśibhāskarau niśi dine svapnepi no kevalaṃ paśyaṃtu śrutirudra sūktajapato mucyeta tenādṛtāḥ \|*  
*satsaṃbhāṣaṇato bhaveddhi narakaṃ nistāravānāsthitaṃ ye bhasmādividhāraṇaṃ hi puruṣaṃ niṃdaṃti maṃdā hi te \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. They may not see the sun during the day and the moon during the night. They may not see them even during sleep. They may be freed by repeating the Vedic Rudra Sūkta. Those who censure a person wearing the Tripuṇḍra are fools. A mere talk with them may cause the fall into hell. There is no way of saving then.

## \|\| 1.24.48 \|\|

*na tāṃtrikastvadhikṛto norddhvapuṃḍradharo mune \|*  
*saṃtaptacakracihnotra śivayajñe bahiṣkṛtaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O sage, tāntrika is not authorised in a Śivayajña nor a person having Ūrdhvapuṇḍra (worn on the forehead by vertical mark by a Vaiṣṇava). A person marked with a heated wheel (a mark of a Vaiṣṇava) is excluded from Sivayajña.

## \|\| 1.24.49 \|\|

*tatraite bahavo lokā bṛhajjābālacoditāḥ \|*  
*te vicāryāḥ prayatnena tato bhasmarato bhavet \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. There are many worlds to be attained as explained in Bṛhajjābāla Upaniṣad; taking that into consideration a man shall be devoted to the ashes (bhasma).

## \|\| 1.24.50 \|\|

*yaccaṃdanaiścaṃdanakepi miśraṃ dhāryaṃ hi bhasmaiva tripuṃḍrabhasmanā \|*  
*vibhūtibhālopari kiṃcanāpi dhāryaṃ sadā no yadi saṃtibuddhayaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Just as sandal paste alone can be applied over sandal paste, so also only the ash shall be applied over the sacred mark on the forehead. A sensible person will not apply anything over the forehead that wears the ornamental mark of ashes on it.

## \|\| 1.24.51 \|\|

*strībhistripuṇḍramalakāvadhi dhāraṇīyaṃ bhasma dvijādibhiratho vidhavābhirevam \|*  
*tadvatsadāśramavatāṃ viśadāvibhūtirdhāryāpavargaphaladā sakalāghahantrī \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The Tripuṇḍra shall be applied upto the forelocks by women. Brahmins and widows shall apply the ash also. Similarly it shall be applied by persons of all Āramas. Thus it bestows salvation and destroys all sins.

## \|\| 1.24.52 \|\|

*tripuṇḍraṃ kurute yastu bhasmanā vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*mahāpātakasaṃghātairmucyate copapātakaiḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. He who makes Tripuṇḍra duly with the ash is freed from groups of great as well as small sins.

## \|\| 1.24.53-54 \|\|

*brahmacārī gṛhastho vā vānaprasthotha vā yatiḥ \|*  
*brahmakṣattrāśca viṭśūdrā stathānye patitādhamāḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

*uddhūlanaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ ca dhṛtvā śuddhā bhavaṃti ca \|*  
*bhasmano vidhinā samyakpāparāśiṃ vihāya ca \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. A Brahmacārin, a householder, a forest-dweller or an ascetic, brahmins, kṣatriyas, vaiśyas, śūdras, the low and the base people become pure by means of Tripuṇḍra and Uddhūlana applied according to the prescribed manner and get their heaps of sins destroyed.

## \|\| 1.24.55 \|\|

*bhasmadhārī viśeṣeṇa strīgohatyādipātakaiḥ \|*  
*vīrahatyāśvahatyābhyāṃ mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. A person regularly applying the ash (bhasma) is freed of the sins of slaughter of women and cows and that of heroes and horses. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 1.24.56-60 \|\|

*paradra vyāpaharaṇaṃ paradārābhimarśanam \|*  
*paranindā parakṣetraharaṇaṃ parapīḍanam \|\| 56 \|\|*

*sasyārāmādiharaṇaṃ gṛhadāhādikarma ca \|*  
*gohiraṇyamahiṣyāditilakambalavāsasām \|\| 57 \|\|*

*annadhānyajalādīnāṃ nīcebhyaśca parigrahaḥ \|*  
*daśaveśyāmataṃgīṣu vṛṣalīṣu naṭīṣu ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

*rajasvalāsu kanyāsu vidhavāsu ca maithunam \|*  
*māṃsacarmarasādīnāṃ lavaṇasya ca vikrayaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

*paiśunyaṃ kūṭavādaśca sākṣimithyābhilāṣiṇām \|*  
*evamādīnyasaṃkhyāni pāpāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*sadya eva vinaśyaṃti tripuṃḍrasya ca dhāraṇāt \|\| 60 \|\|*

56-60. By means of Tripuṇḍra, the following and similar others of innumerable sorts are destroyed immediately:—Theft of others’ wealth, outraging the modesty of other men’s wives, censuring others, usurping and forcibly occupying others’ fields, harassing others, theft of plants, parks etc, incendiarism, acceptance from base people of the gifts of cow, gold, buffalo, gingelly seeds, blankets, cloths, cooked rice, food-grains, water etc; sexual intercourse with prostitutes, women of the tribal castes, fisher women, slave women, actresses, widows, virgins and women in their menstrual periods, selling of flesh, hides, gravy etc. and salt, calumny perjury, deceitful arguments and utterance of falsehood.

## \|\| 1.24.61 \|\|

*śivadra vyāpaharaṇaṃ śivaniṃdā ca kutracit \|*  
*niṃdā ca śivabhaktānāṃ prāyaścittairna śuddhyati \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The theft of Śiva’s property, censure of Śiva in certain places and the censure of the devotees of Śiva can be dispelled by the rites of expiation.

## \|\| 1.24.62 \|\|

*rudrā kṣaṃ yasya gātreṣu lalāṭe tu tripaṃḍrakam \|*  
*sacāṃḍālopi saṃpūjyassarvavarṇottamottamaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Even a Cāṇḍāla who wears Rudrākṣa over his body and the Tripuṇḍra on his forehead, is worthy of respect. He is the most excellent of all castes.

## \|\| 1.24.63 \|\|

*yāni tīrthāni lokesmingaṃgādyāssaritaśca yāḥ \|*  
*snāto bhavati sarvatra lalāṭe yastripuṃḍrakam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. He who wears the Tripuṇḍraka on his forehead gains the same merit as one who takes his bath in the sacred rivers like Gaṅgā and whatever other sacred ponds, lakes and holy centres there are in the world.

## \|\| 1.24.64 \|\|

*saptakoṭi mahāmaṃtrāḥ paṃcākṣarapurassarāḥ \|*  
*tathānye koṭiśo maṃtrāḥ śaivakaivalyahetavaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The five-syllabled mantra which grants Śiva Kaivalya is on a par with seven crores of great and many crores of other mantras.

## \|\| 1.24.65 \|\|

*anye maṃtrāśca devānāṃ sarvasaukhyakarā mune \|*  
*te sarve tasya vaśyāḥ syuryo bibharti tripuṃḍrakam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. O sage, mantras of other deities bestowing all blessedness are easily accessible to the devotee who wears the Tripuṇḍra.

## \|\| 1.24.66 \|\|

*sahasraṃ pūrvajātānāṃ sahasraṃ janayiṣyatām \|*  
*svavaṃśajānāṃ jñātīnāmuddharedyastripuṃḍrakṛt \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. He who wears Tripuṇḍra raises a thousand predecessors and a thousand successors in his family.

## \|\| 1.24.67 \|\|

*iha bhuktvā khilānbhogāndīrghāyurvyādhivarjitaḥ \|*  
*jīvitāṃte ca maraṇaṃ sukhenaiva prapadyate \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. In this life he will enjoy all worldly pleasures and live long without any disease. At the end of the span of his life he will have a peaceful death.

## \|\| 1.24.68-70 \|\|

*aṣṭaiśvaryaguṇopetaṃ prāpya divyavapuḥ śivam \|*  
*divyaṃ vimānamāruhya divyatridaśasevitam \|\| 68 \|\|*

*vidyādharāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ gaṃdharvāṇāṃ mahaujasām \|*  
*iṃdrā dilokapālānāṃ lokeṣu ca yathākramam \|\| 69 \|\|*

*bhuktvā bhogānsuvipulānprajeśānāṃ padeṣu ca \|*  
*brahmaṇaḥ padamāsādya tatra kanyāśataṃ ramet \|\| 70 \|\|*

68-70. He will assume then a divine auspicious body endowed with eight accomplishments. He will travel by a divine aerial chariot attended by celestial gods. He will enjoy the pleasures of Vidyādharas, powerful Gandharvas, in the worlds of Indra and other guardians of the quarters and those of Prajāpatis and finally reach Brahmā’s region where he will sport with a hundred virgins.

## \|\| 1.24.71 \|\|

*tatra brahmāyuṣo mānaṃ bhuktvā bhogānanekaśaḥ \|*  
*viṣṇorloke labhedbhogaṃ yāvadbrahmaśatātyayaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. He will enjoy different kinds of pleasures there for the full period of the span of life of Brahmā. He will then enjoy the pleasures in the Viṣṇuloka till hundred Brahmas die.

## \|\| 1.24.72 \|\|

*śivalokaṃ tataḥ prāpya labdhveṣṭaṃ kāmamakṣayam \|*  
*śivasāyujyamāpnoti saṃśayo nātra jāyate \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Thereafter he will attain Śivaloka and enjoy everlasting bliss there. Finally he will attain Śivasāyujya. No suspicion need be entertained in this matter.

## \|\| 1.24.73 \|\|

*sarvopaniṣadāṃ sāraṃ samālokya muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*idameva hi nirṇītaṃ paraṃ śreyastripuṃḍrakam \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. After going through the essence of all Upaniṣads again and again, this is what has been arrived at that the Tripuṇḍra is conducive to great excellence.

## \|\| 1.24.74 \|\|

*vibhūtiṃ niṃdate yo vai brāhmaṇaḥ sonyajātakaḥ \|*  
*yāti ca narake ghore yāvadbrahmā caturmukhaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. A brahmin who censures the ash is no longer a brahmin but of another low caste. He will undergo the tortures of terrible hell for the period of the span of life of the four-faced Brahmā.

## \|\| 1.24.75 \|\|

*śrāddhe yajñe jape home vaiśvadeve surārcane \|*  
*dhṛtatripuṃḍraḥ pūtātmā mṛtyuṃ jayati mānavaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. A man who wears the Tripuṇḍra while performing Śrāddha, Yajña, Japa, Homa, Vaiśvadeva and the worship of the deities is a purified soul and he conquers even death.

## \|\| 1.24.76 \|\|

*jalasnānaṃ malatyāge bhasmasnānaṃ sadā śuci \|*  
*maṃtrasnānaṃ haretpāpaṃ jñānasnāne paraṃ padam \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. When impurities are evacuated, a bath with water shall be performed; a bath with the ash (bhasma) is always purificatory; a bath with mantras removes sin and if a bath with knowledge is taken, the greatest goal will be reached.

## \|\| 1.24.77 \|\|

*sarvatīrtheṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalam \|*  
*tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti bhasmasnānakaro naraḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. A man who takes the bath of ashes derives that benefit which all holy centres accord. He gets the merit thereof.

## \|\| 1.24.78 \|\|

*bhasmasnānaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ gaṃgāsnānaṃ dine dine \|*  
*bhasmarūpī śivaḥ sākṣādbhasma trailokyapāvanam \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Bath with the ash is a holy centre where Gaṅgā Snāna is possible every day. Śiva is represented by the ash which directly sanctifies the three worlds.

## \|\| 1.24.79 \|\|

*na tadūnaṃ na taddhyānaṃ na taddānaṃ japo na saḥ \|*  
*tripuṃḍreṇa vināyena vipreṇa yadanuṣṭhitam \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Infructuous is the knowledge, meditation, gift and japa if these are performed by a Brāhmaṇa without wearing Tripuṇḍraka.

## \|\| 1.24.80 \|\|

*vānaprasthasya kanyānāṃ dīkṣāhīnanṛṇāṃ tathā \|*  
*madhyāhnātprāgjalairyuktaṃ parato jalavarjitam \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. A forest-dweller, virgins and men without initiation shall apply the ash pasted in water upto the midday and thereafter without water.

## \|\| 1.24.81 \|\|

*evaṃ tripuṃḍraṃ yaḥ kuryyānnityaṃ niyatamānasaḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktaḥ savijñeyo bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca viṃdati \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. He who wears Tripuṇḍra like this regularly with a pure controlled mind must be considered a true devotee of Śiva. He derives worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 1.24.82 \|\|

*yasyāṃgenaiva rudrā kṣa ekopi bahupuṇyadaḥ \|*  
*tasya janmanirarthaṃ syāttripuṃḍrarahito yadi \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. If a person does not wear a bead of Rudrākṣa which accords many merits, if he is devoid of Tripuṇḍra as well, his life becomes futile.

## \|\| 1.24.83 \|\|

*evaṃ tripuṃḍramāhātmyaṃ samāsātkathitaṃ mayā \|*  
*rahasyaṃ sarvajaṃtūnāṃ gopanīyamidaṃ tvayā \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. Thus I have briefly told you the greatness of Tripuṇḍra. This is a secret to be safely guarded by you from all living beings.

## \|\| 1.24.84 \|\|

*tisro rekhā bhavaṃtyeva sthāneṣu munipuṃgavāḥ \|*  
*lalāṭādiṣu sarveṣu yathokteṣu budhairmune \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. O leading sages, in the different parts of the body as the forehead etc. three lines constitute the Tripuṇḍra.

## \|\| 1.24.85 \|\|

*bhruvormadhyaṃ samārabhya yāvadaṃto bhavedbhruvoḥ \|*  
*tāvatpramāṇaṃ saṃdhāryaṃ lalāṭe ca tripuṃḍrakam \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. The Tripuṇḍra on the forehead extends from the middle of the eyebrows to the tips of the brows on either side.

## \|\| 1.24.86 \|\|

*madhyamānāmikāṃgulyā madhye tu pratilomataḥ \|*  
*aṃguṣṭhena kṛtā rekhā tripuṃḍrākhyā bhidhīyate \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. With the middle and the ring fingers a line drawn in the opposite direction is called Tripuṇḍra.

## \|\| 1.24.87 \|\|

*madhyeṃgulibhirādāya tisṛbhirbhasma yatnataḥ \|*  
*tripuṇḍradhārayedbhaktyā bhuktimuktipradaṃ param \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. With the three middle fingers, take the ashes and apply the Tripuṇḍra on the forehead. It would give worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 1.24.88 \|\|

*tisṛṇāmapi rekhānāṃ pratyekaṃ navadevatāḥ \|*  
*sarvatrāṃgeṣu tā vakṣye sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. For each of the three lines there are nine deities everywhere in the body. I shall mention them. Listen attentively.

## \|\| 1.24.89-90 \|\|

*akāro gārhapatyāgnirbhūdharmaśca rajoguṇaḥ \|*  
*ṛgvedaśca kriyāśaktiḥ prātaḥsavanameva ca \|\| 89 \|\|*

*mahadevaśca rekhāyāḥ prathamāyāśca devatā \|*  
*vijñeyā muniśārdūlāḥ śivadīkṣāparāyaṇaiḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

89-90. The nine deities of the first line are:—The syllable “A”, Gārhapatya fire (sacrificial fire), Earth, Dharma, the attribute Rajas, Ṛgveda, Kriyāśakti (the power to do), Prātaḥsavana (morning rituals) and Mahādeva. O foremost among sages, this shall be carefully understood by those who are initiated in the cult of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.91-92 \|\|

*ukāro dakṣiṇāgniśca nabhastattvaṃ yajustathā \|*  
*madhyaṃdinaṃ ca savanamicchāśaktyaṃtarātmakau \|\| 91 \|\|*

*maheśvaraśca rekhāyā dvitīyāyāśca devatā \|*  
*vijñeyā muniśārdūla śivadīkṣāparāyaṇaiḥ \|\| 92 \|\|*

91-92. The nine deities of the second line are:—The syllable “U”, Dakṣiṇā fire (sacrificial fire), the principle of Ether, Attribute Sattva, Yajurveda, Mādhyandina Savana midday rituals), Icchāśakti (the will-power), the Antarātman (the immanent soul) and Maheśvara. O foremost among sages, this must be carefully understood by those who are initiated in the cult of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.93-94 \|\|

*makārāhavanīyau ca paramātmā tamodivau \|*  
*jñānaśaktiḥ sāmavedastṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ tathā \|\| 93 \|\|*

*śivaścaiva ca rekhāyāstṛtiyāyāśca devatā \|*  
*vijñeyā muniśārdūla śivadīkṣāparāyaṇau \|\| 94 \|\|*

93-94. The nine deities of the third line are:—The syllable “M”, Āhavanīya (sacrificial) fire, the supreme soul, the attribute Tamas, heaven, Jñāna Śakti, Sāmaveda, the third Savana (evening rituals) and Śiva. O foremost among sages, this must be carefully understood by those initiated in the cult of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.24.95 \|\|

*evaṃ nityaṃ namaskṛtya sadbhaktyā sthānadevatāḥ \|*  
*tripuṃḍraṃ dhārayecchuddho bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca viṃdati \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. Thus making obeisance to the deities of the different parts with devotion, one shall apply the Tripuṇḍra. One will become pure and derive worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 1.24.96 \|\|

*ityuktāḥ sthānadevāśca sarvāṃgeṣu munīśvaraḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ saṃbaṃdhino bhaktyā sthānāni śṛṇu sāṃpratam \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Thus I have mentioned, O lordly sages, the deities of the different parts of the body. Now please listen to the different parts connected with them.

## \|\| 1.24.97 \|\|

*dvātriṃśatsthānake vārddhaṣoḍaśasthānakepi ca \|*  
*aṣṭasthāne tathā caiva paṃcasthānepi nānyaset \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. These lines are to be made either in thirty-two places, or half of that—in sixteen places, or in eight places or in five places.

## \|\| 1.24.98-102 \|\|

*uttamāṃge lalāṭe ca karṇayornetrayostathā \|*  
*nāsāvaktragaleṣvevaṃ hastadvaya ataḥ param \|\| 98 \|\|*

*kūrpare maṇibaṃdhe ca hṛdaye pārśvayordvayoḥ \|*  
*nābhau muṣkadvaye caivamūrvorgulphe ca jānuni \|\| 99 \|\|*

*jaṃghādvayepadadvandve dvātriṃśatsthānamuttamam \|*  
*agnyabbhūvāyudigdeśadikpālānvasubhiḥ saha \|\| 100 \|\|*

*dharā dhruvaśca somaśca apaścevānilonalaḥ \|*  
*pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca vasavoṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 101 \|\|*

*eteṣāṃ nāmamātreṇa tripuṃḍraṃ dhārayedbudhāḥ \|*  
*kuryādvā ṣoḍaśasthāne tripuṇḍraṃ tu samāhitaḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

98-102. The thirty-two places are:—head, forehead, two ears, two eyes, two noses, mouth, neck, two arms, two elbows, two wrists, chest, two sides, navel, two testicles, two thighs, two knees, two calves, two heels and two feet. The names of the following shall be uttered when the Tripuṇḍra is applied:—Fire, Water, Earth, Wind, the quarters, the guardians of the quarters, the eight Vasus. The eight Vasus are Dharā, Dhruva, Soma, Āpa, Anila, Anala, Pratyūṣa and Prabhasa.

Or the devotee shall apply the Tripuṇḍra in sixteen parts of the body.

## \|\| 1.24.103-109 \|\|

*śīrṣake ca lalāṭeca kaṃṭhe cāṃsadvaye bhuje \|*  
*kūrpare maṇibaṃdhe ca hṛdaye nābhipārśvake \|\| 103 \|\|*

*pṛṣṭhe caivaṃ pratiṣṭhāya yajettatrāśvidaivate \|*  
*śivaśaktiṃ tathā rudra mīśaṃ nāradameva ca \|\| 104 \|\|*

*vāmādinavaśaktīśca etāḥ ṣoḍaśadevatāḥ \|*  
*nāsatyo dasrakaścaiva aśvinau dvau prakīrtitau \|\| 105 \|\|*

*athavā mūrddhni keśe ca karmayorvadane tathā \|*  
*bāhudvaye ca hṛdaye nābhyāmūruyuge tathā \|\| 106 \|\|*

*jānudvaye ca padayoḥ pṛṣṭhabhāge ca ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*śivaścandra śca rudra ḥ ko vighneśo viṣṇureva vā \|\| 107 \|\|*

*śrīścaiva hṛdaye śambhustathā nābhau prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*nāgaśca nāgakanyāśca ubhayorṛṣikanyakāḥ \|\| 108 \|\|*

*pādayośca samudrā śca tīrthāḥ pṛṣṭhe viśālataḥ \|*  
*ityeva ṣoḍaśasthānamaṣṭasthānamathocyate \|\| 109 \|\|*

103-109. The sixteen parts mentioned before are:—head, forehead, neck, two shoulders, two arms, two elbows, two wrists, chest, navel, two sides and back. The names of the deities presiding over them and which are to be worshipped are:—two Aśvins, Dasra and Nāsatya, Śiva, Śakti, Rudra, Īśa, Nārada, and nine Śaktis—Vāmā etc., or the sixteen parts are:—Head, hair, two eyes, the mouth, two arms, chest, navel, two thighs, knees, two feet and the back. The deities are:—Śiva, Candra, Rudra, ka (Brahmā), Vighneśvara, Viṣṇu, Śrī in the heart, Śambhu, Prajāpati in the navel, Nāga, Nāgakanyās, Ṛṣikanyās in the feet and the ocean of vast expansion in the back. Now the eight parts are mentioned.

## \|\| 1.24.110 \|\|

*guhyasthānaṃ lalāṭaśca karṇadvayamanuttamam \|*  
*aṃsayugmaṃ ca hṛdayaṃ nābhirityevamaṣṭakam \|\| 110 \|\|*

110\. The private parts, forehead, the excellent pair of ears, two shoulders, chest and navel—these are the eight parts of the body.

## \|\| 1.24.111 \|\|

*brahmā ca ṛṣayaḥ saptadevatāśca prakīrtitāḥ \|*  
*ityevaṃ tu samuddiṣṭaṃ bhasmavidbhirmunīśvarāḥ \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. The presiding deities are Brahmā and the seven sages. O lordly sages, this is what has been mentioned by those who know about the efficacy of the ashes (bhasma).

## \|\| 1.24.112 \|\|

*atha vā mastakaṃ bāhūhṛdayaṃ nābhireva ca \|*  
*paṃcasthānānyamūnyāhurdhāraṇe bhasmavijjanāḥ \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. Or these five parts are to be used for applying ashes as mentioned by those who know more about the efficacy of the ashes. They are:—forehead, two arms, chest and navel.

## \|\| 1.24.113-114 \|\|

*yathāsaṃbhavanaṃ kuryyāddeśakālādyapekṣayā \|*  
*uddhūlanepyaśaktiścettripuṇḍrādīni kārayet \|\| 113 \|\|*

*trinetraṃ triguṇādhāraṃ trivedajanakaṃ śivam \|*  
*smarannamaḥ śivāyeti lalāṭe tu tripuṇḍrakam \|\| 114 \|\|*

113-114. Considering the place and time whatever possible shall be done by the devotee. If incapable of dusting the whole body with the holy ashes he shall have the Tripuṇḍra on the forehead alone, remembering lord Śiva, the three-eyed, the support of the three Guṇas and the progenitor of the three devas by repeating Namaḥ Śivāya (obeisance to Śiva).

## \|\| 1.24.115 \|\|

*īśābhyāṃ nama ityuktvāpārśvayośca tripuṇḍrakam \|*  
*bījābhyāṃ nama ityuktvā dhārayettu prakoṣṭhayoḥ \|\| 115 \|\|*

115\. He shall have Tripuṇḍra in the sides saying Īśābhyāṃ Namaḥ (obeisance to Śiva and the goddess) and in the forearms by saying Bījābhyām Namaḥ (obeisance to the generating seeds).

## \|\| 1.24.116 \|\|

*kuryādadhaḥ pitṛbhyāṃ ca umeśābhyāṃ tathopari \|*  
*bhīmāyeti tataḥ pṛṣṭhe śirasaḥ paścime tathā \|\| 116 \|\|*

116\. He shall apply the ashes beneath by saying Namaḥ Pitṛbhyām (obeisance to the manes) and above by saying Namaḥ Umesābhyām (obeisance to Umā and Īśa), on the back and the back of the head by saying Namaḥ Bhīmāya (obeisance to Bhīma).

## Chapter 25 - The greatness of Rudrākṣa

## \|\| 1.25.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śaunakarṣe mahāprājña śivarūpamahāpate \|*  
*śṛṇu rudrā kṣamāhātmyaṃ samāsātkathayāmyaham \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:

1\. O sage Śaunaka, highly intelligent, of the form of Śiva, noble-minded, please listen to the greatness of Rudrākṣa. I shall explain it briefly.

## \|\| 1.25.2 \|\|

*śivapriyatamo jñeyo rudrā kṣaḥ parapāvanaḥ \|*  
*darśanātsparśanājjāpyātsarvapāpaharaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Rudrākṣa is a favourite bead of Śiva. It is highly sanctifying. It removes all sins by sight, contact and Japas.

## \|\| 1.25.3 \|\|

*purā rudrā kṣamahimā devyagre kathito mune \|*  
*lokopakaraṇārthāya śivena paramātmanā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O sage, formerly the greatness of Rudrākṣa was declared to the Goddess by Śiva, the supreme soul, for rendering help to the worlds.

## \|\| 1.25.4 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu devimaheśāni rudrā kṣamahimā śive \|*  
*kathayāmi tavaprītyā bhaktānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 4 \|\|*

Śiva said:

4\. O Śivā, Maheśāni, be pleased to hear the greatness of Rudrākṣa. I speak out love for you from a desire for the benefit of the devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.25.5-7 \|\|

*divyavarṣasahasrāṇi maheśāni punaḥ purā \|*  
*tapaḥ prakurvatastrastaṃ manaḥ saṃyamya vai mama \|\| 5 \|\|*

*svataṃtreṇa pareśena lokopakṛtikāriṇā \|*  
*līlayā parameśāni cakṣurunmīlitaṃ mayā \|\| 6 \|\|*

*puṭābhyāṃ cārucakṣurbhyāṃ patitā jalabiṃdavaḥ \|*  
*tatrāśrubindavo jātā vṛkṣā rudrā kṣasaṃjñakāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

5-7. O Mahesāni, formerly I had been performing penance for thousands of divine years. Although I had controlled it rigorously, my mind was in flutter. Out of sport, I being self-possessed just opened my eyes, O Goddess, from a desire of helping the worlds. Drops of tears fell from my beautiful half-closed eyes. From those tear-drops there cropped up the Rudrākṣa plants.

## \|\| 1.25.8 \|\|

*sthāvaratvamanuprāpya bhaktānugrahakāraṇāt \|*  
*te dattā viṣṇubhaktebhyaścaturvarṇebhya eva ca \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. They became immobile. In order to bless the devotees they were given to the four Varṇas devoted to the worship of Viṣṇu

## \|\| 1.25.9-10 \|\|

*bhūmau gauḍodbhavāṃścakre rudrā kṣāñchivavallabhān \|*  
*mathurāyāmayodhyāyāṃ laṃkāyāṃ malaye tathā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*sahyādrau ca tathā kāśyāṃ daśeṣvanyeṣu vā tathā \|*  
*parānasahyapāpaughabhedanāñchrutinodanāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Rudrākṣas grown in Gauḍa\[1\] land became great favourites of Śiva. They were grown in Mathurā, Laṅkā, Ayodhyā, Malaya\[2\], Sahya mountain, Kāśī and other places. They are competent to break asunder the clustered sins unbearable to the others, as the sacred texts have declared.

## \|\| 1.25.11 \|\|

*brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā jātā mamājñayā \|*  
*rudrā kṣāste pṛthivyāṃ tu tajjātīyāḥ śubhākṣakāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. At my bidding they were classified into Brahmins, Kṣatriyas, Vaiśyas and Śūdras. These Rudrākṣas are of auspicious nature.

## \|\| 1.25.12 \|\|

*śvetaraktāḥ pītakṛṣṇā varṇājñeyāḥ kramādbudhaiḥ \|*  
*svajātīyaṃ nṛbhirdhāryaṃ rudrā kṣaṃ varṇataḥ kramāt \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The colours of the four types of Rudrākṣas are respectively white, red, yellow and black. All people shall wear the Rudrākṣa of their own Varna.

## \|\| 1.25.13 \|\|

*varṇaistu tatphalaṃ dhāryaṃ bhuktimuktiphalepsubhiḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktairviśeṣeṇa śivayoḥ prītaye sadā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. If they desire their benefit, namely worldly pleasures and salvation and if the devotees of Śiva wish to gratify Śiva they must wear the Rudrākṣa.

## \|\| 1.25.14 \|\|

*dhātrīphalapramāṇaṃ yacchreṣṭhametadudāhṛtam \|*  
*badarīphalamātraṃ tu madhyamaṃ saṃprakīrttitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. A Rudrākṣa of the size of an Emblic myrobalan (Dhātrīphala) is mentioned as the most excellent; one of the size of the fruit of the jujube tree (Badarīphala) is spoken of as the middling.

## \|\| 1.25.15 \|\|

*adhamaṃ caṇamātraṃ syātprakriyaiṣā parocyate \|*  
*śṛṇu pārvati suprītyā bhaktānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O Pārvatī, lovingly listen to this from a desire for the benefit of the devotees. The meanest of Rudrākṣas is of the size of a gram according to this excellent classification.

## \|\| 1.25.16 \|\|

*badarīphalamātraṃ ca yatsyātkila maheśvari \|*  
*tathāpi phaladaṃ loke sukhasaubhāgyavarddhanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O Maheśvarī, even the Rudrākṣa which is only of the size of the fruit of the jujube accords the benefit and heightens happiness and good fortune.

## \|\| 1.25.17 \|\|

*dhātrīphalasamaṃ yatsyātsarvāriṣṭavināśanam \|*  
*guṃjayā sadṛśaṃ yatsyātsarvārthaphalasādhanam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. That which is of the size of the emblic myrobalan is conducive to the destruction of all distresses. That which is of the size of a Guñjā (the berry) is conducive to the achievement of the fruit of all desires.

## \|\| 1.25.18 \|\|

*yathā yathā laghuḥ syādvai tathādhikaphalapradam \|*  
*ekaikataḥ phalaṃ proktaṃ daśāṃśairadhikaṃ budhaiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The lighter the Rudrākṣa, the more fruitful it is. Each of these is fruitful and that of a weight of one tenth is considered by scholars as the most fruitful.

## \|\| 1.25.19 \|\|

*rudrā kṣadhāraṇaṃ proktaṃ pāpanāśanahetave \|*  
*tasmācca dhāraṇī yo vai sarvārthasādhano dhruvam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The wearing of Rudrākṣa is recommended for the sake of destroying sins. Hence that which is conducive to the achievement of every object has to be worn certainly.

## \|\| 1.25.20 \|\|

*yathā ca dṛśyate loke rudrā kṣaphaladaḥ śubhaḥ \|*  
*na tathā dṛśyate'nyā ca mālikā parameśvari \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O Parameśvarī, no other necklace or garland is observed in the world to be so auspicious and fruitful as the Rudrākṣa.

## \|\| 1.25.21 \|\|

*samāḥ snigdhā dṛḍhāḥ sthūlāḥ kaṃṭakaiḥ saṃyutāḥ śubhāḥ \|*  
*rudrā kṣāḥ kāmadā devi bhuktimuktipradāḥ sadā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O Goddess, Rudrākṣas of even size, glossy, firm, thick and having many thornlike protrusions yield desires and bestow worldly pleasures and salvation for ever.

## \|\| 1.25.22 \|\|

*krimiduṣṭaṃ chinnabhinnaṃ kaṃṭakairhīnameva ca \|*  
*vraṇayuktamavṛttaṃ ca rudrā kṣānṣaḍvivarjayet \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Six types of Rudrākṣas shall be discarded:—that which is defiled by worms, is cut and broken, has no thornlike protrusions, has cracks and is not circular.

## \|\| 1.25.23 \|\|

*svayameva kṛtadvāraṃ rudrā kṣaṃ syādihottamam \|*  
*yattu pauruṣayatnena kṛtaṃ tanmadhyamaṃ bhavet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. That which has a natural hole from end to end is the most excellent; that which is bored through by human effort is the middling one.

## \|\| 1.25.24 \|\|

*rudrā kṣadhāraṇaṃ prāptaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam \|*  
*rudra saṃkhyāśataṃ dhṛtvā rudra rūpo bhavennaraḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The wearing of Rudrākṣa is spoken of as conducive to the destruction of great sins. If eleven hundred Rudrākṣas are worn on the person, the man assumes the form of Rudra.

## \|\| 1.25.25 \|\|

*ekādaśaśatānīha dhṛtvā yatphalamāpyate \|*  
*tatphalaṃ śakyate naiva vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Even in hundreds of years it is impossible to describe adequately the benefit derived by wearing eleven hundred and fifty Rudrākṣas.

## \|\| 1.25.26 \|\|

*śatārddhena yutaiḥ paṃcaśatairvai mukuṭaṃ matam \|*  
*rudrā kṣairviracetsamyagbhaktimānpuruṣo varaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. A devout man shall make a coronet consisting of five hundred and fifty Rudrākṣas.

## \|\| 1.25.27 \|\|

*tribhiḥ śataiḥ ṣaṣṭiyuktaistrirāvṛttyā tathā punaḥ \|*  
*rudrā kṣairupavītaṃ va nirmīyādbhaktitatparaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. A person of pious nature shall make three circular strings in the manner of the sacred thread, each having three hundred and sixty beads.

## \|\| 1.25.28 \|\|

*śikhāyāṃ ca trayaṃ proktaṃ rudra kṣāṇāṃ maheśvari \|*  
*karṇayoḥ ṣaṭ ca ṣaṭcaiva vāmadakṣiṇayostathā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O Maheśvarī, three Rudrākṣas must be worn on the tuft and six in each of the ears right and left.

## \|\| 1.25.29-30 \|\|

*śatamekottaraṃ kaṃṭhe bāhvorvai rudra saṃkhyayā \|*  
*kūrparadvārayostatra maṇibaṃdhe tathā punaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*upavīte trayaṃ dhāryaṃ śivabhaktiratairnaraiḥ \|*  
*śeṣānurvaritānpaṃca sammitāndhārayetkaṭau \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Hundred and one Rudrākṣas shall be worn round the neck; eleven Rudrāksas shall be worn round each of the arms, elbows and wrists. Devotees of Śiva shall have three Rudrākṣas in the sacred thread and round the hips five Rudrākṣas shall be tied.

## \|\| 1.25.31 \|\|

*etatsaṃkhyā dhṛtā yena rudrā kṣāḥ parameśvari \|*  
*tadrū paṃ tu praṇamyaṃ hi stutyaṃ sarvairmaheśavat \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O Parameśvarī, the person by whom so many Rudrākṣas are worn is worthy of being bowed to and adored by all like Maheśa.

## \|\| 1.25.32 \|\|

*evaṃbhūtaṃ sthitaṃ dhyāne yadā kṛtvāsanairjanam \|*  
*śiveti vyāharaṃścaiva dṛṣṭvā pāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Such a person while in contemplation shall be duly seated and addressed “O Śiva”. Seeing him, every one is freed from sins.

## \|\| 1.25.33 \|\|

*śatādikasahasrasya vidhireṣa prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*tadabhāve prakāronyaḥ śubhaḥ saṃprocyate mayā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. This is the rule regarding eleven hundred Rudrākṣas. If so many are not available, another auspicious procedure I mention to you.

## \|\| 1.25.34-36 \|\|

*śikhāyāmekarudrā kṣaṃ śirasā triṃśataṃ vahet \|*  
*paṃcāśacca gale dadhyādbāhvoḥ ṣoḍaśa ṣoḍaśa \|\| 34 \|\|*

*maṇibaṃdhe dvādaśadviskaṃdhe paṃcaśataṃ vahet \|*  
*aṣṭottaraśatairmālyamupavītaṃ prakalpayet \|\| 35 \|\|*

*evaṃ sahasrarudrā kṣāndhārayedyo dṛḍhavrataḥ \|*  
*taṃ namaṃti surāḥ sarve yathā rudra stathaiva saḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

34-36. One Rudrākṣa shall be worn on the tuft, thirty on the head, fifty round the neck; sixteen in each of the arms; twelve round each of the wrists; five hundred on the shoulders, and three strings each having hundred and eight in the manner of the sacred thread. He who wears in all a thousand Rudrākṣas and is of firm resolve in performing rites is bowed to by all Devas like Rudra himself.

## \|\| 1.25.37-39 \|\|

*ekaṃ śikhāyāṃ rudrā kṣaṃ catvāriṃśattu mastake \|*  
*dvātriṃśatkaṇṭhadeśe tu vakṣasyaṣṭottaraṃ śatam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*ekaikaṃ karṇayoḥ ṣaṭṣaḍbāhvoḥ ṣoḍaśa ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*karayoravimānena dviguṇena munīśvara \|\| 38 \|\|*

*saṃkhyā prītirdhṛtā yena sopi śaivajanaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*śivavatpūjanīyo hi vaṃdyassarvairabhīkṣṇaśaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

37-39. One Rudrākṣa shall be worn on the tuft, forty on the forehead, thirty-two round the neck; hundred and eight over the chest; six in each of the ears; sixteen round each of the arms; O lord of sages, according to the measurement of the forearms, twelve or twice that number shall be worn there. A person who wears so many, out of love, is a great devotee of Śiva. He shall be worshipped like Śiva. He is worthy of being always honoured by all.

## \|\| 1.25.40 \|\|

*śirasīśānamaṃtreṇa karṇe tatpuruṣeṇa ca \|*  
*aghoreṇa gale dhāryaṃ tenaiva hṛdayepi ca \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. It shall be worn on the head repeating Īśāna\[4\] mantra; on the ears with Tripuruṣa\[5\] mantra; round the neck with Aghora mantra and on the chest also likewise.

## \|\| 1.25.41 \|\|

*aghorabījamaṃtreṇa karayordhārayetsudhīḥ \|*  
*paṃcadaśākṣagrathitāṃ vāmadevena codare \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The wise devotee shall wear the Rudrākṣa round the forearms with Aghora Bīja mantra. A string of fifteen beads shall be worn on the stomach with Vāmadeva mantra.

## \|\| 1.25.42 \|\|

*paṃca brahmabhiraṃgaśca trimālāṃ paṃcasapta ca \|*  
*athavā mūlamaṃtreṇa sarvānakṣāṃstudhārayet \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. With five mantras—Sadyojāta etc. three, five or seven garlands shall be worn. Or all beads shall be worn with the Mūla mantra.

## \|\| 1.25.43 \|\|

*madyaṃ māṃsaṃ tu laśunaṃ palāṇḍuṃ śigrumeva ca \|*  
*śleṣmāṃtakaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ bhakṣaṇe varjayettataḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. A devotee of Śiva shall refrain from eating meat, garlic, onion, red garlic, potherb, Śleṣmātaka, pig of rubbish and liquors.

## \|\| 1.25.44 \|\|

*valakṣaṃ rudrā kṣaṃ dvijatanubhireveha vihitaṃ suraktaṃ kṣattrāṇāṃ pramuditamume pītamasakṛt \|*  
*chinnaṃ khaṃḍitaṃ bhinnaṃ vidīrṇa \|*  
*tato vaiśyairdhāryaṃ pratidivasabhāvaśyakamaho tathā kṛṣṇaṃ śūdrai ḥ śrutigaditamārgoyamagaje \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. O Umā, daughter of the mountain, the white Rudrākṣa shall be worn by the brahmin, the red by the Kṣatriya, the yellow by the Vaiśya, the black by the Śūdra. This is the path indicated by the Vedas.

## \|\| 1.25.45 \|\|

*varṇī vanī gṛhayatīrniyamena dadhyādetadra hasyaparamo na hi jātu tiṣṭhet \|*  
*rudrā kṣadhāraṇamidaṃ sukṛtaiśca labhyaṃ tyaktvedametadakhilānnarakānprayāṃti \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Whether he is a householder, forest-dweller, ascetic or of any Order, none shall go out of this secret advice. Only by great merits can the opportunity to wear the Rudrākṣa be obtained. If he misses it he will go to hell.

## \|\| 1.25.46 \|\|

*ādāvāmalakātsvato laghutarā rugṇāstataḥ kaṃṭakaiḥ saṃdaṣṭāḥ kṛmibhistanūpakaraṇacchidre ṇa hīnāstathā \|*  
*dhāryā naiva śubhepsubhiścaṇakavadrudrā kṣamapyaṃtato rudrā kṣomama liṃgamaṃgalamume sūkṣmaṃ praśastaṃ sadā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The Rudrākṣas of the size of an Emblic myrobalan and those of lighter weight but depressed with thorns, those eaten by worms or without holes and those characterized by other defects shall not be worn by those wishing for auspicious results. They shall avoid small ones of the size of gram. O Umā, Rudrākṣa is an auspicious complement to my phallic image. The small one is always praiseworthy.

## \|\| 1.25.47 \|\|

*sarvāśramāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ strīśūdrā ṇāṃ śivājñayā \|*  
*dhāryāḥ sadaiva rudrā kṣā yatīnāṃ praṇavena hi \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. People of all Varṇas and Āśramas even women and Śūdras can wear Rudrākṣa at the bidding of Śiva. The ascetics shall wear it with the Praṇava.

## \|\| 1.25.48 \|\|

*divā bibhradrā trikṛtai rātrau vibhraddivākṛtaiḥ \|*  
*prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhne mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. If any one wears it during the day he is freed from sins committed during the night; if he wears it during the night he is freed from the sins committed during the day. Similar is the result with its wearing during morning, midday or evening.

## \|\| 1.25.49 \|\|

*ye tripuṇḍradharā loke jaṭādhāriṇa eva ye \|*  
*ye rudrā kṣadharāste vai yamalokaṃ prayāṃti na \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Those who wear Tripuṇḍra, the matted hair and the Rudrākṣa do not go to Yama’s abode.

## \|\| 1.25.50-52 \|\|

*rudrā kṣamekaṃ śirasā bibharti tathā tripuṇḍraṃ ca lalāṭamadhye \|*  
*paṃcākṣaraṃ ye hi japaṃti maṃtraṃ pūjyā bhavadbhiḥ khalu te hi sādhavaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*yasyāṇge nāsti rudrā kṣastripuṇḍraṃ bhālapaṭṭake \|*  
*mukhe paṃcākṣaraṃ nāsti tamānaya yamālayam \|\| 51 \|\|*

*jñātvā jñātvā tatprabhāvaṃ bhasmarudrā kṣadhāriṇaḥ \|*  
*te pūjyāḥ sarvadāsmākaṃ no netavyāḥ kadācana \|\| 52 \|\|*

50-52. \[Yama’s directive to his attendants:—\] “Those who wear at least one Rudrākṣa on their heads, Tripuṇḍra on the forehead and repeat the five-syllabled mantras shall be honoured by you all. They are indeed saintly men. You can bring the man here who has no Rudrākṣa on his person, and no Tripuṇḍra on his forehead and who does not utter the five-syllabled mantra. All those who have the ash and Rudrākṣa shall be honoured always by us after knowing their power. They shall never be brought here”.

## \|\| 1.25.53 \|\|

*evamājñāpayāmāsa kālopi nijakiṇkarān \|*  
*tatheti mattvā te sarve tūṣṇīmāsansuvismitāḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Yama commanded his attendants like this. They too remained quiet agreeing to it. In fact they were surprised.

## \|\| 1.25.54 \|\|

*ata eva mahādevi rudrā kṣotyaghanāśanaḥ \|*  
*taddharo matpriyaḥ śuddho'tyaghavānapi pārvati \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Hence Mahādevī, the Rudrākṣa as well as the person who wears it is my favourite. O Pārvatī, even if he has committed sins he becomes pure.

## \|\| 1.25.55 \|\|

*haste bāhau tathā mūrdhni rudrā kṣaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|*  
*avadhyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ rudra rūpī caredbhuvi \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He who wears Rudrākṣa round the hands and arms and over the head cannot be killed by any living being. He shall roam in the world in the form of Rudra.

## \|\| 1.25.56 \|\|

*surāsurāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ vaṃdanīyaḥ sadā sa vai \|*  
*pūjanīyo hi dṛṣṭasya pāpahā ca yathā śivaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. He shall be respected by the Gods and Asuras always. He shall be honoured like Śiva. He removes the sin of any one seen by him.

## \|\| 1.25.57 \|\|

*dhyānajñānāvamuktopi rudrā kṣaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|*  
*sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. If a person is not liberated after meditation and acquisition of knowledge he shall wear Rudrākṣa. He shall be freed from all sins and attain the highest goal.

## \|\| 1.25.58 \|\|

*rudrā kṣeṇa japanmantraṃ puṇyaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet \|*  
*daśakoṭiguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ dhāraṇāllabhate naraḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. A mantra repeated with Rudrākṣa is a crore times more efficacious. A man wearing Rudrākṣa derives a hundred million times more merit.

## \|\| 1.25.59 \|\|

*yāvatkālaṃ hi jīvasya śarīrastho bhavetsa vai \|*  
*tāvatkālaṃ svalpamṛtyurna taṃ devi vibādhate \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. O Goddess, as long as the Rudrākṣa is on the person of a living soul he is least affected by premature death.

## \|\| 1.25.60 \|\|

*tripuṃḍreṇa ca saṃyuktaṃ rudrā kṣāvilasāṃgakam \|*  
*mṛtyuṃjayaṃ japaṃtaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā rudra phalaṃ labhet \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. One shall attain Rudra on seeing a person with Tripuṇḍra, his limbs covered with Rudrākṣa and repeating the Mṛtyuñjaya mantra.

## \|\| 1.25.61 \|\|

*paṃcadevapriyaścaiva sarvadevapriyastathā \|*  
*sarvamantrāñjapedbhakto rudrā kṣamālayā priye \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. He is a favourite of the five deities and a favourite of all gods. O beloved, a devotee shall repeat all mantras wearing a garland of Rudrākṣas (or counting on the beads).

## \|\| 1.25.62 \|\|

*viṣṇvādidevabhaktāśca dhārayeyurna saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*rudra bhakto viśeṣeṇa rudrā kṣāndhārayetsadā \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Even the devotees of Viṣṇu and other deities shall unhesitatingly wear the Rudrākṣa. Especially the devotee of Rudra shall wear Rudrākṣas always.

## \|\| 1.25.63 \|\|

*rudrā kṣā vividhāḥ proktāsteṣāṃ bhedānvadāmyaham \|*  
*śṛṇu pārvati sadbhaktyā bhuktimuktiphalapradān \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Rudrākṣas are of various types. I shall explain their different classifications. O Pārvatī, hear with great devotion. These Rudrākṣas bestow worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 1.25.64 \|\|

*ekavaktraḥ śivaḥ sākṣādbhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ \|*  
*tasya darśanamātreṇa brahmahatyā vyapohati \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. A Rudrākṣa of a single face is Śiva Himself. It bestows worldly pleasures and salvation. The sin of brahmin-slaughter is washed off at its mere sight.

## \|\| 1.25.65 \|\|

*yatra saṃpūjitastatra lakṣmīrdūratarā na hi \|*  
*naśyaṃtyupadra vāḥ sarve sarvakāmā bhavaṃti hi \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Where it is adored, Fortune cannot be far off. Harms and harassments perish. All desires are fulfilled.

## \|\| 1.25.66 \|\|

*dvivaktro devadeveśassarvakāmaphalapradaḥ \|*  
*viśeṣataḥ sa rudrā kṣo govadhaṃ nāśayeddrutam \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. A Rudrākṣa with two faces is Īśa, the lord of devas. It bestows the fulfilment of all desires. Especially, that Rudrākṣa quickly quells the sin of cow-slaughter.

## \|\| 1.25.67 \|\|

*trivaktro yo hi rudrā kṣaḥ sākṣātsādhanadassadā \|*  
*tatprabhāvādbhaveyurvai vidyāḥ sarvāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. A Rudrākṣa with three faces always bestows means of enjoyment. As a result of its power all lores become firmly established.

## \|\| 1.25.68 \|\|

*caturvaktraḥ svayaṃ brahmā narahatyāṃ vyapohati \|*  
*darśanātsparśanātsadyaścaturvargaphalapradaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. A Rudrākṣa of four faces is Brahmā Himself. It quells the sin of man-slaughter. Its vision and its contact instantaneously bestow the achievement of the four aims of life.

## \|\| 1.25.69 \|\|

*paṃcavaktraḥ svayaṃ rudra ḥ kālāgnirnāmataḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*sarvamuktipradaścaiva sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. A Rudrākṣa with five faces is Rudra Himself. Its name is kālāgni. It is lordly. It bestows all sorts of salvation and achievement of all desired objects.

## \|\| 1.25.70 \|\|

*agamyāgamanaṃ pāpamabhakṣyasya ca bhakṣaṇam \|*  
*ityādisarvapāpāni paṃcavaktro vyapohati \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. A five-faced Rudrākṣa dispels all sorts of sins such as accrue from sexual intercourse with a forbidden woman and from eating forbidden food.

## \|\| 1.25.71 \|\|

*ṣaḍvaktraḥ kārtikeyastudhāraṇāddakṣiṇe bhuje \|*  
*brahmahatyādikaiḥ pāpairmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. A Rudrākṣa with six faces is Kārtikeya. A man who wears it on the right arm is certainly absolved of the sins of brahmin-slaughter and the like.

## \|\| 1.25.72 \|\|

*saptavaktro maheśāni hyanaṃgo nāma nāmataḥ \|*  
*dhāraṇāttasya deveśidaridro pīśvaro bhavet \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. A Rudrākṣa with seven faces, O Maheśāni, is called Anaṅga. O Deveśī, by wearing it even a poor man becomes a great lord.

## \|\| 1.25.73 \|\|

*rudrā kṣaścāṣṭavaktraśca vasumūrtiśca bhairavaḥ \|*  
*dhāraṇāttasya pūrṇāyurmṛto bhavati śūlabhṛt \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. A Rudrākṣa with eight faces is called Vasumūrti and Bhairava. By wearing it a man lives the full span of life. After death he becomes the Trident-bearing lord (Śiva).

## \|\| 1.25.74 \|\|

*bhairavo navavaktraśca kapilaśca muniḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*durgā vāta dadhiṣṭhātrī navarūpā maheśvarī \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. A Rudrākṣa with nine faces is also Bhairava. Its sage is Kapila. Its presiding goddess is Durgā of nine forms, Maheśvarī Herself.

## \|\| 1.25.75 \|\|

*taṃ dhārayedvāmahaste rudrā kṣaṃ bhaktitatparaḥ \|*  
*sarveśvaro bhavennūnaṃ mama tulyo na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. That Rudrākṣa shall be worn on the left hand with great devotion. He shall certainly become Sarveśvara like me.

## \|\| 1.25.76 \|\|

*daśavaktro maheśāni svayaṃ devo janārdanaḥ \|*  
*dhāraṇāttasya deveśi sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. O Maheśānī, a Rudrākṣa with ten faces is Lord Janārdana Himself. O Deveśī, by wearing it, the devotee shall achieve the fulfilment of all desires.

## \|\| 1.25.77 \|\|

*ekādaśamukho yastu rudrā kṣaḥ parameśvari \|*  
*sa rudro dhāraṇāttasya sarvatra vijayī bhavet \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. O Parameśvarī, a Rudrākṣa with eleven faces is Rudra. By wearing it one becomes victorious everywhere.

## \|\| 1.25.78 \|\|

*dvādaśāsyaṃ tu rudrā kṣaṃ dhārayetkeśadeśake \|*  
*ādityāścaiva te sarvedvādaśaiva sthitāstathā \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. One shall wear the twelve-faced Rudrākṣa on the hair of the head. All the twelve Ādityas (suns) are present therein.

## \|\| 1.25.79 \|\|

*trayodaśamukho viśvedevastaddhāraṇānnaraḥ \|*  
*sarvānkāmānavāpnoti saubhāgyaṃ maṃgalaṃlabhet \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. A Rudrākṣa with thirteen faces is Viśvedeva. By wearing it, a man will attain the realisation of all desires. He will derive good fortune and auspiciousness.

## \|\| 1.25.80 \|\|

*caturdaśamukho yo hi rudrā kṣaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*dhārayenmūrdhni taṃ bhaktyā sarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. A Rudrākṣa with fourteen faces is the highest Śiva. It shall be worn on the head with great devotion. It quells all sins.

## \|\| 1.25.81 \|\|

*iti rudrā kṣabhedā hi proktā vai mukhabhedataḥ \|*  
*tattanmaṃtrāñchṛṇu prītyā kramācchailleśvarātmaje \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ 1 oṃ namaḥ 2 oṃ klīṃ namaḥ 3 oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ 4 oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ 5 oṃ hrīṃ huṃ namaḥ 6 oṃ huṃnamaḥ 7 oṃ huṃ namaḥ 8 oṃ hrīṃ huṃ namaḥ 9 oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ namaḥ 10 oṃ hrīṃ huṃ namaḥ 11 oṃ krauṃ kṣauṃ rauṃ namaḥ 12 oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ 13 oṃ nama \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. O daughter of the king of mountains, thus I have explained to you the different types of Rudrākṣas based on the number offaces. Please listen to the mantras with devotion.

Om Hrīṃ obeisance Single-faced Om obeisance 2 faces Klīṃ obeisance 3 faces Om Hrīṃ obeisance 4 faces Om Hrīṃ obeisance 5 faces Om Hrīṃ Huṃ obeisance 6 faces Om Huṃ obeisance 7 faces Om Huṃ obeisance 8 faces Om Hrīṃ Huṃ obeisance 9 faces Om Hrīṃ obeisance 10 faces Om Hrīṃ Huṃ obeisance 11 faces Om Krauṃ Kṣauṃ Rauṃ obeisance 12 faces Om Hrīṃ obeisance 13 faces Om obeisance 14 faces

## \|\| 1.25.82 \|\|

*bhaktiśraddhā yutaścaiva sarvakāmārthasiddhaye \|*  
*rudrā kṣāndhārayenmaṃtrairdevanālasya varjitaḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. For the achievement of all desired objects, the devotee shall wear the Rudrākṣa with mantras. He shall have great devotion and faith. He shall be free from lethargy.

## \|\| 1.25.83 \|\|

*vinā maṃtreṇa ho dhatte rudrā kṣaṃ bhuvi mānavaḥ \|*  
*sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadindrā ścaturdaśa \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. The man who wears the Rudrākṣa without mantra falls into a terrible hell and stays there during the tenure of fourteen Indras.

## \|\| 1.25.84-85 \|\|

*rudrā kṣamālinaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhūtapretapiśācakāḥ \|*  
*ḍākinīśākinī caiva ye cānye dro hakārakāḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

*kṛtrimaṃ caiva yatkiṃcidabhicārādikaṃ ca yat \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ dūrato yāti dṛṣṭvā śaṃkitavigraham \|\| 85 \|\|*

84-85. On seeing a man with the garland of Rudrākṣas, all evil spirits, ghosts, Piśācas, witches like Dākinī and Śākinī, other malignant spirits, evil charms and spells etc. fly away suspecting a quarrel.

## \|\| 1.25.86 \|\|

*rudrā kṣamālinaṃ dṛṣṭvā śivo viṣṇuḥ prasīdati \|*  
*devīgaṇapatissūryaḥ surāścānyepi pārvati \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Seeing a devotee with the garland of Rudrākṣas, O Pārvatī, Śiva, Viṣṇu, Devī, Gaṇapati, the sun and all the Gods are pleased.

## \|\| 1.25.87 \|\|

*evaṃ jñātvā tu māhātmyaṃ rudrā kṣasya maheśvari \|*  
*samyagdhāryāssamaṃtrāśca bhaktyādharmavivṛddhaye \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. Thus realising its greatness the Rudrākṣa must be worn well, O Maheśvarī, repeating the mantras with devotion to make virtues flourish.

## \|\| 1.25.88 \|\|

*ityuktaṃ girijāgre hi śivena paramātmanā \|*  
*bhasmarūdrā kṣamāhātmyaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Thus, the greatness of ash and Rudrākṣa that bestow worldly pleasures and salvation, was explained to Girijā by Śiva, the supreme soul.

## \|\| 1.25.89 \|\|

*śivasyātipriyau jñeyau bhasmarudrā kṣadhāriṇau \|*  
*taddhāraṇaprabhāvaddhi bhuktirmuktirna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. The persons who apply ash and wear Rudrākṣa are great favourites of Śiva. Enjoyment of worldly pleasures and salvation are certainly due to their influence.

## \|\| 1.25.90 \|\|

*bhasmarudrā kṣadhārī yaḥ śivabhaktassa ucyate \|*  
*paṃcākṣarajapāsaktaḥ paripūrṇaśca sanmukhe \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. He who applies ash and wears Rudrākṣa is called a devotee of Śiva. A person devoted to the Japa of the five-syllabled mantra is a perfect and noble being.

## \|\| 1.25.91 \|\|

*vinā bhasmatripuṃḍreṇa vinā rudrā kṣamālayā \|*  
*pūjitopi mahādevo nābhīṣṭaphaladāyakaḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. If Mahādeva is worshipped without the Tripuṇḍra of ash and without the garland of Rudrākṣa, he does not bestow the fruit of cherished desire.

## \|\| 1.25.92 \|\|

*tatsarvaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ hi munīśvara \|*  
*bhasmarudrā kṣamāhātmyaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhidam \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. Thus, O lord of sages, whatever has been asked has now been explained. The greatness of ash and Rudrākṣa bestows the luxuriant fulfilment of all desires.

## \|\| 1.25.93 \|\|

*etadyaḥ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ māhātmyaparamaṃ śubham \|*  
*rudrā kṣabhasmanorbhaktyāsarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. He who regularly listens to the highly auspicious greatness of ash and Rudrākṣa with devotion shall attain the fulfilment of all desires.

## \|\| 1.25.94 \|\|

*iha sarvasukhaṃ bhuktvā putrapautrādisaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*labhetparatra sanmokṣaṃ śivasyātipriyo bhavet \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. He will enjoy all happiness here. He will be blessed with sons and grandsons. In the next world he will attain salvation. He will be a great favourite of Śiva.

## \|\| 1.25.95 \|\|

*vidyeśvarasaṃhiteyaṃ kathitā vo munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*sarvasiddhipradā nityaṃ muktidā śivaśāsanāt \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. O lordly sages, thus the compendium of Vidyeśvara-saṃhitā has been narrated to you all. As ordered by Śiva it bestows achievement of everything and salvation.
